Status values: `planned`, `in progress`, `blocked`, `complete`.

Baseline environment (recorded at kickoff, 2026-09-19):

- Repo: `/Users/arnaud/code/edytor`, branch `master`, HEAD `d6c4781 trying to avoid composition footguns`
- Working tree: 111 modified/untracked entries at start — **uncommitted work is the baseline; do not reset/clean**
- node v24.21.0, pnpm 10.32.1
- `node_modules` absent at start; `pnpm install` required
- Network: github.com and registry.npmjs.org reachable

- 2026-09-19 — **U04 complete.** Text ownership landed: stable backing texts + anchored slice claims (ADR `docs/crdt-v14-text-ownership-adr.md`). Split/merge never copy atoms — `slices` records `{t,s,e,g}` + `{m}` claims derive display ownership. Per-atom key `(g, start, stamp)` chosen to give seam-preserving nested partitions on concurrent different-anchor splits (both seams survive; earlier `(g,stamp)`-only was winner-take-all) and deterministic same-anchor multiplicity (all siblings exist, max-stamp tail wins, losers stay empty). Dead-holder merge claims made inert → delete-beats-merge (ST02b). Children of merged-away blocks follow the effective display parent (`displayParentOf` in placement model). TX01–TX09 + ST01–ST03 + HI01a/b activated in `active-text.ts` (both delivery orders, convergence, structural validity, snapshot/log reload per scenario); pending rows removed. Duplicate-inline eliminated from model corpus (`atomClaim` per-record emission). Bench `textOwnership`: split-100k 334B / merge-100k 35B, flat vs payload size. Model corpus: `lost-edit×127–133, unrecoverable-loss×5–6` — both expected evidence classes; live-atom coverage holes investigated (seed 24) and ruled out (holes = records held by deleted blocks = dead-holder semantics, not ownership loss). Undo proofs: undo split preserves remote suffix edit (`hello world!`), undo merge revives source with remote edits/moves intact. No commits.

- 2026-09-20 — **U12 chromium regression resolved — app-layer selection race, NOT a U11 defect.** `tests/editor-dom/inline-atomic.spec.ts:371` (`Worldeza ez ` → `Worldeza z e`/`Worldeza  ez`, ~25% failure rate in isolation) root-caused to a pre-existing race the U11 timing shift exposed. **Facade exonerated first**: a focused CRDT repro (since removed) showed `DocChange` reports `content: [b1]` on undo AND redo, fresh `anchorAt`/`resolveAnchor` land the correct offsets, and the direct facade path produces the expected text — `takeSnap()`/`childrenIndex()` under-report nothing. **Mechanism**: undo/redo runs TWO independent delayed selection restores — the hotkey/onInput path's `restoreCollapsedHistorySelection` (chained off `refreshDomAfterHistoryChange`'s ~2-tick refresh) and the `stack-item-popped` handler's own `await tick()` → `setAtTextOffset` (selection.svelte.ts `init()`). Both re-apply the snapshot's caret offset unconditionally — guarded only by "did another history command run", never by "did the document change". A keystroke landing in the ~1-3 frame window commits `insertText`, then the late restore fires `setAtNodeOffset`→`applySelectionSnapshot` and snaps DOM+model caret back to the stale offset; the next `beforeinput` derives position from that regressed caret (`syncSelectionFromBeforeInputTargetRange`), producing the observed orderings (`z`@9,` `@10 → "Worldeza z e"; ` `@9 after z@10 → "Worldeza ez"). **Fix**: pending restores now invalidate on any committed change after the history command's own commit — new `edytor._docCommitVersion` counter (bumped only inside the facade `onChange` listener; `_docVersion` was disqualified because `ensureFacadeChangeSub` also bumps it on every `{#key}` reattach — an invalidation bump, not an edit, which deterministically swallowed Enter after undoing a paragraph split via the same stale-caret path). `captureHistoryCommandDocVersion` baselines synchronously inside `refreshDomAfterHistoryChange` (every call site reaches it post-commit); `beginHistoryCommandRestore`'s `shouldRestore` and the `stack-item-popped` `isCurrentRestore` both require version AND commit-counter match. **Regression coverage**: `src/tests/historySelectionSnapshot.test.tsx` (3 tests — intervening commit skips the pending restore, restore still applies when nothing intervenes, new command resets baseline; verified red on unguarded code). Evidence: `playwright --project=chromium` **367p/1s/0f** incl. target spec 8/8 isolated + `hotkeys.spec.ts:1200` 4/4; `pnpm test` 1222p/1s/11t; `test:crdt` 1233p/7s; `test:dom` 142p; `check` 0/0; `lint` clean; `bench:crdt` unchanged (app-layer fix, no CRDT hot-path edit — move-100k 54B, typing local p50/p95 0.0165/0.041ms, load-1k 2.50ms, split-100k 346B, merge-100k 35B). - 2026-09-20 — **Follow-up work unit 1 complete — three facade-level ownership defects fixed** (`docs/archive/crdt-v14-follow-up-prompt.md` §Work unit 1). All three reproduced through `bindEdytorDoc` before the fix with a dedicated regression lane (`src/tests/crdt/text/ownership-regression.test.ts`, 9 tests — all failing pre-fix on intended assertions, green post-fix). **Defect A (left-edge steal)**: inserting at offset 0 of a block whose covering record had _disjoint_ resolved coverage rewrote one record `{insertAt, record.e}` under a bumped generation, re-claiming atoms a rival record had legitimately won (`early = Xdefghij`, `late = ""`). Fix: the rewrite now emits one record per resolved owned seg — typed-into seg extends left over the new atoms, later segs shift by the insert length, earlier segs re-anchor unchanged; ownership rows pinned (`b b b early early early mid mid early early early`). **Defect B (empty-head revive)**: inserting into an empty display anchored the revive record's start against the _pre-insert_ text — `anchorAt(tLen)` at `tLen === len` produced the dynamic E sentinel, which then advanced past the appended atoms so the record claimed nothing and the neighbour's `{B,E}` record displayed them (`b` stayed empty, `tail` gained `X`). Fix: insert the payload first, then bind `s` to the first appended atom on the post-insert text (`e: E`, `g: maxG+1`), skipped when an existing record already covers the insert point. **Defect C (`'dead'` collision)**: the internal deletion sentinel was the string `'dead'`, colliding with a valid caller block id — `DEAD` is now a `unique symbol` exported from `text/model.ts`; all consumers (placement `displayEdge`/`displayParentOf`/ancestry walks, run view, facade, test oracles) compare against the shared symbol; a block literally named `"dead"` is an ordinary editable block (text, subtree, anchors, JSON, merge, deletion, undo, reload all exercised). **Semantic edge pinned, not "fixed"**: the vendored UndoManager resurrects deleted atoms as _new_ items; item-bound anchors on the tombstoned originals resolve to the gap under `followUndoneDeletions=false`, so a resurrected atom can be claimed by different coverage than the original's owner — visible content stays convergent and atom-preserving, only ownership drifts (documented in the ADR _Undo inverses_). Coverage: both delivery orders + duplicate delivery for A, binary encode/apply reload, follow-up insert/delete, inline atoms, marks, undo/redo, explicit ownership assertions. Evidence: `test:crdt` **1242 pass/7 skip** (was 1233/7); corpus evidence unchanged — model/doc `lost-edit×129, unrecoverable-loss×5`, raw classes identical to baseline (the defects were facade-scenario bugs the 150-seed op mix doesn't generate). `pnpm test` 1231 pass, `test:dom` 142 pass, `check` 0/0, `lint` clean. Scratch debug test removed; no commits.

- 2026-09-20 — **Follow-up work unit 3 (provider-schema-boundary half) complete — validated-staging boundary on every write path.** The gate-2 "syncs but signals" regime is replaced by refusal at the boundary: `sync.ts` gains `applyUpdateStaged` (merge the incoming payload into a throwaway doc seeded with live state, `checkSchema` the merged result, integrate only when clean — BC is connectionless so handshake-schema metadata was rejected as the mechanism; staging is the only option that gets `meta.v` LWW right for free and cannot refuse schema-less incremental updates). **Inbound**: BC + websocket `messageSync` handlers stage SyncStep2/Update payloads — refused updates never mutate the live doc, never persist, never rebroadcast (all `doc.on('update')`-driven); SyncStep1 replies are withheld when our own doc is problem-state; corrupt payloads keep the `message-error` + `console.error` contract. **Outbound**: `_storeUpdate`/`_updateHandler`, `connectBc` SyncStep2 publish, `beforeApplyUpdatesCallback`, `storeState` all upgraded from `unversioned`-only to any schema problem. **IDB hydration**: fast path unchanged (clean batch applies); problem batch re-stages row-by-row — clean rows hydrate, poisoned rows are skipped and left in the store byte-intact (non-destructive), refusal suppresses `synced`, rejects `whenSynced` with `SchemaMismatchError`, and still joins the BC room (recoverability); a self-poisoned doc takes the same path. **`synced` honesty**: websocket `synced` only on an applied SyncStep2 — a refused handshake yields no false sync claim. **New suite** `src/tests/crdt/providers/schema-boundary.test.ts` (8 tests): v99 refused on BC + ws + IDB-reload, unversioned self-poison refuse/heal, schema-less incrementals apply, no rebroadcast (byte-subseq on sent frames), bytes-intact + surgical mixed-store hydration. **Two gate2 tests re-pinned to the new contract** (`envelope.test.ts` unknown-meta.v → refused + never persisted/shipped; `compaction-schema.test.ts` coexist → v1 doc never enters unsupported state, `bx` arrival correctly LWW-dependent). **Server topology documented** (`crdt-v14-providers.md`): opaque byte-relay is the supported topology (upstream `y-websocket` `setupWSConnection` with no persistence hook qualifies); any server interpreting the y-protocols sync format cannot read the envelope and is NOT compatible — its own v13 frames drop at the version gate, its pushed data would die at staging anyway. - 2026-09-20 — **Follow-up work unit 3 (corpus-strictness half) complete — strict lane green, zero real loss found.** The random corpus used to report model/doc `lost-edit×129, unrecoverable-loss×5` as "evidence"; it now enforces a strict gate with an atom-level oracle. **Oracle** (`classifyTagAtoms`/`locateTagAtoms` on the model+doc adapters, `TagAtom`/`AtomFate` types in `crdt-ops.ts`): at each successful `insertText` the runner pins the written atoms' engine ids; at the barrier each atom is classified `present`/`moved`/`tombstoned`/`dead-owner`/`unreachable`/`uncovered`/`gone` — distinguishing explicit deletion, owner-movement, pending-destroyed coverage, and real loss. **Cross-replica rule**: an atom hard (`uncovered`/`gone`/`unreachable`) on peer A but soft on any other replica is pending-destroyed → `convergent-loss`; hard on every replica is a violation UNLESS the run observed environment loss (`sawLoss`: reload-state regression or dropped pending state, now tracked as two separate channels). **Store lookup is safe**: `findIndexSS` is called only after clock containment is verified (it throws on absent clocks — triage bug fixed). **Classification of all 134 residual seed-level cases**: verdict totals identical on `model` and `doc` — `present×1972, deleted-legit×2000, moved-edit×1176, convergent-loss×84`, zero `lost-edit`, zero `unreachable-block`; per-seed verdicts print in the corpus run and the mapping is recorded in `docs/crdt-v14-harness.md`. The five tags that surfaced `lost-edit` mid-triage (`µ24x39`, `µ24x58`, `µ135x42`, `µ135x86`, `µ135x97`) were proven pending-destroyed coverage records by direct per-replica store inspection (integrated on one replica, `pendingStructs` on the others — Yjs applies a deleteSet on receipt while structs wait on destroyed deps). **No real model defect found → no model fix required, none was reclassified to force green.** **Lanes**: `model`+`doc` strict (any hard violation fails, engine stack frames do not excuse crashes); `raw` diagnostic (copy-semantics evidence); seed-86/140 upstream crash repros replayed by a permanent test asserting `upstream-engine-crash` evidence — never a gate failure. **Injection proof**: permanent self-tests fail the strict lane on an injected `uncovered` fate (lossless schedule → `lost-edit`), an adapter throw (→ `crash`), and a `locateTagAtoms→null` success (→ `lost-edit`). Failure artifacts now persist `evidence` + `tagVerdicts`; `seed-5.model.json` self-cleaned once green. Evidence: `test:crdt` corpus file 470/470 across raw+model+doc; full-lane numbers in the run log. No commits.

- 2026-09-20 — **Follow-up work unit 2 complete — commands integrated into typed nodes; compatibility bridge removed.** `src/lib/crdt/nodes.ts` is now the single mutation surface: typed DocBlock/DocText/DocInline methods (`insertChildren`, `deleteChildren`, `insertParts`, `deleteParts`, `insertAt`, `deleteAt`, `formatAt`, `setData`, `isInDocument`, …) called directly by `Block`/`Text`/`InlineBlock` wrappers, `block.utils`/`text.utils`/`edytor.utils` operations, the events/selection/history/hotkey layer, and `CodePlugin`. `src/lib/crdt/compat.ts` is **deleted**; `FACADE_MUTATORS` (the mutation-name Proxy), the fake `yRootBlock`, and `_docVersion` are gone. Cache invalidation keys on the facade-owned `version` token — bumped by every mutating facade call and every committed update — so reads inside an uncommitted transaction still observe read-your-writes without a Proxy enumerating method names. Audits: zero production imports of `compat.ts`; zero runtime `.yText`/`.yBlock`/`.yChildren`/`.yContent`/`._item`/fake `.doc`/`FACADE_MUTATORS`/`yRootBlock` outside docs comments. **Browser-test adaptation to the WU3 contract**: hydration refusal now suppresses `synced` and rejects `whenSynced`, and `storeState` refuses schema-problem docs — so the collab route catches `provider.whenSynced` into `window.__EDYTOR_SYNC_ERROR__`, and the schema spec seeds the v99 update directly into `edytor-v14:<room>`'s `updates` store via a new `seedDocument` helper on the route's collaboration test runtime (writes `custom.generation` + the raw encoded update, bypassing `storeState` by design). **Pre-existing defect fixed**: `insertLineBreak` in `beforeInputCommands.ts` inserted `'\n'` without marks — a soft break inside bold produced unmarked runs (reproduced identically at clean HEAD `d9b7de0`, not a migration regression); it now resolves marks exactly like `insertText` (`getInsertionMarksForSelectionReplacement` ?? `getMarksForTypedInsertion`), which also fixes desktop Shift+Enter mark inheritance. - 2026-09-21 — **Follow-up work unit 4 complete — real-websocket browser proof + packed Svelte consumer.** **Local opaque relay** (`tests/editor-dom/ws-relay.ts`): dependency-free RFC6455 server in the Playwright worker process (no `ws` dep — none resolvable; `y-websocket` is v13-tied and unusable) implementing exactly the documented supported topology — group sockets by room path, forward binary frames verbatim, never decode the v14 envelope. Evidence: serial matrix 1209 tests ~6.8m — chromium 372p/1s, firefox 371p/2s (post-fix rerun), webkit 369p/6s/1 flaky→pass, mobile-chromium 45p, mobile-webkit 42p/3s; socket spec 6/6 on all three engines; multiclient+companion 10/10 chromium rerun post-WU2; `pnpm test` 1247p/1s/11t, `test:dom` 142p, `test:crdt` 1258p/7s, `check` 0/0, `lint` clean (eslint flat-config now ignores `**/dist`,`**/dist-ssr` + covers `.mjs`; prettierignore gains `dist-ssr`), packed-consumer ALL PASS. Doc updated: `docs/archive/crdt-v14-browser-proof.md`. No commits.

- 2026-09-21 — **Gate-F1 adversarial review resolved — all six findings closed; engine green for WU5.** Review doc `docs/archive/crdt-v14-gateF1-review.md` (resolution section appended). **F1 (correctness blocker, fixed in `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts`)**: the insert paths trusted coverage _existence_, never coverage _winning_ — a losing record on the block's own list suppressed the empty-display revive record while a rival's higher-generation claim owned the typed atoms (merge→split→delete→delete→insert repro: text typed into `v` displayed under `thief`). One mechanism now guards every boundary: `claimRoutesToB` replays the exact `(g, resolved-start, stamp)` contest `computeOwnership` runs (shared `ClaimKey`/`claimKeyBetter`) at the fresh atoms with post-insert anchor resolution — no pre-existing record boundary can fall inside a new atom run, so one point decides the run's owner. Empty display: revive record written iff the append winner does not route to `b`. Display-end append (the generalization the corpus found — seed-20 `stolen:sp20-49`, atoms born inside the rival's split claim at the seam): bare `text.insert` now checks the winner and writes a `{insertAt, insertAt+n, g:maxG+1}` claim on `b`'s own list, appended last for append-last display order. Left edge: the `isB` shortcut removed — a begin-sentinel start can still lose a contested prepend — every left-edge insert takes the per-seg record rewrite (`g:maxG+1`). `wu1-ownership.test.ts`: 11 probes, `it.fails` removed, adds rival-wins-partial/split-coverage, dead-owner revive, and coverage-seam variants. **F3 (`stolen` verdict)**: `classifyTagAtoms` takes `TagClassifyContext` — `insertOwners` (at-birth steal signature, dominates legit) + `legitOwners` (recorded split `newId`/merge `intoId` PLUS the atoms' home block — the natural owner reclaiming them when a claim dissolves — and the insert target). Unexplained visible owner → `stolen` → `stolen-edit`, hard on every strict lane. First cut over-flagged claim-dissolve reverts (seed-46); the home-block rule fixed the false positives while real steals still fired — then vanished once the engine fix landed. **F4 (correlated loss)**: run-global `sawLoss` excuse removed; `lostRanges` records the item-id ranges each lossy reload actually destroyed (pending structs + pendingDs captured pre-reload, regressed SV tails) and `strandedRanges` the post-barrier residue — a hard-on-every-replica atom is `convergent-loss` only when its own id or a `tagAtomDeps` coverage stamp intersects either set. WU3a injection probe: real destroyed-by-reload loss stays `convergent-loss`, unrelated injected `uncovered` stays `lost-edit`. **F5 (schema boundary)**: `checkSchema` validates `meta.schema` — `kind:'foreign'` when `meta.v` is supported but the manifest name is missing/different (`SCHEMA_NAME='edytor-doc'`); bare foreign manifest on an untouched doc still refuses. `SchemaMismatchError` reports the observed name; staging + all outbound paths inherit it; WU3b foreign-schema probe now asserts refusal. **F6**: stale-view caveat verified in place (`edytor-doc.ts:544-549` + wu2 probe). **F2 deferred to WU5 — checklist item**: staging fast path for updates that cannot touch `meta` — measured 18.58ms/update staged vs 0.055ms direct (~337×) on a 1,000-block doc (full-state encode 305,840B, 100 updates) — **constraint: `checkSchema` must stay non-bypassable**; refusal semantics are the boundary contract, not an implementation detail. Evidence: `test:crdt` **1285p/7s/0f** (49 files; corpus 470/470, evidence only `moved-edit`/`deleted-legit`/`convergent-loss`, zero `stolen-edit`/`lost-edit`), `pnpm test` **1273p/0f**, `test:dom` **142p/0f**, `check` 0/0, `lint` clean. No commits.

- 2026-09-21 — **Follow-up work unit 6 complete — dense ownership rows replaced by disjoint intervals.** `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts`: `computeOwnership()` no longer fills per-position `atomOwner`/`atomClaim` arrays; `sweepOwnership` builds ordered disjoint `OwnInterval {i0,i1,owner,claim}` per backing text — claim-endpoint sweep with a lazy-expiry max-heap over the unchanged `(g, s0, stamp)` winner key; adjacent elementary spans coalesce only on identical winning record (a gap breaks coalescing so same-record coverage on both sides of a hole stays two intervals — the dense `claims[i] === entry` semantics); interval count ≤ 2·claims − 1, O(c log c) per text independent of length. Query helpers `intervalAt`/`ownerAt` (point → binary search), `intervalsOver` (range), `nearestOwned` (boundary hops). `maxG` still counts every live slice record including losers; merge routing/cycles, dead owners, unresolved anchors, empty ranges unchanged; `claimRoutesToB` kept as direct contest replay (it must evaluate post-insert resolution). **All production consumers migrated**: `flatten`/`flattenTracked` emit only `iv.owner === b && iv.claim === rec` spans (WU1 no-duplicate invariant preserved — interval identity is the winning _record_, not just owner); `runs.ts` `AtomRow` → interval rows with the same sweep; `edytor-doc.ts` `emissionOffset` sums matching interval widths, `resolveAnchor` uses point + nearest-owned queries. No production code builds a dense row. **Test-only oracle** `src/tests/crdt/harness/dense-ownership-oracle.ts` keeps the corrected dense algorithm; `src/tests/crdt/text/ownership-intervals.test.ts` (15 tests) differentiates interval vs oracle per position/record/`maxG`/flatten/`nearestOwned`/`resolveAnchor` across WU1 regressions, merges, cycles, dead holders, equal endpoints, Unicode, inline atoms, delivery orders, duplication, reload, undo. **Counters redefined** (`bench/lib/baseline.js`): `ownershipPositions` = emitted intervals, `claimsVisited` = records swept — the WU5 goalposts now measure what scales. **Measured** (`bench/results/2026-09-21T00-32-18-276Z.json` vs `…00-10-26-013Z`, `docs/crdt-v14-benchmarks.md` §15): keystroke @5k `ownershipPositions` 318,891→5,000, ownMs 9.411→6.151, facade 11.771→11.302 (p95 23.5→19.2); @1k ownMs 1.113→0.684; text-100k ownMs 0.784→**0.007** (~109×, intervals 9 flat at every length); claims-128 ownMs 0.172→0.102 with 129 intervals (was ~9,934 positions); fragmented-history own 0.822 vs fresh 0.745 (+10%, no cliff — sweeps 1,050 records vs 1,000); ops own ~0.71–0.80; resolveAnchor 1.49→1.14; retained heap unchanged (dense arrays were transient garbage — win is CPU/GC, not heap). Remaining ownMs is per-claim resolution (~0.7 µs/claim), not per-atom traversal. Evidence: `test:crdt` 1304p/7s, `test:crdt:extensive` 1310p/1s, `pnpm test` 1293p/1s/11t, `test:dom` 142p, `check`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` 0e/0w, `lint` clean, packed-consumer ALL PASS, chromium lane 372p/1s. - 2026-09-21 — **Follow-up work unit 7 complete — shared incremental indexes across commands and rendering** (full record `docs/crdt-v14-benchmarks.md` §16, artifact `bench/results/2026-09-21T01-50-52-327Z.json`). **Problem**: every command preamble, anchor resolution, `takeSnap`, and `DocChange` commit paid a fresh `collectBlocks`+`computeOwnership`+`resolvePlacements`+`childrenIndex` (~9.8 ms of the 11.3 ms facade p50 @5k — the §5 deferred "facet-driven DocChange" item). **Architecture**: `bindRuns` attach state is now the single per-document model-state owner — `WeakMap<EngineDoc, ViewRecord>`, lease/refcounted so multiple `EdytorDoc` facades share it, torn down on last dispose or doc destroy. It maintains `blocks` (full `BlockRec` map patched per event), lazy ownership over interval rows, lazy `placements`/`kids` facets rebuilt only on structure/placement version bumps (content-only commits preserve map identity), `modelCtx` shared `ModelView`, and `commitInfo()` `{seq, fast, content, meta}`. `bindModel` takes a `view()` provider — `bindEdytorDoc` injects `(doc) => R.modelState(doc)` — so typed-node command preambles, anchors, projection, `takeSnap`, `childrenIdsIn`, and `DocChange` all read the same indexes; bare `bindModel` keeps the fresh-collect fallback. **Facet classification**: `content` dirties only touched rows; `slices`/`del`/registry inserts structural; `at` placement-only; `id`/`type`/`data` metadata; unknown attrs conservative-structural. **Read-your-writes**: `syncTransaction` walks `doc._transaction.changed` mid-transaction so command sequences observe their own writes without waiting for the update event. **`DocChange`**: `updateHandler` skips no-op events via `lastSeenSeq`; `fast` commits run two-pass `diffFast` — validate all touched ids against the previous skeleton WITHOUT mutating it, escalate to the full diff on visible-but-untracked, then patch only touched content/meta; structural commits take the full path (correctness over fast-path eligibility). A first-pass `diffFast` that mutated `prev` during validation caused silent mirror divergence (content ops emitting no change after escalation) — caught by the mirror differential, fixed by splitting validate/patch into two passes. **Measured** (WU6→WU7): keystroke facade p50 0.215→**0.047** / 1.496→**0.230** / 11.302→**0.839** ms @100/1k/5k (~4.6×/6.5×/13.5×); shared `view()` read 0.4/1.4/3.5 µs vs 0.14/1.45/9.77 ms fresh-equivalent; `runsRecomputed`=1, `commitFast`=1 per keystroke at every size; DocChange callback 4.226→**0.122 ms**, no-subscriber 1.496→0.234; structural ops keep `commitFast`=0 yet improve (split 0.659→0.217, merge 0.541→0.162, same-parent move 0.331→0.080, paste-subtree 2.023→0.768 — write side no longer re-collects, full snap reuses `kids`); `resolveAnchor` 1.137→**0.009**; `undo` 0.042→0.029; `serialization` treeWalk 1.708→0.515; retained heap within noise (+23.2/+151.7 MB @1k/5k vs +29.7/+153.5 — maintained maps are new O(blocks) state, no material regression). `engine-api.ts` typings extended for `_transaction.changed` + item `parent`/`parentSub`. New `src/tests/crdt/runs/shared-state.test.ts` (15 tests): shared-state identity, facet invalidation, placements/kids identity preservation, `commitInfo` classification, `tr.changed` read-your-writes, `DocChange` fast-path payloads, two-facade lease sharing, dispose/destroy teardown. Evidence: `test:crdt` **1319p/7s** (49 files), `test:crdt:extensive` **1325p/1s**, `check`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` 0e/0w, `lint` clean, packed-consumer in artifact (467,746 B min / 120,448 B brotli). No commits.

- 2026-09-21 — **Follow-up work unit 8 complete — direct formatted range reads; whole-delta materialization removed from the content read path** (full record `docs/crdt-v14-benchmarks.md` §17, artifact `bench/results/2026-09-21T02-29-48-807Z.json`). **Problem**: `itemsOfRange()` rendered the WHOLE backing text through `text.toDelta().toJSON()` then clipped — a ~2k visible slice of a shared 100k-unit text paid a full render + JSON conversion per read, and sibling slices re-rendered the same sequence. A stale commit-time cache was not a valid substitute because fresh reads must keep read-your-writes inside an open transaction. **Architecture** (`src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts`, outside the vendored engine — no patch): a forward-only `RangeCursor` walks the live item list (`_start`/`right`) like `YNode.toDelta()` does — deleted items skipped, `ContentFormat` markers (non-countable) fold into copy-on-write mark state (a `shared` flag mirroring the delta builder's `useFormats` so emitted marks/checkpoint seeds can't be corrupted in place), `ContentString.str` slices directly, `ContentType` children emit one live `getAttr`-read `{id,type,data}` item, adjacent text pieces under `canonKey`-equal marks merge — identical output shapes either way. Mid-transaction RYW holds because the walk sees the same live links `toDelta()` reads; detached nodes (`doc === null`) still read empty without touching `.delta`. **Checkpoint index**: `readCheckpoints` builds sparse `{pos,it,formats}` seeds every ≤64 walked items (`READ_CP_ITEMS`); `runs.ts` keeps `readIndexes` per text, marks `dirtyContentTexts` on any `content`-facet event (local edit, remote update, undo/redo, format, inline write — all surface as `content`) and rebuilds lazily on next read; `computeFresh` seeds one cursor per backing text shared across a block's owned segs; `readIndexes` drops entries on block removal and clears on resync. **Placement**: `contentItemsOf` now delegates to `T.itemsOfRange(content, 0, content.length)` — zero production `toDelta().toJSON()` calls remain in the content-read path (the raw-node test adapter's `contentTail` keeps its own `content.delta` walk for diversity). **Counters**: `RunView.debug` gains `itemsWalked`/`markersWalked` (items stepped over / markers applied in maintained range reads — the scaling metric) and `readIndexBuilds`; `reset()` zeroes all. **Measured** (`rangeReads` lane — 50 sibling blocks × ~2k slices of one 100k formatted text): warm read p50 **0.0102 ms** vs the pre-WU8 `toDelta().toJSON()` reference **0.3995 ms** (~39×), ~61 items walked vs ~100k rendered; first read incl. the one-time index build 0.452 ms p50 (~0.009 ms/read amortized over 50 reads); `readIndexBuilds`=10 (one per sample doc). **Tests**: `src/tests/crdt/runs/range-reads.test.ts` (18) — oracle parity of every range of a richly formatted text vs the retained delta-JSON reader, mark set/clear boundaries incl. null clears, surrogate pairs, live inline attrs, detached guard, shared-cursor forward/backward reads, checkpoint identical-reads + bounded gaps, mid-txn RYW (stateless + maintained per step), index staleness on insert-before-checkpoint, range-sized cost on one long text, disjoint-seg cursor sharing, remote format parity, undo rebuilds, cross-block mark interning, frozen snapshots, decorations overlay, multi-backing merged reads. **Lanes**: `test:crdt` 1337p/7s (50 files), `test:crdt:extensive` 1343p/1s, `pnpm test` 1326p/1s/11t (63 files), `test:dom` 142p, `test:integration:serial` 1197p/12s, `check`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` 0e/0w, `lint` clean, `pnpm build`+publint clean, packed-consumer ALL PASSED, `bench:runs` green (invalidation granularity unchanged — 1 block recomputed/op). Vendor tree untouched. No commits.

upstream's `ArraySearchMarker` pool couldn't seed `applyDelta` because the cursor also needs `currentFormats` (upstream disabled markers on any format integration). **Patch** (`src/lib/crdt/vendor/yjs/src/`): markers gain a `formats` snapshot (left-edge `currentFormats`) written only at quiescent points — never mid-mutation (an earlier `forward()`-hook variant captured transient `{hl,b}` state and produced byte-divergent stores; abs-pos resolve 0.13→0.0004 ms; rel-pos create 0.13→0.0005 ms; sequential append ~1.0→0.009 ms on unguarded fragmented text vs ~1.0 ms (correctly rejected) on format-dense text — position-dependent by design, never a regression. **Oracle**: `marker-seed.test.ts` (11) replays ops with markers on/off requiring byte-identical `encodeStateAsUpdate` + identical deltas; `interop.mjs` syncs patched↔HEAD peers through bootstrap/edits/formats/anchors/GC/undo/conflicts — all pass. **Memory verdict**: the +120MB post-undo lifecycle was V8 conservative-stack pinning of dead loop locals — same-frame +78–117MB vs deferred-frame −0.3MB; lane now unwinds via setImmediate (churn +7.7MB held, **0.0MB** post-teardown; `runs.ts` WeakMap lease verified collectable). **Fixture fixes**: reparent `d0`→`d19` (was a no-op — target never attached — now 73B/op); undo lane times via `beforeTransaction`/`beforeObserverCalls`/`afterAllTransactions` (was 0/0 — `UndoManager` bypasses `doc.transact`). **Bundle**: only upstream's 396B `logNode` is tree-shakeable; removal rejected (public `edytor/crdt` surface) — `used-surface` lane proves consumers without `import *` pay nothing. **Rejected**: forward()-hook snapshots, formatted-retain seeding, renderer-path seeding, dead-export removal. **Lanes**: `test:crdt` 1348p/7s (51 files), `test:crdt:extensive` 1354p/1s, `pnpm test` 1337p/1s/11t (64 files), `test:dom` 142p, check/typechecks 0e/0w, lint clean (vendor-baseline ignore added), `pnpm package`+publint clean, dts regen emitted (expected TS2589 only), marker-seed 11/11, interop all pass. **Remaining risks**: snapshot invalidation is conservative-by-design (miss → rejected seed = slower, still correct — byte-identity oracle is the net); independent review of marker invalidation + benchmark comparability still open. No commits.

- 2026-09-21 — **Gate-F2 adversarial review resolved — verdict FAIL → PASS; F2-B1 blocker fixed, F2-M1 wire divergence closed at the facade boundary, F2-m1 disclosed.** Review doc `docs/archive/crdt-v14-gateF2-review.md` (per-finding Resolution paragraphs + post-fix suite results appended). **F2-B1 (blocker, `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts`)**: `deleteRange` resolved all owned segs once then deleted in displayed order — tombstoned atoms stop counting immediately, so a second span of the SAME backing text deleted at stale coordinates (rival-owned atoms died; `Exceeded content range` mid-loop left a committed partial delete since `transact` doesn't roll back). Now: one flatten → per-seg overlap spans → two pre-mutation validation passes (every span resolvable + in live range; per-text spans pairwise disjoint) → deletes execute per backing text in **descending `i0`** so a landed delete only shifts atoms after the remaining spans — every recorded coordinate stays live until its turn, and a refused delete returns before the first mutation (atomic). Descending order chosen over re-flattening: same semantics, one flatten, no mid-loop anchor drift. `wu6-delete-range.test.ts` → 7 probes: retained three plus gap-spanning mid-range, delete-all + over-range clamp, undo (convergent re-contest — the WU1-pinned redone-anchor semantic, disclosed as review limitation 7), concurrent local+remote multi-seg delete. **F2-M1 (major, upstream-inherited)**: lone UTF-16 surrogates stored verbatim locally but encode to U+FFFD on the wire — permanent same-item divergence. Fixed at the facade boundary (vendor untouched): `sanitizeWireString` (`toWellFormed`) + `sanitizeWireJson` (recursive keys+values clone) in `src/lib/utils/json.ts` applied at every ingress — `T.insertIntoText` payload+marks, `M.insertBlock` `sanitizeSpec` (ids/types/data/content/inline fields, recursive children — covers insertBlock/insertSubtree/`E.init`/`setBlock` children/`duplicateBlock`/seed), `buildInline`, `setInlineData`, `setBlockType`/`setBlockData`, `formatRangeIn`, `splitBlock` newId, merge-reset defaultType, `appendItems`, migration materialization (`migrate.ts`), `nodes.ts` handle id rebind. Identifier references stay verbatim (resolve against stored normalized ids or refuse); valid pairs untouched; caller objects never mutated. `wu9-surrogate-wire.test.ts` rewritten — raw-engine probe reframed as boundary documentation (proves why the boundary exists), "diverges" name fixed, 10 probes pin per-ingress normalization + pair pass-through. **F2-m1**: benchmarks §3 typing cell now carries v13 0.0094ms vs v14 0.0198ms local p50 (~2.1×) + remote numbers + raw-microbenchmark caveat. Evidence: `test:crdt` **1396p/7s**, `test:crdt:extensive` **1402p/1s** (331/331 vendored upstream, 470-check corpus — evidence only `moved-edit`/`deleted-legit`/`convergent-loss`), `pnpm test` **1385p/0f**, `test:dom` **142p/0f**, `test:integration:serial` **1197p/12s** (all five browser projects), `check`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` 0e/0w, `lint` clean, `build`+`package`+publint clean — the release-gate set re-run end to end. - **Hardening U1 complete — P4 marker patch repaired, cannot corrupt formatting (R1).** Root cause: `updateMarkerFormats` folded a freshly integrated `ContentFormat` into **every** cached snapshot at-or-right of the insertion, even past a live same-key format marker that still governs the key at the snapshot's position — so a `retain(50)+insert(Z,{b})` negation overwrote the snapshot inside a `[100,200)` bold span with `{}` and the next seeded insert dropped bold from the span tail (bold coverage `[101,153)` instead of `[101,203)`). Repair (ynode.js `updateMarkerFormats`): the fold now walks the linked list right from the insertion and **stops at the first live same-key `ContentFormat`** — markers anchored at-or-before it (i.e. whose left-edge state precedes the boundary item's own effect) still get the fold; everything past it keeps its still-valid snapshot. Position comparison stays list-order-aware (same-index non-countable/deleted run left of the insertion is excluded via a lazily built `Set`). Candidates the walk never reaches — stale records whose anchor was unlinked/merged or whose index lies about the true position — get `formats = null` instead of a blind update: **invalidate, never corrupt**; the seed path's anchor-linkage check already rejects such anchors. No other path needed changing: format-item tombstones clear all snapshots in `Item#delete`, re-anchors/merges clear via `overwriteMarker`/`updateMarkerChanges`/`Item#mergeWith`, remote integration and undo clear the pool wholesale. Evidence: `r1-p4-format.test.ts` **22/22** — pinned facade + raw-engine repros flip green unweakened, plus distant/format-before-span, mark-clears (`{key:null}`), alternating keys, zero-width/same-index boundaries, deletes through formatted spans, tombstone invalidation, remote integration of the same op, undo/redo through UndoManager, split/coalesce cycles, sparse/dense/holed fixtures, bounded randomized replay, and per-op `toDelta`+store-byte equality vs both `_searchMarker=null` and the materialized pre-P4 baseline; `marker-seed.test.ts` **12/12** (added a compact same-key-boundary regression); `gateF2/wu9-vendor.test.ts` green; a one-off 18 000-op fuzz (3 fixtures × 40 seeds × 150 ops, insert/delete/format/undo/remote/GC) produced zero divergences; `bench/lib/interop.mjs` all checks pass (patched↔baseline bootstrap, alternating edits, format ops across seeded regions, rel-pos anchors, deletes/GC, undo, concurrent edits). Benchmarks post-repair (`bench/lib/engine-micro.mjs quick`, 60 k-item doc): distant retain+insert **0.92 ms/op** (pre-P4 baseline 1.80, pre-repair 0.93 — benefit retained), mid 0.50 vs 0.99, scattered 0.83 vs 1.01, sequential append 1.01 vs 1.02 (parity — format-guarded by design), near unchanged ~0.007; anchor ops single distant 0.0004 vs 0.14 ms, rel-pos create 0.0005 vs 0.13 ms, ×200-resolve 0.46 vs 13.2 ms. The boundary repair costs nothing measurable — it bounds a walk that only runs on local format-item integration. Remaining honesty notes: the fold is incremental only for format inserts; tombstones/remote/undo stay wholesale-invalidate; `formats` snapshots are verified by linkage at seed time, so a stale record degrades to an unseeded walk, never a wrong seed. Full `pnpm test:crdt` lane: **1430 pass / 7 skip / 12 fail** — the 12 failures are all other units' in-flight hardening pins (gateF2 `wu6-delete-range` U3, R3 undo-ownership, R4 snapshot-isolation, R5 commit-notify, R6 oracle-injection), none in U1-owned files. No commits.

- **Hardening U2 complete — refused IndexedDB rows survive compaction (R2).** Mechanism: **blocking**, not selective — `storeState` skips snapshot+delete whenever `_hydrationRefused` is set (checked AFTER the fetch so a refusal discovered inside the same `storeState` call is covered; the fetch still runs so valid peer rows keep hydrating). Rationale: a compacting delete may only remove rows the replacement snapshot represents; a clean live schema is not proof of that once any stored row was refused, and a selective scheme would have to prove each excluded row plus its dependency closure — blocking needs no such proof and is the smallest change consistent with the provider contract. Bookkeeping: `_dbref` still advances past refused rows (fetch cursor only — refused rows are not re-staged); `_dbsize` keeps counting real rows; no counter ever serves as a delete boundary again for a refused instance. Promise/error semantics: `storeState` now returns the full request chain AND the transaction's `complete`/`abort` — it settles only after the storage transaction commits and rejects on failure (upstream dropped the inner chain); synchronous `transact`/`fetchUpdates` throws are deferred into rejections at both call sites; the timed path and `_storeUpdate`'s row write forward failures to `'message-error'` instead of leaving unobserved rejections. Tests (`hardening/r2-idb-compaction.test.ts`, 10/10): the 3 pinned tests flipped green unweakened + new coverage — close/reopen re-refuses and stays blocked, repeated attempts are inert (no partial deletion, accepted writes still persist), dependent refused deltas survive in BOTH seed orders (engine staging detail pinned: an out-of-order delta goes entirely pending, so the LATER clean row is the refused one), a late foreign row refused inside `storeState`'s own fetch blocks that very call, a fully-admitted store still compacts to one row with `await storeState` meaning commit, and closed-handle failures reject/emit. Contract documented in `docs/crdt-v14-providers.md` §Refusal ↔ compaction.

- 2026-09-21 — **Hardening U0 complete — every confirmed review failure is pinned RED; zero production changes.** Each file's header records the observed-vs-expected diff; each assertion encodes the CORRECT semantic so the same tests go green when the owning unit lands. Pins: **R1 (P4 marker corruption)** — `r1-p4-format.test.ts`: facade + raw-engine repro produce bold coverage `[[50,51],[101,153]]` instead of `[[50,51],[101,203]]` (the last 50 chars of the bold span lose `b`); the per-step differential vs `_searchMarker = null` AND vs the pre-P4 vendored baseline (`bench/vendor-baseline`, runIf materialized) stay byte+delta identical through ops 0–2 and diverge at op 3 (the `Q` insert). **R2 (compaction deletes refused rows)** — `r2-idb-compaction.test.ts`: explicit `storeState()` collapses `[v99-row, live-snapshot]` to the snapshot alone (v99 bytes gone); the timed `_dbsize >= PREFERRED_TRIM_SIZE` path does the same after 505 local updates + debounce; the mixed valid+v99 seed shows the valid row hydrating while the refused row is still deleted. **R3 (undo restores into the wrong paragraph)** — `r3-undo-ownership.test.ts`: post-undo `b='hello world'`, `tail=''` instead of `'hello '/'world'`; the wrong ownership survives binary save→reload, is derived identically by a peer that never ran the delete, carries restored marks into the wrong block, and absorbs a concurrent remote edit (`b='hello !world'`). Noted side observation: a remote edit landing BETWEEN the delete and the undo is currently eaten together with the missing restore — left for U3 to classify (capture-window vs real selective-undo loss). **R4 (mutable public snapshots)** — `r4-snapshot-isolation.test.ts`: `project()`/`contentItems`/`block.items` return live `{marks,data}` object references — mutating them reaches live state with no `update` event, changes `encodeStateAsUpdate` bytes (a peer synced after the mutation inherits the corruption), and poisons the `runs` checkpoint index on the next recompute; `runs()`/`contentJSON` already intern/clone and stay clean — the pin asserts isolation on every surface. **R5 (premature subscriber notify)** — `r5-commit-notify.test.ts`: a mid-transaction `block.runs` read publishes `'ab'` to `subscribeBlock` before the commit publishes `'abc'`; control case (no mid-txn read) already green. **R6 (oracle fault acceptance)** — `r6-oracle-injection.test.ts`: an unrequested delete inside `setMark` returns `ok:true` (`deleted-legit [tombstoned×4]`); an unrequested merge into a recorded split destination returns `ok:true` (`moved-edit [moved:sp-unrel×3]`) — the oracle checks end-state fate but not schedule authorization. **Gate-F2 amendment**: `wu6-delete-range.test.ts` undo probe now asserts restoration to the deleting owner (`early='dehj'`, rivals unchanged) instead of the redistributed-ownership expectation that blessed R3 — documented inline why the old expectation was wrong. **R7** (benchmark/source-identification concerns) and **R8** (`docs/crdt-v14-providers.md` upstream `ws-relay.ts` example incompatible with the v14 envelope) — no tests by design; R8 is deferred to U6. Every pin reproduces; nothing was left unpinned or unfalsifiable.

- 2026-09-21 — **Hardening U6 complete — transport proof closed + upstream-compatibility claim corrected (R8).** **Doc fix**: `docs/crdt-v14-providers.md` §server compatibility classification now states what the review verified against upstream `y-websocket` `bin/utils.js` (v1.5.4 AND v2.1.0, same shape): `setupWSConnection` is a PARTICIPATING server, not an opaque relay — even with no persistence hook it creates a server-side `WSSharedDoc`, `messageListener` decodes `varuint messageType` handling only sync(0)/awareness(1), and it initiates its own un-enveloped SyncStep1. A v14 frame's leading `14` reads as message type 14 → unhandled → **dropped, never relayed**; the server's own frames hit our version gate as `protocol-mismatch`. The prior "upstream qualifies as an opaque relay" claim was wrong and is corrected; the documented supported topology is opaque-relay-only with `tests/editor-dom/ws-relay.ts` (real RFC6455 socket) and `websocket.test.ts`'s in-memory room as the ONLY proven implementations — a participating server must run the vendored v14 engine + protocol modules (none exists). Matching comments fixed in `ws-relay.ts` and `crdt-v14-browser-proof.md`. **New spec** `tests/editor-dom/collaboration-websocket-3client.spec.ts` (5 tests, THREE independent browser contexts each, `disableBc` forced by the route): (1) held concurrent split(`collab-b2`@2→`be`/`splitA`=`ta`)+bold-marked insert+`moveBlocks` → converged with per-replica semantic assertions (block ids/order, exact `{text,marks}` runs, moved-block identity); (2) one-client `provider.disconnect()` partition with A↔B still syncing → semantically divergent edits both sides → `connect()` re-handshake → converged, then `page.reload()` heals over the socket alone (no IndexedDB on this route); (3) held three-way edits released `permute:true, duplicates:3` → converged; `dropNext` dropped one inbound update to BOTH other members → healed by resync; `setLatency` fan-out → converged; (4) selective undo — A's `undo()` removes only `-A`, B's marked `-B` and C's `-C` survive run-for-run on all replicas; B's undo then removes `-B`+its mark while `-C` persists; (5) rogue-peer refusal over a REAL socket — a Node `WebSocket` room member answers SyncStep1 with a SyncStep2 carrying `meta.v=99` (clientID=MAX_SAFE_INTEGER → deterministic LWW winner): the joining client's staging boundary refuses every reply, `provider.synced`/`edytor.synced` stay false across ≥4 resync cycles, zero blocks render, `evil-v99` never enters the doc, `schema-mismatch`+`message-error` fire — then two clean peers join and the refused client syncs honestly → all three converge. **Evidence**: `playwright test --project={chromium,firefox,webkit} tests/editor-dom/collaboration-websocket` → **11/11 pass each engine** (chromium 9.6s, firefox 12.2s, webkit 18.8s), 0 skip. **Honest limits recorded** (`crdt-v14-browser-proof.md` §limitations): N≤3 contexts, local in-process relay only, no hosted server/TLS/auth, fault injection is harness replay not TCP, rogue peer covers schema refusal only (not hostile framing/floods). No production code touched; packed-consumer unchanged (no need).

- 2026-09-21 — **Hardening U5 complete — the random corpus is now an honest strict semantic oracle; one real placement-model defect pinned (seed 96).** The R6 review found the fate-only oracle accepted unauthorized deletion/movement whenever the end state looked legitimate. **Per-op intent envelopes** (`runner.ts` `planIntent`/`checkEnvelope`): every scheduled op resolves its target and gets a bounded envelope — `atomsIn` (new atoms confined to reachable texts), `atomsOut` (exactly the displayed delete range), `claimsWrite`/`claimsRemove` (holder/kind/`t`/`m`-shaped), `blocksCreate/Remove`, `placements`, exact mark-key sets, `touchedTexts`; undo/redo get envelopes bounded by the peer's own recorded history (resurrection copies, R3 repair claims, re-issued merge claims). `captureOpState` snapshots atoms+claims+del-flags+placements around each op; `checkEnvelope` fails `unrequested-effect`/`mutated-edit` per op, and `recordEffects` feeds the authorization ledger — `blessedClaims` (the barrier's `authorizedClaims`) accepts only envelope-approved claim stamps plus the seed baseline; remote delivery inherits the ORIGINATING op's stamp, so delivery never creates local ambiguity; the pseudo stamp `-1:-1` is never authorized. The barrier `classifyTagAtoms` now classifies `stolen` when an atom's winning claim stamp or any merge-route stamp is outside `authorizedClaims` (the pre-U5 `legitOwners` name-check applies only when no stamp set exists). **Two harness defects found and fixed this way**: (a) `authorizedClaims` was collected but not wired into the final oracle context — ~287 mass `stolen-edit` false positives; (b) `locateTagAtoms` substring search could match a COINCIDENTAL same-substring assembly built from stale fragments of other inserts (seed-26 `µ26x149` matched a `µ26x1`+`49` assembly under `b3` while the real atoms `28:151-157` sat correctly in the insert's own target text) — `locateTagAtoms` now accepts an `onlyNew` set (`diff.atomsNew`) and only returns occurrences composed entirely of fresh atoms; no all-fresh occurrence ⇒ `null` (the existing "success but nothing findable" lost-edit signature). **Determinism**: `peer-set.ts` pins `SEED_DOC_CLIENT_ID`, derives peer ids + per-doc `rand` streams and reload-generation client ids from `schedule.seed`; gate-1 `determinism.test.ts` 4/4 (committed schedule replays identical results twice). **Diagnostic artifacts**: `seed-86.json`/`seed-140.json` restored byte-identical from HEAD and exempted from corpus self-clean (`PINNED_DIAGNOSTIC_ARTIFACTS`) — the live raw seeds pass again while the frozen repros still replay `upstream-engine-crash`; live raw evidence-aborts now land at seeds 37/59 (lib0 `Unexpected case`, `upstream-engine-crash×2`, artifacts regenerate). **REAL MODEL BUG — pinned, not fixed**: seed 96 (model AND doc lanes) crashes at step 185 inside `insertBlock`: `resolvePlacements` rehomes a block whose placement candidates are ALL gone (undone/dropped/cycle-rejected — `placement/model.ts` fallback) at root under `MIN_RANK = {v:RANK_VMIN,t:0}`; Full `pnpm exec vitest run --config ./vitest.crdt.config.ts`: **66 files, 1456 pass / 1 expected-fail / 7 skip** — corpus 470/470 across raw+model+doc (150 seeds each, strict lanes report zero un-pinned violations; evidence `deleted-legit×149, moved-edit×142, convergent-loss×6` identical on model and doc). **Zero `src/lib` changes.**

- 2026-09-21 — **Hardening U4 complete — public read surfaces can no longer alias live replicated state (R4), and `subscribeBlock` publishes only committed snapshots (R5).** **R4 mechanism**: the internal range readers (`readRange`/`itemsOfRange`/`contentItemsOf` in `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts`) keep emitting BORROWED payloads — cursor mark state, checkpoint seeds, and inline `data` attr objects alias live structures on purpose for incremental performance. Isolation happens at a single publication boundary: `protectItems(items, intern)` rewrites each item's `marks`/`data` to the payload's interned deep-frozen canonical clone while item wrappers and the array stay fresh/mutable. `intern` lives on the shared per-document model state (`runs.ts`: `canonKey` → `internMap` → `deepFreeze(JSON clone)`), so equal payloads are `===` across `project()`/`contentItems()`/`runs()` and `runEquals`/`marksEqual` compare by reference; the bare-`bindModel` fallback (no attached state owner) is `deepFreeze(cloneJson(v))`. Applied at every public seam: `M.project()` block content, facade `contentItems()` (hence `DocBlock.items`), `takeSnap().nodeFor` added-subtree `content` (the `DocChange` boundary), and — verified already-interned/cloned — `runs()`, `contentJSON()`, `toJSON()`. Mutating a returned nested object now throws on the frozen value (strict mode) or lands on a detached interned clone — live state, `encodeStateAsUpdate` bytes, remote peers, and the read-checkpoint index are all unreachable; no `update` event fires because no engine write occurs. **R5 mechanism**: `computeRuns` still updates the run cache immediately (mid-transaction `runs()` reads keep read-your-writes) but, while `doc._transaction != null`, queues the block into `pendingNotify` instead of calling listeners; `flushPending()` runs inside the registry `observeDeep` handler — which fires during transaction cleanup, once per outermost committed batch — and `publish` diffs against `publishedRuns` via element-wise `sameRuns` (marks/data by interned reference). Net: at most one notification per block per commit, carrying the FINAL runs; a mid-transaction read no longer leaks `ab` before `abc`; change-then-revert (net-zero) transactions notify nothing; `subscribeBlock` primes `publishedRuns` with the baseline at subscribe so the initial compute is never delivered; teardown clears both maps. **Tests**: `r4-snapshot-isolation.test.ts` 3→**5** (mutating `project()`/`contentItems`/`block.items` nested `marks.link.href` + inline `data.label` — asserts unchanged subsequent reads, unchanged `encodeStateAsUpdate` bytes, zero extra `update` events, peer isolation, no checkpoint poisoning on later recompute, repeated reads, `$state`-proxy-backed payloads) and `r5-commit-notify.test.ts` 2→**6** (mid-transaction read → exactly `['abc']`; control `['abc']`; nested transactions; multiple blocks; net-zero silent; remote/undo commit paths). **Perf** (`bench:runs` vs prior artifact `runs-2026-09-21T06-42-53-843Z.json`): every reuse/invalidation metric identical — warm `runs()` p50 0.0003ms, `contentJSON` p50 0.0004ms, unrelated-edit reuse 999/1000, split/merge/inline fan-outs unchanged; the only deltas are sub-millisecond noise on cold/full-recompute rows (plus a small intern pass on cold first-reads, ~1µs/block, within run variance). Fix belongs to the R3 lifecycle scope (module-level dedupe + refcounted release on last facade dispose); left unpatched here to avoid weakening the per-doc repair guarantee.

- 2026-09-21 — **Hardening Unit 7 complete — repaired editor measured end-to-end; R7 "3.8 ms input pipeline" claim retracted; 5k browser bottleneck located in the editor layer and reported, not patched (zero `src/lib` changes).** **R7 fix**: the old browser lane ended `keystrokeMs` at a rAF callback and `facadeToDomMs` at a MutationObserver over different samples, then subtracted the independent medians — invalid. `bench/browser.js` now opens one record per operation with a shared `t0` and stamps the same keystroke across `keydown → beforeinput → beforeTransaction → beforeObserverCalls → afterTransaction → update → afterAllTransactions → facade.onChange(published, subscribed after the editor's own handler) → first MutationObserver record → document-bubble handlerDone → rAF`. An arm-and-confirm-before-dispatch ordering fix (Runtime.evaluate and Input.dispatchKeyEvent travel different queues) eliminated the earlier run's 11–12/24 arm-race timeouts — post-fix **0/24 timeouts on every keystroke lane at every scale**; the `shared-100k` format lane honestly keeps 1/15 (a `bold:false` no-op produces no DOM mutation). rAF is labeled a scheduling callback, never paint; `facadeToDom` stays a separate lane, never subtracted. **Source identity**: content sha256 of `src/lib` (178 files), vendored engine src (32 files), and the repacked `edytor.tgz` (sha256 `b90ff22c…`, 463,912 B — rebuilt + all packed-consumer checks green); engine labeled "vendored rc.26 + P1/P4 patches", not "unmodified". **Node lanes — WU6/WU7 survive hardening with bounded cost**: facade keystroke p50 0.097/0.408/1.026 ms @100/1k/5k (WU7: 0.047/0.230/0.839 — +0.18/+0.19 ms hardening delta), `commitFast=1`, `runsRecomputed=1`, fragmented-history +7%, `applyUpdateStaged` 0.041 ms (staged=0), seam-vs-caret premium ~0 B, packed consumer brotli 123,819 B ≤ 125 KB budget. **Browser keystroke (beforeinput→rAF)**: 6.8 / 32.8 / 185.2 ms p50 at 6/1k/5k blocks — decomposed @5k as input 3.1 + write 3.9 + publish 58.9 + flush 61.3 + post-dom dispatch tail 55.6 + raf 57.3 (stage medians, tail ⊂ dom→raf window). **5k bottleneck = editor layer, not the engine**: node facade is 1.03 ms while browser pays ~185 ms. Honest limits in §19: single machine/browser, synthetic fixtures, rAF≠paint, MO=first-observed-mutation, stage medians non-additive.

- 2026-09-21 — **U7 follow-up executed — the three measured 5k keystroke hot spots fixed minimally in the editor layer; browser keystroke @5k 185.2 → 29.5 ms p50 (−84%), remote apply 57.6 → 3.3 ms/update.** Scope: `src/lib/edytor.svelte.ts` + `src/lib/block/block.svelte.ts` only; zero `src/lib/crdt`/vendor/provider/bench-instrumentation changes. **(1) Placeholder sweep**: the commit subscriber's `tick().then` handler ran `removeStalePlaceholders`+`scheduleRemoveStalePlaceholders` for every text in `idToText` (~5k × ~6 scheduled `querySelectorAll` runs, amplified through the MutationObserver) — replaced by one `removeStalePlaceholdersIn(node)` + one `scheduleRemoveStalePlaceholdersIn(node)`, which provably covers the same placeholder space (every text's placeholder parent is inside `node`) while keeping the multi-tick race coverage for IME/observer races. **(2) Lazy `edytor.value`**: the getter is memoized on `facade.version` + root identity, and the commit subscriber computes the export only when `onChange`/plugin `onChange` consumers exist — zero export cost per commit otherwise. **(3) Incremental mirror reconcile**: commit-dispatch `flushMirror` applies the committed `DocChange` directly (`applyMirrorChange`) — `order` lists re-set changed parents, `added` roots adopt their projected subtree, `removed` roots drop, `meta`/`content` patch touched blocks — no `project()` (~4.7 ms), no 5,001-entry index, no full-tree diff per commit. Any unresolvable id (mirror divergence) or mid-transaction `flushMirror` (no DocChange exists — `project()` is required for same-transaction read-your-writes) falls back to the full `reconcileChildren` path. **Latent bug fixed in passing**: `Block._drop` cascaded unconditionally, killing children a commit moved out of a deleted parent (`keepChildren`); the next reconcile remounted them as fresh wrappers (lost DOM state). `_drop` now takes a `keepAlive` predicate — `isVisibleBlockId` by default, the change's `claimed` set incrementally — dropping exactly the invisible nodes. **Measured** (`bench/results/browser-latest.json`, same instrumentation/scales/machine as §19): keystroke p50 6.8→6.9 small, 32.8→6.1 @1k, 185.2→29.5 @5k (publish 58.9→0.2, flush 61.3→0.6, tail 55.6→19.1, raf 57.3→21.8); remote burst @5k 3,900→123 ms→raf; reconnect apply 59.9→4.1 ms. The ~60–70 ms U7 ceiling estimate was beaten — `flushMs` was never a 5,000-child Svelte diff, it was the full-mirror rebuild underneath it. Remaining @5k tail (~19 ms dispatch + ~22 ms raf) is DOM/frame-scheduling work, not per-commit JS. **Verification**: `pnpm test` 1,494 pass (7 pre-existing RED-by-design gateH probe failures, unchanged), `pnpm test:dom` 142/142, crdt lane 1,501 pass (same probes); new `src/tests/mirror-incremental.test.tsx` proves wrapper tree == fresh `project()` after every op class incl. remote commits + `keepChildren` wrapper-identity preservation + lazy-value memoization. Report: `docs/crdt-v14-benchmarks.md` §19 addendum.

- 2026-09-21 — **Gate-H R3 repairs complete — undo-ownership repair is now origin-agnostic, permanently armed, and publishes one merged observer frame.** `src/lib/crdt/edytor-doc.ts` + `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts` only (zero `runs.ts`/vendor/provider/harness changes). **Detection**: the `transaction.origin instanceof UndoManager` gate is replaced by a structural resurrection signature evaluated on `beforeObserverCalls` — phase 1 requires a live countable `content` atom in `insertSet` carrying `keep === true` (set only by `redoItem`'s `keepItem`; nothing else sets it before `afterTransaction`), phase 2 requires a tombstone `redone` pointer landing inside the same `insertSet` (split tombstones propagate `redone` with the offset — `Item.mergeWith`). Origin can no longer bypass detection (`ed.transact(() => um.undo())`, `doc.transact(fn, customOrigin)`, raw UndoManagers all repair); plain inserts/deletes carry no kept atoms → the planner never runs → no spurious claims; remote `applyUpdate` carries neither `keep` nor `redone`. **Copy spans**: each `redone` target names a copy's first clock; contiguous same-client kept pieces to its right (split copies) are walked and resolved to live `[i0,i1)` spans per content node; overlapping/adjacent spans merge so contiguous copies from separate `redoItem` calls can't double-claim. **Pre-delete oracle sharpened**: `undoRepairClaims` gains a `freshClaim` filter — slice records minted in the SAME transaction that are NOT resurrections are excluded from the redone-space contest (a batched `insertText` edge-rewrite can widen `e` to the live end and would otherwise outrank the real pre-delete owner — pinned: `insertText+undo` in one transaction now yields `b='Qhello '`, `tail='world'`); they still participate as the normal-resolution winner. **Permanent listener**: refcounted lease removed — `undoRepairAttached` is a module-level `WeakMap<doc,true>`; the `beforeObserverCalls` observer installs on the first `create()` and is NEVER detached (`dispose()` drops only facade listeners; the WeakMap entry + doc-held listener die with the doc) — undo after the last facade dispose still repairs (pinned). **Torn frame closed**: the claim write runs inside the committing transaction's `beforeObserverCalls` phase as a follow-up transaction appended to the in-flight cleanup sweep, and its `changed` entries are unioned into the committing transaction's `changed` map — the runs view's `observeDeep` handler recomputes post-repair runs in the SAME frame: `subscribeBlock` gets zero `b` notifications (was `['hello world','hello ']`) and `onChange` emits exactly one DocChange under the undo origin; two wire `update` events remain (resurrection + claims), receivers converge identically. Repair writes stay under `UNDO_REPAIR_ORIGIN` — untracked by UndoManager, un-undoable, non-recursive (slices items aren't content atoms). **Tests**: `r3-undo-ownership.test.ts` 12/12 (listener-retention tests rewritten for never-detach semantics + undo-after-last-dispose pin), `gateH-r3-probes.test.ts` 12/12 (all 3 previously-RED probes green: nested/custom-origin bypass, torn-frame pair — plus new pins: single-frame subscriber assertions, redo-resurrection, mixed transaction). **Verification**: crdt lane 1029 pass / 1 fail + 5 file-level fails — ALL in parallel-agent files (`random/runner.ts` mid-edit syntax error breaking 5 importers; `runs/range-reads.test.ts` `decorateRuns` marks-identity — untracked file, no undo involvement, unrelated); `svelte-check` clean on owned files (1 parallel-agent error in `runs.ts:1701`). ADR `docs/crdt-v14-undo-ownership-adr.md` updated (structural detection, permanent listener, merged-frame contract, fresh-claim caveat). Caveats: same-transaction claim writes lose to the repair on the resurrected span; per-type `'delta'` consumers see repair records as a second delta event (facade emits none — latent); a doc with no facade ever attached still gets no repair.

- 2026-09-21 — **Post-hardening browser-matrix regression fixed — `edytor.value` export was non-reactively memoized; ~40 deterministic cross-engine failures traced to a single missing reactive invalidation.** **Symptom**: serial Playwright matrix dropped to ~1089 pass / ~35 unique failures after the U9 editor-layer optimization — deterministic on all five projects, 5–15 s `expect.poll` timeouts, while CRDT/unit lanes stayed green. **Root cause**: U9 replaced the reactive `_docVersion` invalidation token with a `facade.version`-keyed memoized `value` getter (`src/lib/edytor.svelte.ts`). `facade.version` is a plain closure counter — invisible to Svelte tracking — so `edytor.value` executed inside a `$derived`/`$effect` registered no reliable invalidation source. The test page's `serializedValue = $derived(JSON.stringify(edytor?.value))` froze on its first computed export (`'a'` typed at version 8) while the model, `facade.project()`, the wrapper tree, and the DOM all stayed correct (`'ab'`) — every test asserting on the exported value timed out on state that was already right. Verified end-to-end by instrumentation: `deleteText(b,2,1)` → `T.deleteRange` deleted the correct `'2+1'` span → DocChange carried `content:'ab'` → mirror reconciled `_items:'ab'` → DOM `'ab'` — while `_valueCache` held the stale `'a'` and no recompute ever fired. **Fix**: `valueRevision = $state(0)` bumped inside the facade `onChange` dispatch (before consumers read `value`) and read by the `value` getter + included in the cache key — every commit now re-triggers tracked reads, `facade.version` remains the freshness key, and mid-transaction torn reads still cannot survive the commit bump. Same contract family as `domVersion`/`editorDomRevision`/`remotePresenceRevision`. No model/engine/provider change; `_projectedTree`'s `facade.version` keying is unaffected (its consumers are imperative command paths, never Svelte-tracked). **Verification**: focused spec 6/6; clipboard+hotkeys 40/40; multiclient+websocket+3-client 18/18 incl. IndexedDB cold reload and v99-peer refusal; **serial matrix 1211 pass / 1 fail / 12 skipped** — the single failure (`firefox input.spec.ts:1109` input-only history undo, expected `yStart:1` got `0`) is a load-order flake: passes 4/4 in isolation and 61/61 under a full-file rerun; `pnpm test` 1507/0, `test:dom` 142/142, `test:crdt:extensive` 1520/1520 (331 vendored upstream), check/lint/both typecheck lanes clean, `pnpm build` + publint + packed-consumer (node/vite build/SSR/mount/edit/teardown/tsc nodenext+tsc bundler) ALL PASS. Debug instrumentation and `tests/editor-dom/debug-regression.spec.ts`/`src/tests/crdt/debug/` removed. Caveat: the flake warrants a follow-up look if it recurs — it is a selection-restore timing read under serial memory pressure, not a deterministic defect.

# Integrated document & vendor simplification (handoff 2026-09-21)

## U0 — baseline re-established

**Source identity**: branch `feat/crdt-v14-engine` @ `d9b7de09e7110fc7a4c2fb1eec36f8267aede086` + full uncommitted hardening/U9/reactivity worktree (139 files). Vendored engine `@y/y@14.0.0-rc.26`, upstream `96c96e1fcb1ef6ce866d5264b3f97f7f77b11f64`, patches P1 (lib0-v14 specifier rewrite), P2 (test specifiers), P4 (formatting-aware marker seeding, `UPSTREAM.md`); `lib0-v14` → `lib0@1.0.0-rc.32` (npm alias).

**Current integration facts (verified in source, not assumed)**:

- `bindCrdt(Y)` (`src/lib/crdt/index.ts:195`) returns `{createDoc→raw Y.Doc, Awareness (unbound class), doc:bindEdytorDoc, providers, migration, sync}` — **no assembled document exists**.
- `Edytor` (view) per instance: own `bindCrdt(Y)`, `crdt.createDoc()` raw doc (or injected `doc` prop), `crdt.doc.create(doc,{roleOf,defaultType})` facade, `new Awareness(doc)` (or injected), `facade.createUndoManager({trackedOrigins:[transaction,null]})` deferred to end of `sync()` to keep bootstrap-before-capture.
- `bindRuns` maintains views in a per-binding `WeakMap<doc>` with refcounted leases — correct sharing **within** one binding, but every `bindCrdt` builds an independent map ⇒ N Edytor views on one doc keep N run views (the handoff's "binding-scoped" claim confirmed at binding level).
- Remote no-origin applies CAN enter the default undo stack today: `trackedOrigins:[transaction,null]` captures `null`-origin transactions, which is how remote integration arrives.
- Provider factories exist twice: `bindProviders` (`src/lib/crdt/providers/index.ts`) vs `src/lib/collaboration/providers.ts` thin sync-factory wrappers (`createIndexeddbSync`/`createWebsocketSync` — the `{sync}` prop contract).
- Native attribution APIs confirmed present in vendored engine: `IdMap`/`createContentAttribute`/`IdSet` (`utils/ids.js`), `ContentMap`/`createContentMapFromContentIds`/`mergeContentMaps`/`writeContentMap`/`readContentMap` (`utils/meta.js`), `AttributionsRenderer`/`DiffRenderer`/`SnapshotRenderer` (`utils/Renderer.js`), `createContentIds`. Edytor projection carries text/marks only — no actor/capture/durability exists.
- **U2 move discrepancy reproduces**: `block.utils.ts:240` uses `this.path.some((p,i)=>targetPath[i]===p) && this.path.length<targetPath.length` — `some` on shared prefix elements rejects sibling-nesting moves (`[0,0]→[0,1,0]` into sibling `b`) that `model.moveTo({parent:b,index:0})` legitimately allows. Predicate must test `this.path` as proper prefix of the destination **parent** path.
- `valueRevision = $state(0)` reactive export fix landed (see entry above) — serial matrix back to 1211p/1-flake.

**Baseline measurements (this tree, recorded earlier this session)**: facade keystroke p50 0.097/0.408/1.026 ms @100/1k/5k; browser keystroke @5k 29.5 ms p50 (185.2 pre-U9); remote apply 3.3 ms; packed consumer Brotli 123,819 B; `commitFast=1`, `runsRecomputed=1`; `pnpm test` 1507/0, `test:dom` 142, `test:crdt:extensive` 1520 (331 vendored upstream), serial matrix 1211/1-flake/12-skip, check/lint/typecheck×2/build/publint/packed-consumer clean.

**Package surface today**: `.` (svelte entry), `./crdt` (raw engine JS + dts), `./crdt/edytor` (bound TS surface).

## U1 — assembled document composition owner (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree as U0 (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` @ `d9b7de09` + uncommitted worktree). Implements the handoff's single composition/lifecycle owner.

**What landed** — new `src/lib/crdt/document.ts` (`EdytorDocument` + `bindDocument(Y)` + module-singleton `createDocument`/`loadDocument`/`attachDocument` bound once to the vendored engine); `src/lib/crdt/index.ts` exports the surface (reachable from `edytor` root via `export *` and from `edytor/crdt/edytor` — publint-verified emit); `Edytor` view now consumes an injected `document` or internally owns one around `{doc,awareness}` (legacy path preserved); `Edytor.svelte` gained a `document` prop; sync factories consolidated into `bindProviders` — `src/lib/collaboration/providers.ts` is a pure re-export shim.

**Public API** (`src/lib/crdt/document.ts`): `createDocument({value?, actor?, awareness?, semantics?}) → EdytorDocument`; `loadDocument(update, {actor?, awareness?, semantics?}) → EdytorDocument` — fresh replica identity, `assertSchema` **before** facade/history/awareness attach; `attachDocument(doc, opts)` — caller-owned doc (legacy `{doc}` injection). `EdytorDocument` exposes `doc`, `facade`, `history` (lazy, `DocumentNotReadyError` while pending), `awareness`, `actor`, `transaction`, `clientID`, `ready`, `readiness` (`'pending'|'local'|'hydrated'` — distinguishes local-ready from provider/restored-ready), `destroyed`, `semantics`, `adoptSemantics()`, `sync(value?)`, `trackOrigin(origin)`, `attachSync(sync,{value?})`, `encode()`, `destroy()`.

**Decisions**:

- **Readiness**: `createDocument({value})` seeds synchronously → `ready`. No value → `pending` (nothing bootstrapped — a provider may hydrate); `sync()` is the transition: `isInitialized` ⇒ `assertSchema` (hydrated wins — never re-seeded), else `init` (seed-if-empty ⇒ bootstrap block). History attaches only inside `sync()` — bootstrap-before-capture preserved.
- **Semantics are document-level**: normalized `{void,island}` role map + `defaultType` live on the document; the facade's `roleOf` resolves from it. Views contribute plugin-implied roles via `adoptSemantics` — first declaration wins, conflicting re-declaration throws `SemanticConflictError` (never silent override). Rules survive the contributing view's teardown.
- **History locality vs sharing**: `trackedOrigins` is a LIVE per-document set by identity — `{document.transaction, null}` + each view's `TRANSACTION` instance via `trackOrigin`. A shared document captures every enrolled view (one shared undo stack — the U1 contract); two independent editors on one raw doc keep undo local (the pre-existing `collaboration.test.tsx` contract — a class-level `TRANSACTION` tracking first attempt regressed this and was reverted).
- **Owned vs borrowed** (`_ownsDoc`/`_ownsAwareness`): `create`/`load` own their doc ⇒ `destroy()` releases sync cleanups → undo manager → facade/runs lease → awareness → `doc.destroy()`, exactly once (double-destroy safe). `attachDocument`/injected awareness ⇒ borrowed ⇒ never `.destroy()`ed by the document (borrowing grants no destruction authority). View `destroy()` releases only view-lifetime listeners/mirror; `document.destroy()` only when `ownsDocument` (internally-created path). Bonus fix: the view-created awareness is now actually destroyed (old code left its interval alive).
- **Actor (U6 stub seam)**: supplied `{id,name?,color?}` or generated opaque `anon-<uuid>`; published into awareness (`actor` field + `user` profile).
- **Run-view sharing**: all documents share one `bindEdytorDoc` binding ⇒ one `bindRuns` WeakMap ⇒ one maintained run view per raw doc, refcounted across facades.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0/0; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm test -- --run` **1537 pass**/1 skip/11 todo (was 1507 — +29 new U1 tests +1 restored collaboration contract); `pnpm test:dom` **142 pass**; `pnpm package` + publint clean, emitted `dist/crdt/index.d.ts` re-exports the full surface. New tests `src/tests/crdt/document/{document,document-views}.test.ts` (29): headless create/edit/history/encode/load round-trip, fresh-replica restore, schema-gate-before-attach, pending/no-premature-seed, hydration-wins-over-seed, 1/2/10-view sharing (facade/awareness/history identity), cross-view mirror + undo, sibling-preserving teardown, semantics-survive-teardown, conflict rejection, borrowed-doc/awareness survival, exactly-once + double destroy, sync-prop deferral.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: remote no-origin applies still enter undo via `null` tracking (pre-existing; U4 hardening scope); `attachSync` is the headless provider seam — the mounted `Edytor.svelte` still drives `sync` prop cleanup itself (unchanged); `document` prop is constructor-time (not reactive — same as `doc`/`value` before it); awareness `user` profile field shape mirrors the legacy presence contract and may be revisited by U5/U6; `Edytor.crdt` kept as a lazy compat accessor (no eager per-view binding).

## U2 — view adapters delegate structural permission & persisted marks to the document (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree as U1 (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` @ `d9b7de09` + uncommitted worktree incl. concurrent U-stream edits). Implements the handoff's "views are adapters" collapse: repeated live command-policy/content-preparation implementations removed from `block.utils.ts`/`text.utils.ts`; the document/facade (`block.model` → `DocBlock` → `edytor-doc.ts`) is the single authority for structural permission and persisted mark mutation.

**What landed**:

- **Sibling-nest bug fixed by deletion, not repair** (the U0 finding): `moveBlock` previously ran `this.path.some((p,i) => targetPath[i]===p) && this.path.length<targetPath.length` — a shared-ELEMENT test that rejected any destination sharing a path element, so the legal move `[0,0]→[0,1,0]` (nest source inside its next sibling) returned `null`. The view-side ancestry/island/void policy block was removed wholesale; `moveBlock` now resolves the caller path to `{parent, index}` (adapter duty) and `model.moveTo` returns `false` for every refusal — placement's `isSelfOrDescendant(placements, own, dest.parent, id)` is the sole ancestry check. Pinned BEFORE the fix: two `hierarchy.fixtures.tsx` fixtures (`[0,0]→[0,1,0]` and a target with existing children `[0,0]→[0,1,1]`) failed with `expected null to be an instance of Block`; post-fix they pass with result paths `[0,0,0]`/`[0,0,1]` (destination indexes shift after the source leaves its slot — expected-path semantics, asserted explicitly).
- **Per-command split — kept vs delegated**: `moveBlock` keeps path shape validation + parent resolution + post-op `flushMirror`/`normalizeChildren`, delegates permission to `model.moveTo` (island-sealed sources, void/island/inside-island destinations, own-subtree). `nestBlock` keeps previous-sibling resolution, delegates to `model.nestUnder`. `unNestBlock` delegates to `model.unNest` (keeps grandParent normalize). `splitBlock` keeps `partOffsetOf` segment-offset math and the baseline sibling type/data reset (`defaultType`, `{}`), delegates the split to `model.split(offset,newId)`. `mergeBlockBackward/Forward` delegate to `model.mergeBackward/Forward` (facade owns void + island-boundary rules via `mergeUnnesting`), keep mirror flush + `idToBlock` handle re-resolution. `removeBlock` delegates to `model.delete({keepChildren})`. `setBlock` delegates to `model.set({type?,data?,content?,children?})`. `pushContentIntoBlock`/`addInlineBlock`/`deleteContentAtRange`/`removeInlineBlock`/`deleteContentWithinSelection` (edytor-level) all write through `model.insertText/insertInline/deleteText/deleteParts/insertParts/insertChildren` — content edits, no structural permission to duplicate.
- **Spec preparation without disposable trees**: canonical `jsonContentToItems`/`jsonBlockToSpec` now live in `src/lib/utils/json.ts` (re-exported from `block.svelte.ts` for the historical import path). `setBlock` children go JSON→`BlockSpec` directly — the old path built `new Block({block:child})._toSpec()` per child (detached wrappers constructed solely to serialize back, also registering/leaking into `edytor.idToBlock`). `_toSpec` survives ONLY where the wrapper is the live carrier adopted post-insert (`insertChildren` → `_pendingBlocks`), which is the designed use. Semantics preserved: ids kept/`id('b'|'i')` minted, `freshIds` forces fresh identity, `data`/`marks` `cloneJson`'d, recursive children, `data` replacement (not merge) semantics unchanged.
- **Persisted marks delegated**: `removeMarksFromText` on a bound text calls `parent.model.clearMarks(segStart+start, end-start)` — the facade discovers range mark names from the maintained runs view and unsets via `formatRange`; detached spec buffers keep the local collect+`formatAt` path (documented). `markText`/`formatAt`/`insertAt`/`deleteAt`/`setText`/`splitText`/`insertText` all route bound writes through `parent.model.*` with `segStart` mapping segment-local → block display offsets; detached wrappers splice `_items`. Reads (`getMarksAtRange`, toggle derivation, `markOnNextInsert`) stay view-side — they decide WHAT to write.
- **Normalization unification decision**: the live-content invariant (text-first/text-last, no adjacent same-kind parts) is document-derived — `deriveContentParts` over `contentItemsOf` projects stored items into normalized parts, so `jsonContentToItems` stores caller items verbatim (the old `_toSpec` path had pre-injected `groupContent` `''` separators — projection of either store yields identical parts). `groupContent` remains only for DETACHED spec mirrors (`new Block({block})` content, `suggestText` staging); `normalizeContent` is now the plugin hook only; `normalizeChildren` keeps the root-empty bootstrap + plugin hook. Documented in the `block.utils.ts` header.
- **Adapter contract preserved**: `batch()` still runs `onBeforeOperation` (first-wins `prevent`), `edytor.transact`, `onAfterOperation`; readonly gate unchanged; selection defaults (`isCollapsed`, `yStart`/`yEnd` fallbacks) and DOM repair/selection restores stay view-side; `flushMirror` after every committed mutation.
