- **Retained view-side checks — all non-permission**: `beforeInputDeleteCommands` void/island branches choose input SEMANTICS (delete-into-void ⇒ select the void block; islandRoot backspace ⇒ select/no-op) — the facade merge would refuse anyway, the view picks the user-visible alternative; `hotkeys`/`selection.svelte` island/void reads are caret navigation (facade owns no selection policy); `plugins/blockHandles` `canMoveBlockTo` is a drag-hover affordance predicate (the facade is apply-only — no dry-run surface — so the plugin gates its own drop UI; the real move still delegates and re-validates); `richTextOperations.canConvertBlock`/`markdownShortcuts`/`slashMenu` gate plugin UI features, not document permission. `block.utils.ts` header documents the contract: facade `false`/`null` is the sole permission signal.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0/0; `pnpm test:typecheck` 0/0; `pnpm test:dom:typecheck` 0/0; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm test -- --run` **1557 pass**/1 skip/11 todo (88 files — U1 baseline 1537; +2 sibling-nest fixtures + concurrent-stream additions); `pnpm test:dom` **142 pass** (3 files); focused `transforms.test.ts` 108/108 with the two pins green. New tests: 2 model-transform fixtures in `hierarchy.fixtures.tsx` (shared-prefix sibling nest, nest into sibling-with-children).

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: `document.ts` carries a private `jsonBlockToSpec` duplicate (U1 seeding path) — semantically identical (id preserve/mint, cloneJson payloads, recursive children; no `freshIds` arm) but the file belongs to the document workstream, so convergence on the `utils/json.ts` helper is a noted follow-up, not divergence. `canMoveBlockTo` keeps island/void knowledge view-side as a hover predicate — acceptable duplication for UX affordance, enforced regardless by `moveTo`/`moveBlocks`. `splitBlock`'s sibling type/data reset is baseline semantics (engine copies type+data on split; baseline produced a default-type sibling) — adapter policy, documented. Prohibited files untouched by this unit (document/selection/edytor/crdt nodes+placement+text models show only concurrent-workstream diffs).

## U3 — shared physical-sequence reads: vendored `RangeCursor` + `readItemPieces` (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree as U2 (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` @ `d9b7de09` + uncommitted worktree incl. concurrent U-stream edits). Implements the handoff's "consolidate physical text reading into the vendored engine" unit: a native, read-only, bounded range cursor plus the single item-piece traversal both it and `toDelta` consume. Vendor patch record: `src/lib/crdt/vendor/yjs/UPSTREAM.md` **P5**.

**What landed**:

- **`readItemPieces(out, renderer, item, scratch?)`** (`vendor/yjs/src/utils/renderer-helpers.js`) — the `AttributedContent` pieces one item contributes to a _current-state_ render: renderer-claimed → `readContent` mode `1`; `ContentFormat` → one marker piece; tombstoned → nothing; live → the item whole. This is now the ONLY physical-sequence interpretation: `YNode#toDelta`'s current-state walk (`itemsToRender === null && !retainInserts`) iterates it through the existing `processContent` state machine (op-identical — change renders and `retainInserts` overlays keep their original paths), and `RangeCursor` consumes the same pieces for bounded reads. `scratch` is a caller-owned `AttributedContent` reused for the trivial piece — both consumers drain `out` before the next item, so the hot path stays allocation-free (added after the first benchmark showed per-item wrapper allocation cost ~47 % on `toDelta`; post-fix probe p50 0.314 ms on the 100 k formatted fixture).
- **`RangeCursor`** (`vendor/yjs/src/utils/RangeCursor.js`, exported from `index.js`) — forward-only, read-only cursor over one `YNode` list. `read(i0, i1, stats?)` seeds from `_searchMarker` checkpoints (the P4 format-aware markers double as read checkpoints), folds `ContentFormat` exactly like `ItemTextListPosition#forward`, and emits `RangePiece`s — borrowed content, first-element `id`, rendered `index`/`offset`/`len`, effective `deleted`, native `attrs`, shared folded `formats`. Cold walks plant sparse format-aware markers every `READ_PLANT_GAP = 64` items (`renderer === null` only; `plantSearchMarker` exported from `ynode.js`). Reads never open a transaction — no updates, splits, undo or renderer changes; the only writes are `_searchMarker` maintenance, non-replicated and behavior-invisible.
- **Read seed deliberately weaker than the mutation seed**: a read may resume on any linked item at-or-left of the target (the forward fold reproduces format state); `applyDelta` still requires the _first_ item at the index. Pinned counterexample `a[b]b[b=null]c` — a tail marker anchored on `c` seeds reads but is rejected for mutations.
- **Edytor projection consolidated** (`src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts`, `src/lib/crdt/text/runs.ts`): `readRange` converts native pieces into `RangeItem`s (UTF-16 slices for text, `{kind:'inline',id,type,data}` projection for atoms, adjacent text runs merged under canonically equal marks); `contentItemsOf` keeps logical ownership/slices while using one cursor per backing text; `runs.ts` keeps ownership, slice selection, invalidation, caching, interning, freeze/publication — now instrumented via `itemsWalked`/`markersWalked` and seeded by the native marker pool. The Edytor-side checkpoint index is **deleted** (`readCheckpoints`, `READ_CP_ITEMS`, `readIndexBuilds`, read-side `dirtyContentTexts` bookkeeping) — duplicated state the native pool now owns.
- **Tests**: new `src/tests/crdt/range-cursor.test.ts` (19): bounded output vs whole-node `toDelta` over every range, inherited marks + null clears, surrogate-boundary clipping, inline atoms, tombstoned content/markers, `AttributionsRenderer` traversal, remote `applyUpdate`, undo/redo, open-transaction reads, forward/backward/repeated reuse, purity (no updates/splits/undo/renderer mutation; planted markers invisible to mutations — byte-identical stores), traversal bounds, read-vs-mutation-seed counterexample. `runs/range-reads.test.ts` (18) + `gateF2/wu8-range-reads.test.ts` (5) rewritten from removed Edytor APIs onto `_searchMarker` inspection, marker pinning (`index` = anchor left edge, `formats` = fold at edge, private-copy adoption), and work bounds.
- **Facade seam (documented)**: `text/model.ts` keeps `openRangeCursor`/`itemsOfRange`/`readRange`/`protectItems` as the Edytor-side bridge — the vendored engine owns the physical sequence; Edytor keeps ownership, slices, inline projection, interning, and immutable publication.

**Measured** (`bench:crdt` range-reads workload — 50 sibling blocks each reading ~2 k of one shared 100 k formatted text; new `bench/results/2026-09-21T21-17-34-712Z.json` vs WU8 baseline `2026-09-21T14-58-49-261Z.json`):

| metric                            | U3                                                                 | WU8 baseline             |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------ |
| itemsWalkedPerRead (mean / p95)   | 48.96 / 66                                                         | 62.0 / 92                |
| markersWalkedPerRead (mean / p95) | 31.63 / 43                                                         | 40.69 / 61               |
| firstReadMs p50                   | 0.389                                                              | 0.437                    |
| warmReadMs p50                    | 0.0119                                                             | 0.0102                   |
| preWU8ToDeltaMs p50               | 0.461 (10 samples, bimodal — GC; isolated 50-iter probe p50 0.314) | 0.356                    |
| searchMarkers planted             | ≈ 33 per fixture (330 total)                                       | n/a (metric added in U3) |

`bench:runs` (run-view lane) unchanged in shape: warm `runs()` p50 0.0009 ms/block, unrelated-edit reuse 999/1000, remote apply+recompute 0.079 ms, delta-cache read p50 0.0006 vs fresh `toDelta` 0.0036.

**Checks run**: `scripts/regen-crdt-vendor-types.sh` — 34 dts emitted (expected upstream `TS2589` in `delta-helpers.js` only); `pnpm test -- --run` **1569 pass**/1 skip/11 todo (89 files); `pnpm test:crdt` **1562 pass**/7 skip (74 files); `pnpm test:crdt:extensive` **1568 pass**/1 skip (74 files incl. 331 vendored upstream + 30 k-op array stress, ~155 s); `pnpm check` 0/0; `pnpm test:typecheck` 0/0; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm bench:crdt` + `pnpm bench:runs` completed.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**:

- `_searchMarker` is local mutable cache state — reads are pure w.r.t. replicated state but DO write the checkpoint pool (same adaptive writes upstream's own `findMarker` read-path performs); stores stay byte-identical with the pool disabled (pinned).
- `RangePiece.content`/`attrs` are BORROWED engine objects — consumers must treat them read-only (Edytor's publication boundary interns/freezes; the `formats` object is shared per fold state for the same reason).
- The `toDelta` current-state consolidation costs a small per-item overhead vs the removed inlined dispatch (piece plumbing through `readItemPieces`+`processContent`; ~0-15 % on the 10-sample bench, ≈ parity in isolated probes) — accepted as the price of the single interpretation; documented in UPSTREAM.md P5.
- Read-seed and mutation-seed eligibility intentionally differ (weaker vs first-at-index) — the counterexample test is the guard against conflating them.
- Expected upstream `TS2589` in `delta-helpers.js` remains at dts emit time (declarations still emit complete).
- `pnpm lint` initially flagged two files owned by the concurrent document/selection workstream (`src/lib/selection/selection.svelte.ts`, `src/tests/crdt/document/history.test.ts`) — reformatted with `prettier --write`, no semantic edits; the owning stream should review.

## U4 — document-owned local history: selective capture, remote exclusion, per-view anchors (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree as U1–U3 (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` @ `d9b7de09` + uncommitted worktree incl. concurrent U-stream edits). Implements the handoff's history unit: the document's single `Y.UndoManager` made a first-class owned contract — selective local ownership, provable remote exclusion, bootstrap/hydration/system-bookkeeping exclusion, shared anchor machinery with independent per-view selection state, and an explicit retention surface.

**What landed**:

- **Remote exclusion is now transaction-locality, not origin hygiene** (`src/lib/crdt/document.ts` `_attachHistory`): the manager's `captureTransaction` is `transaction.local !== false`. The engine marks every update-integration non-local — `applyUpdate` runs `transact(..., local=false)` AND forces `transaction.local = false` on an in-flight transaction (`vendor/yjs/src/utils/encoding.js:249`), so a remote apply nested inside a local `doc.transact` marks the whole commit non-local. No provider path or raw `Y.applyUpdate(doc, u)` — stamped or originless — can enter the local undo stack, and the guarantee cannot be bypassed by a provider forgetting to stamp an origin. This closes the ADR hole (`docs/crdt-v14-undo-ownership-adr.md` §241): `null` remains in `trackedOrigins` (it is the untyped-LOCAL marker — `document.facade.<op>` outside `document.transact`, raw `doc.transact(fn)`), which previously admitted originless remote applies; now `local` decides remote-ness and `trackedOrigins` decides local ownership — orthogonal gates, both required.
- **Origin contract documented** (module header + `protocols/sync.ts` header): tracked by identity = `{document.transaction, null, each enrolled view TRANSACTION}` (live set — the manager also adds itself for undo/redo capture). Providers keep stamping their instance (required for `doc.on('update')` echo suppression regardless); `bindSync` gains `remoteApplyOrigin` — a per-binding `Symbol('edytor-remote-apply')` stamped by `readSyncStep2`/`readUpdate`/`applyUpdateStaged` whenever the caller's `transactionOrigin` is `null`/`undefined`, so inbound applies on the live doc ALWAYS carry a non-null, non-tracked origin (scratch-doc staging applies deliberately stay originless — no consumers). IndexedDB hydration already wraps applies in `Y.transact(doc, …, idbPersistence, local=false)` — provider origin + non-local, doubly excluded. Awareness writes never touch the doc — out of scope of capture entirely.
- **Bookkeeping exclusion**: untracked origins (the undo-ownership repair's `UNDO_REPAIR_ORIGIN`; the same hook is the documented U6 attribution/metadata seam) fail `trackedOrigins`; non-local commits fail `captureTransaction` — either channel suffices, both are free of the undo stack. The R3 repair itself is untouched (origin-agnostic `beforeObserverCalls` detection + permanent `WeakMap<doc>` listener — `r3-undo-ownership` 12/12, `gateH-r3-probes` 12/12 still green).
- **Retention is explicit**: stacks stay unbounded (the engine prunes nothing; `captureTimeout` only fuses adjacent commits — default 500 ms, now tunable per document via `createDocument({history: {captureTimeout}})` → `DocumentOptions.history`, threaded through `create`/`load`/`attach`). New `document.clearHistory()` prunes undo+redo — no-op while history is unattached (never forces attach), destroyed-guarded like the rest of the surface. `destroy()` releases the manager exactly once (idempotent).
- **Per-view selection snapshots on shared stack items** (`src/lib/selection/selection.svelte.ts`, `src/lib/history/historySelectionSnapshot.ts`): `stackItem.meta['cursor-location']`/`['restore-cursor-location']` now hold `Map<view transaction origin, UndoSelectionSnapshot>` instead of a single shared snapshot — every live view writes and restores only its own entry, so on a shared document undo restores EACH view's own caret (previously every view raced one slot: first writer won, all views restored it). `getHistorySelectionSnapshot(edytor, stackItem, opts)` resolves the caller view's entry; call sites updated (`hotkeys.ts` ×2, `onInput.ts`, `onBeforeInput.ts` ×2, `historySelectionSnapshot.test.tsx`).
- **Shared-manager listener lifecycle fixed** (same file): `init()` registered `stack-item-*` handlers on the document-owned manager per attach with no removal — every remount duplicated them and dead views kept writing/restoring snapshots on the shared stack. Handlers are now captured and released via `_unbindHistoryListeners()` — called by `selection.destroy()` (which `Edytor.destroy()` and the attach teardown already run) and re-entered safely on re-`init()`.

**New tests** — `src/tests/crdt/document/history.test.ts` (12): originless raw `applyUpdate` never captures + local undo spares remote text; remote apply nested inside a local transact still excluded; `readSyncMessage` inbound applies (provider-stamped AND unstamped) never capture; `remoteApplyOrigin` default stamping observed on `doc.on('update')`; `loadDocument`/`attachDocument`+hydration never become undo steps; untracked-origin vs `null`/`document` origins; `captureTimeout: 0` vs default merge window; `clearHistory()` (stacks emptied, capture continues, no-op while pending); exactly-once destroy; per-view snapshot maps keyed by view origin, undo restoring each view's own caret from one shared stack item, destroyed view stops writing.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0/0; `pnpm test:typecheck` 0/0; `pnpm test -- --run` **1569 pass**/1 skip/11 todo (89 files); `pnpm test:crdt` **1574 pass**/7 skip (75 files); `pnpm test:dom` **142 pass** (3 files); `pnpm exec eslint` clean on all touched files. Required regression lanes green: `r3-undo-ownership` 12/12, `gateH-r3-probes` 12/12, `selective-undo` 7/7, `document`+`document-views` 29/29, model `collaboration` 3/3 + DOM `collaboration` 7/7, `historySelectionSnapshot` 3/3.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: none blocking. `pnpm lint`'s prettier pass flags this ledger file's pre-existing formatting drift (the file accumulates long entries — reformatted, cosmetic only). Selection restore on undo remains the pre-existing two-path design (hotkey/onInput `restoreCollapsedHistorySelection` + the popped handler's own restore); U4 made it per-view-correct but did not redesign the dual-restore flow — a future consolidation candidate. Provider-level end-to-end remote-exclusion evidence rides on `transaction.local` (pinned at protocol + raw-apply level); a live websocket-provider undo test remains a possible browser-lane addition, not a gap in the contract.

## U4b — adversarial follow-up: attach dedupe, lifecycle hygiene, pop-restore policy (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` worktree incl. concurrent U2/U5 edits). Adversarial review/fix pass on the U1 document layer + U4 history seams. Owned files only: `src/lib/crdt/document.ts`, `src/lib/edytor.svelte.ts`, `src/lib/selection/selection.svelte.ts`, plus call-site conversions in `hotkeys.ts`/`onBeforeInput.ts`/`onInput.ts` and tests. `protocols/awareness.ts`, `components/`, `collaboration/` untouched (U5-owned); `utils/json.ts` read-only imported (U2's canonical helpers).

**Per-finding resolutions**:

- **F1 — pop-restore policy decided: issuing-view-only.** Verified the hole: the shared manager's `stack-item-popped` fires on EVERY view's listener, so a sibling could restore a snapshot recorded when the undone edit committed — regressing a caret that moved since. Policy: undo/redo through a view is a VIEW command — only the issuing view restores its caret; every other view treats the pop as an ordinary document change (the same semantics a REMOTE peer's undo has — remote undos never restore local carets either). Implemented via `edytor.historyUndo()`/`historyRedo()` → `runHistoryCommand` sets `selection.expectHistoryRestore` for the synchronous pop window; the popped handler returns early without it. Call sites converted: `hotkeys.ts` mod+z/mod+shift+z, `onBeforeInput.ts` historyUndo/historyRedo, `onInput.ts` fallback. Headless `document.history.undo()` restores NO view caret — pinned. Documented in `selection.svelte.ts` (field doc + "POP POLICY" block). Regression: `history.test.ts` — v2 caret stays put through v1's undo (not regressed to its recorded snapshot); new "headless undo restores no view" pin.
- **F2 — `attachDocument` deduplicates by raw doc.** `bindDocument` now holds `WeakMap<YDoc, EdytorDocument>`; every factory registers (`createDocument`/`loadDocument`/`attachDocument`), and reattaching a live raw doc returns the SAME document after a compatibility check (`assertAttachCompatible`): a different `awareness` instance, `actor.id`, `history.captureTimeout`, or conflicting `semantics` raises `SemanticConflictError` — compatible semantics merge additively through `adoptSemantics`. Each attach retains one reference (`_retain`/`_refs`); `destroy()` releases one and the teardown runs only when the last release lands — dedupe + refcount fixes the real bug that two `{doc}` views on one raw doc built TWO facades/histories/awareness compositions over it, and that the first view's destroy tore the shared document under its sibling. **Deliberate contract change**: two `Edytor`s on one raw doc now share the document AND its undo stack — undo locality is per-document, not per-view. `createDocument`/`loadDocument` still always allocate fresh raw docs. Tests updated for the new contract: model `collaboration.test.tsx` ("one document — and one shared history" + a separate "undo local between DIFFERENT raw docs" pin), `gate3/app-context` listener counts (one facade's doc listeners across 5 views), `mirror-incremental` unchanged (shared facade dispatches to both mirrors).
- **F5 — `untrackOrigin(origin)`** on `EdytorDocument`; `Edytor.destroy()` releases the view's `TRANSACTION` (borrowed-document path included). Safe because the manager consults the tracked set live at commit: already-captured stack items stay undoable, only future commits from the dead origin stop capturing. Silent no-op on a destroyed document (teardown ordering never throws). Pinned via `document.history.trackedOrigins.size` before/after view destroy + a fresh commit under the dead origin not capturing while the view's prior edit still undoes.
- **F7 — constructor-failure cleanup in `Edytor`.** Everything after document acquisition (plugins → snippets → `adoptSemantics` → `trackOrigin` → awareness listeners → `sync()` → selection/hotKeys) sits in one try; catch releases the awareness listeners (off() is a no-op when never bound), `untrackOrigin`, and — only for `ownsDocument` — `document.destroy()`; injected/borrowed documents are never destroyed by a failed view. `trackOrigin` moved AFTER `adoptSemantics` so a semantic conflict strands no origin. Covered by throwing-plugin (injected survives + no stale origin; owned document fully released — verified through the dedupe map) and conflict-strands-no-origin pins.
- **F8 — `bindCrdt(Y)` hoisted to a module-level `sharedCrdt`** in `edytor.svelte.ts`; `get crdt()` returns it (the per-view lazy binding is gone). Doc comment pins the contract: `crdt.doc.create` on a doc already wrapped by an `EdytorDocument` forks a second maintained run view — forbidden; `create` on an unwrapped doc (peer replicas in tests) stays legitimate. Side benefit: kills the U4-hardening noted leak where each `Edytor` built a fresh binding (`undoRepairDocs` WeakSet per binding → stacked `repairUndoOwnership` listeners) — the gate3 listener-retention probe now returns to baseline exactly.
- **F9 smalls**: (a) `history` getter guards external `manager.destroy()` — `_attachHistory` wraps the manager's own `destroy` to set `_historyDead`; the getter reattaches a fresh manager instead of returning the corpse (pinned: `dead.destroy()` → `document.history` is a different, still-capturing instance). (b) `attachSync` cleanup self-unregisters — early caller teardown can't be re-run by `document.destroy()`; a factory throwing mid-build registers nothing (pinned both). (c) `adoptSemantics` is now two-pass — validate every incoming role + `defaultType` first, apply only when all proved compatible; a late conflict leaves zero partial adoption (pinned: valid role preceding a conflicting one is not adopted). (d) new `DocumentDestroyedError` (terminal-state sibling of `DocumentNotReadyError`) used across `history`/`sync`/`adoptSemantics`/`trackOrigin`/`clearHistory`/`attachSync` — typed + pinned; note `crdt/index.ts` was out of scope so the class is not re-exported there (tests import `crdt/document.js` directly). (e) the private `jsonBlockToSpec` in `document.ts` is deleted — `sync()` delegates to U2's canonical `utils/json.ts` helper (call-site wraps the map callback so `freshIds` stays false — `children.map(jsonBlockToSpec)` bare would have fed the index as `freshIds`). (f) legacy ownership verified: `git show HEAD:edytor.svelte.ts` — the pre-U1 view NEVER `doc.destroy()`ed its raw doc (owned or injected), so `attachDocument`'s `ownsDoc:false` on the `{doc}` path is already the faithful semantic — no change needed; documented. (g) `DocumentOptions.awareness` comment now documents the borrowed-awareness `setLocalStateField` side effect (the LAST attached document's actor wins). (h) **Awareness double-destroy investigated**: real — owned `document.destroy()` calls `awareness.destroy()`, then `doc.destroy()` re-fires it via the awareness's `doc.on('destroy')` hook. Pre-fix the second invocation was delivery-wise a no-op (`ObservableV2.destroy()` clears `_observers` before the re-entrant emit) but still re-ran `setLocalState(null)`/`clearInterval`; U5's concurrent `_destroyed` guard in `protocols/awareness.ts` now makes the re-entrant call return early — verified `destroy`/`update` each emit exactly once through `document.destroy()`. Flagged to U5 as resolved-by-them; no edit made here.
- **New/updated tests** — `document-views.test.ts` +9 (dedupe identity/refs, divergent-attach conflicts incl. awareness/actor/history/semantics, additive semantic merge, createDocument registration, two `{doc}` views sharing + ref-wise release, origin untracking lifecycle, three constructor-failure pins), `document.test.ts` +7 (atomic adoption, self-unregistering + factory-throw attachSync, dead-manager reattach, `DocumentDestroyedError` gating every public service), `history.test.ts` +1 (headless undo restores no caret) + pop-policy rewrite of the multi-view test, `collaboration.test.tsx` rewritten for shared-document history + a different-raw-doc undo-locality control, `app-context.test.tsx` updated for single-document listener accounting.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0/0; `pnpm lint` clean (prettier + eslint); `pnpm test -- --run` **1603 pass** / 1 skip / 11 todo (90 files); `pnpm test:dom` **146 pass** (4 files). Focused reruns: `document.test.ts` 21/21, `document-views.test.ts` 22/22, `history.test.ts` 13/13, `presence.test.ts` 18/18, `app-context` 7/7, `collaboration` 4/4, `mirror-incremental` 4/4, `historySelectionSnapshot` 3/3.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: `DocumentDestroyedError` is exported from `crdt/document.ts` but not re-exported by `crdt/index.ts` (that file was outside this unit's allowed set — a one-line export addition for whoever owns it). `edytor.undoManager` still caches the manager instance captured at `sync()` — an externally destroyed+reattached history manager leaves that cached reference stale (the document getter itself is guarded; the view field is documented legacy surface). The two-restore path note from U4 stands — `historyUndo`/`historyRedo` now gate the popped-handler path per view; the explicit `restoreCollapsedHistorySelection*` flow is unchanged.

**Source identity**: same tree as U1–U4 (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` @ `d9b7de09` + uncommitted worktree incl. concurrent U-stream edits). Implements the handoff's presence unit: one `Awareness` per `EdytorDocument` shared by every view, sibling-safe per-view selection publication, document-lifetime provider attachment for injected documents, and idempotent awareness teardown. Forbidden files untouched (`edytor.svelte.ts`, `selection/**`, `document.ts`, `crdt/index.ts`, block/text/events/history/hotkeys, `protocols/sync.ts`) — the unit is implemented entirely in the collaboration layer + `Edytor.svelte` + `protocols/awareness.ts` around the existing seams.

**Presence design decision — per-view keying, not a designated publisher.** A designated publisher (one view owns the `selection` field) was rejected: it forces an election/handoff protocol on view teardown (publisher dies → sibling must adopt the slot → a publish gap + ordering hazards across remounts), and it still cannot express two live sibling carets. Instead the local awareness state carries `selections: Record<viewId, EdytorAwarenessSelection & {t}>` — each view owns exactly one key for its lifetime — plus a `selection` mirror of the freshest entry (max `t`, a client-local publish sequence) so pre-U5 peers/readers keep working and every remote client still renders exactly one caret ("where is this actor now"). `actor`/`user` stay document-level fields published once at document construction.

**What landed**:

- **F4 — per-view keyed presence** (`src/lib/collaboration/awarenessSelection.ts`): `viewPresenceIds` (WeakMap<Edytor,string>) mints a stable per-view key; `presenceOwners` (WeakMap<awareness, Map<viewId,Edytor>>) records which Edytor owns each key. `publishAwarenessSelection` writes only the caller's key (`t: ++publishSeq`), early-returns on `edytor.destroyed`, and sweeps keys whose owner Edytor is dead on every write. `clearAwarenessSelection` (called from `selection.destroy()` — signature unchanged, forbidden file untouched) resolves the tearing-down view through the owner registry + the `Edytor.destroy()` ordering contract (`destroyed = true` before `selection.destroy()` runs, `edytor.svelte.ts:1569→1582`): only dead-view keys drop; a `{#key}`-remount selection-layer teardown on a LIVE view deliberately keeps its presence.
- **F4 — remote rendering** (`remoteSelection.ts` `getSelection`): reads `selections` max-`t` entry first, falls back to the legacy `selection` field — both wire shapes accepted, one caret per remote client preserved.
- **F3 — document-lifetime sync** (`src/lib/collaboration/documentSync.ts`, new): `attachDocumentSync(document, sync, value)` dedupes by factory identity (`WeakMap<EdytorDocument,Set>` — the same `sync` prop shared by sibling mounts attaches ONE provider; distinct factories still compose), wraps `synced` to run `document.sync(value)` then wake waiting views, and delegates cleanup to `document.attachSync` — released by `document.destroy()`, never by a view unmount. `whenDocumentReady(document, notify)` waits out `pending` documents: provider `synced` (synchronous), doc `update` (local-seed readiness), and a 50 ms `unref`'d poll backstop (externally-driven hydrated `document.sync()` writes nothing → no event). `Edytor.svelte` routes on `edytor.ownsDocument`: view-owned keeps component-lifetime `sync()` + destroy-on-unmount; injected attaches to the document and the view syncs only after readiness (never seeds a pending doc early).
- **F6 — idempotent `Awareness.destroy()`** (`src/lib/crdt/protocols/awareness.ts`): `_destroyed` guard makes the double-invocation path (`document.destroy()` → `awareness.destroy()` + the `doc.on('destroy')` hook) a no-op — 'destroy' emits once, `setLocalState(null)`/removed-broadcast run once, the stale-check interval clears once. Interval also `unref`'d so SSR/headless awareness can't pin a node process.
- **Awareness stays outside history**: presence writes touch `states`/`meta` maps only — zero doc updates, zero undo entries (pinned by test); U6 attribution seam is the `actor` field + `untrackOrigin`/untracked-origin bookkeeping from U4.

**New tests** — `src/tests/crdt/document/presence.test.ts` (18): shared awareness identity + actor/user published once; sibling views publish distinct `selections` entries (no clobber); teardown of view A preserves view B's caret (mirror recomputes to the survivor; last-view teardown drops presence fields while `actor` survives); dead-owner sweep on sibling publish; null-selection clears only the publisher's key; presence writes → 0 doc updates + empty undo stack; `Awareness.destroy()` exactly-once via `document.destroy()` AND via `doc.destroy()` hook; remote awareness merge carrying `selections`; `attachDocumentSync` dedupe / distinct-factory composition / destroyed-document no-op / `document.destroy()` releasing the provider; `whenDocumentReady` already-ready, local-seed, hydrated-no-event poll, and release paths; one attached provider waking two waiting views. `src/tests/fixtures/dom/collaboration/document-sync.test.tsx` (4): `<Edytor {document} {sync}>` attaches provider to document lifetime — unmounting the carrier doesn't kill it, two views sharing one factory attach once and both sync on `synced`, `document.destroy()` cleans up after every view is gone, view-owned keeps component teardown. `collaboration.test.tsx` teardown probe rewritten for the live-vs-dead view distinction; `EdytorHarness`/`test.utils` gained a `document` prop passthrough.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0/0; `pnpm test:typecheck` 0/0; `pnpm test:dom:typecheck` 0/0; `pnpm lint` clean (prettier + eslint); `pnpm test -- --run` **1601 pass** / 1 skip / 11 todo (92 files — 2 transient failures observed mid-run were concurrent U4b-workstream edits in flight; both files re-run green: `document-views` 22/22, `app-context` 7/7); `pnpm test:dom` **146 pass** (4 files — was 142 pre-U5, +4 new document-sync tests). Presence lanes: unit `presence.test.ts` 18/18; DOM `collaboration.test.tsx` 7/7 (remote caret render, anchor shift on local edit, live-view teardown keeps presence, dead-view teardown clears). Browser presence coverage unchanged and green per repo guidance — chromium `collaboration.spec.ts` (remote selection/caret), `collaboration-multiclient.spec.ts` (awareness + remote caret), `collaboration-websocket.spec.ts` (awareness + caret over socket), `collaboration-websocket-3client.spec.ts`; collaboration specs also run firefox/webkit — full matrix not run per instruction.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: (1) Dedupe is factory-IDENTITY keyed — `sync={createIndexeddbSync('room')}` evaluated inline per view produces distinct factories → two providers on one doc (documented in module header; callers must hoist the factory). (2) `whenDocumentReady`'s 50 ms poll exists solely for externally-driven hydrated `document.sync()` — a `readiness`-changed event on `EdytorDocument` (seam request to the document owner) would remove it. (3) The dead-view sweep depends on `Edytor.destroy()` setting `destroyed` before `selection.destroy()` — currently guaranteed at `edytor.svelte.ts:1569→1582`; a view destroyed through a path that skips that ordering could leave a stale caret until the next sibling publish (self-heals on any publish; sweep also runs inside `publishAwarenessSelection`). (4) `selection` mirror makes the freshest view's caret canonical for remote peers — correct for "one caret per actor"; peers wanting per-remote-VIEW carets (out of scope) would read `selections` directly — the shape is forward-compatible.

## U6 — automatic native attribution: capture, replicated durability, actor dictionary (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` worktree incl. U1–U5). Implements the handoff's attribution unit on native v14 metadata — no attribution semantics reimplemented; the module wires `insertSet`/`deleteSet` → `createContentIds`/`createContentMapFromContentIds`-equivalent primitives (`createIdMapFromIdSet`, `insertIntoIdMap`, `encodeContentMap`/`decodeContentMap`, `intersectContentMap`, `createAttributionsRenderer`) and persists each captured map as its own immutable replicated record. Owned files: `src/lib/crdt/attribution/{attribution,index}.ts` (new), `src/lib/crdt/document.ts` (init bag `attribution`, ctor attach, `attribution` getter, destroy release), `src/lib/crdt/edytor-doc.ts` (`SCHEMA.roots.attribution`, `UNDO_REPAIR_ORIGIN` hoisted to module scope so capture can exclude the repair's bookkeeping), `src/lib/crdt/index.ts` (attribution exports + the U4b-noted missing `DocumentDestroyedError` re-export), `src/tests/crdt/attribution/**` (new).

**Representation** — one reserved replicated root `doc.get('attribution')` carrying independently-keyed immutable records, no protocol or provider change:

- `a/<writerClient>/<seq>` → `encodeContentMap` bytes — one record per captured transaction. Immutability makes concurrent captures non-destructive (the one-shared-blob defect is structurally impossible); receivers merge, never overwrite; duplicate/reordered delivery is idempotent because records are independent keys merged by `insertIntoIdMap`.
- `c/<replicaClient>` → actorId — replica→actor binding (`attribution.actorOf(client)` resolves an item's inserting replica).
- `u/<actorId>` → `{name?, color?}` — one profile record per stable actor id (LWW; several replicas of one user dedupe to one key — pinned); `setProfile` rewrites only this record and never relabels content.

**Capture seam** — one `beforeObserverCalls` listener per `EdytorDocument`, same ordering slot as the undo-ownership repair: committed **local** transactions only (`transaction.local === true` — the real remote-exclusion contract, `applyUpdate` is forced non-local), gated on `ready()` so the pending bootstrap/hydration window is never attributed, excluding `ATTRIBUTION_ORIGIN` (own bookkeeping), `UNDO_REPAIR_ORIGIN` (repair bookkeeping), metadata-only transactions, and empty cleanup-only changes. `insert = actor` on every inserted id (text atoms AND format markers — the native renderer turns marker attrs into `format: {key: [actor]}` provenance, verified through `toDelta` `attribution` output); `delete = actor` on `deleteSet − transaction.cleanUps` (engine format-normalization removals are system reshaping, not user deletions). The record write runs as a follow-up transaction inside the committing transaction's cleanup sweep — two wire updates (content, then metadata), same shape as the repair's claims.

**Receive/torn delivery** — the same listener scans `transaction.changed` for attribution-root keys on every transaction (local AND remote): unseen `a/` records decode+merge before the observer pass; `u/`/`c/` refresh the dictionaries; own-prefix records skip (merged at capture). A peer may see content before its record — those ids simply report unknown authorship until the record lands (self-heals, pinned); records may also arrive before their content (id-keyed maps don't need payloads — pinned). Attach-time replay merges every existing record, so `attachDocument`/`loadDocument` rebuild the full map on a doc that already carries state (pinned through destroy→reattach replay).

**Undo/redo** — `undo()` resurrects content as NEW ids (`redoItem` copy; `redone`/`keep` are local-only and never serialized). Naive capture would label copies with the undoing actor. Instead, on undo-shaped transactions (a `keep`-marked item in `insertSet`, then an O(store) `redone`-into-`insertSet` scan — the same structural signature the ownership repair uses, reached only on suspicion) each resurrected copy inherits its tombstone's recorded insert-side attrs (minus `delete`) plus `_restored = undoing actor` provenance; unknown prior authors stay unknown; the undo's own deletions still attribute `delete = undoer`. Pinned: bob undoes his delete of alice's `X` → copy carries `insert:alice` + `_restored:bob`, never `insert:bob`, and converges identically on alice's replica; redo re-attributes `delete` on the copy ids. Record writes never enter the undo stack (pinned: `undoStack` counts only user transactions).

**Document surface** — `document.attribution: DocumentAttribution` = `{map, inserts, deletes, actors, actorOf, live(), createRenderer(), setProfile()}`. `map` is the live merged ContentMap; `live()` intersects with `createContentIdsFromDoc(doc, false)` so editing surfaces never see tombstoned attribution; `createRenderer` mints a fresh native `AttributionsRenderer` per call (renderers cache input — never held across commits). Access after teardown throws `DocumentDestroyedError` (getter, same contract as `history`); the merged maps on a released view stay readable on a borrowed doc by design.

**Measurements** (`src/tests/crdt/attribution/overhead.test.ts`, printed + bounded): 200 single-character commits → exactly 200 records, **30.7 B/record**, merged native map **827 B** total (attr-range dedup interns the repeated actor attribute — merge output is ~7× smaller than raw record bytes), full `encode()` 16,582 B, amortized capture **0.075–0.110 ms/commit** incl. the follow-up transaction; 20 format toggles + 5 deletes → 25 records, 51.3 B/record. Cost model: one immutable record per COMMIT (not per character — a 500-char paste is one record); no global blob rewrite, no per-keystroke full-map encode.

**Retention/GC contract (explicit)**: attribution records key id-ranges, not payloads — `gcIdSet` on tombstoned ids drops the bytes (content → `ContentDeleted`) while `insert`/`delete` attribution on those ids survives in the merged map (pinned). Deleted payload can therefore never be reconstructed from attribution after GC — same engine limit, documented in the module header. Records themselves are not compacted by this unit: they are the durability format; a future compaction unit could merge `a/<client>/*` runs into fewer keys (the merged in-memory map already dedupes to ~4 B/covered-id at steady state).

**New tests** — `src/tests/crdt/attribution/` **29 tests, all green**: `capture.test.ts` (insert/format/delete authorship; pending-window exclusion; raw `doc.transact` + custom origins; actor dictionary publish/dedupe/`actorOf`/profile-LWW-never-relabels; undo-stack isolation + one-record-per-commit; `live()` projection; delete-of-unattributed-content; `DocumentDestroyedError` post-teardown; destroy→reattach replay), `durability.test.ts` (encode→`loadDocument`; IndexedDB provider round-trip incl. `storeState` compaction; live sync with no recapture — receiver writes zero `a/` records for remote edits; ownership move keeps content authorship while the mover's placement rewrite is his; offline concurrent convergence; torn content→record delivery heals; metadata-first + duplicated delivery), `undo.test.ts` (cross-actor + same-actor restore; redo re-deletion attribution; history isolation), `overhead.test.ts` (measurements + regression bounds; records travel inside `encode()`).

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0 errors/0 warnings; `pnpm exec prettier --check` + `eslint` clean on owned files; `pnpm vitest run src/tests/crdt/attribution` **29/29 pass**; full `pnpm vitest run src/tests/crdt/` **78 files / 1308 tests pass** (incl. the 150-seed × 3-adapter random corpus running under live capture — no capture-side regressions).

**Unresolved risks / deviations (U7/U8 handoff)**: (1) `_restored` is an underscore-provenance attr that never renders — if U7/U8 wants a public restoring-operation surface it should read `_restored` rather than infer it. (2) `u/` profile LWW means a replica racing a profile change can transiently show the older name — acceptable for presence-adjacent metadata; no vector clock was added deliberately. (3) `beforeObserverCalls` fan-out is now two listeners (repair + capture) on every commit — measured cost above stays sub-0.1 ms, but a third listener should reuse the same scan pass rather than add another. (4) Record count is linear in commit count by design; long-lived docs wanting bounded metadata rows need the noted future compaction (merge `a/<client>/*` ranges) — recorded, not built. (5) `attrsH`/attr interning keeps merged-map memory flat per distinct attribute; many DISTINCT actor ids on one doc grow `attrs` sets — fine at collaboration scale, watch for synthetic actor-id spam (a hostile replica could mint ids; no quota yet — documented).

## U6b — adversarial review follow-up: renumber-proof records, torn-undo provenance, reference capabilities, U5 smalls (2026-09-22)

**Source identity**: same tree (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` worktree incl. U1–U9, U7 attribution read-surface work concurrent in `attribution/**`). Implements the review's R1–R6 + the destroyed-attach gate. Owned files touched: `src/lib/crdt/attribution/attribution.ts` (capture/key internals only — `live()`/`createRenderer`/`actorOf` read semantics untouched), `src/lib/crdt/document.ts` (over-release guard + gate + docs), `src/lib/collaboration/{documentSync,awarenessSelection}.ts` (U5 smalls), `src/tests/crdt/{attribution,document}/**`, this ledger.

- **R1 (high) — frozen record namespace vs. engine clientID renumbering.** `a/` record keys are now `a/<nonce>/<seq>` where `nonce` is a per-attach `crypto.getRandomValues` value — never interpreted, so the in-place `doc.clientID` renumber (`Transaction.js` fires when a remote `insertSet` contains our id) cannot collide record namespaces, cannot make two replicas share a self-skip prefix, and cannot LWW-eat one replica's records (the client segment was never semantically read). Self-skip now means exactly "the current attach's nonce" — a superseded attach's records arriving late re-merge (`insertIntoIdMap` is an idempotent range union — verified). `c/<clientID>` republish is lazy-detected in the existing `beforeObserverCalls` hook (no engine event exists): a change sets `republishPending`, and the write runs on the FIRST transaction that is not itself doomed to renumber — **critical**: the write must NOT run inside a remote transaction whose `insertSet` contains `doc.clientID` (the engine's own renumber predicate, which fires in cleanup AFTER this hook). `setAttr` mints an attr Item under `doc.clientID`; republishing there minted `(shared,clock)` items that collided with the peer's content at the same id and silently dropped it — verified at store level before the defer was added. `c/<old>` is kept (pre-renumber ids keep resolving); two replicas that shared one clientID have indistinguishable shared-id items — `c/<shared>` LWW between their actors is inherent (records' own attrs stay exact). Pins: `renumbering.test.ts` — forced collision hydrates+renumbers, records merge both directions, `actorOf` resolves the renumbered replica on both docs; forked-replica mints under the stolen id → receiver renumbers → records still merge.
- **R2 (medium) — undo under torn delivery.** `_restored:<undoer>` is now stamped on EVERY resurrected subrange, including attr-less tombstone gaps (record still in flight → provenance only, authorship stays unknown rather than invented). Residual documented in the module header + ledger: the late record attributes the tombstone's ORIGINAL ids only; the copy's `insert` stays unknown — convergent. Pinned in `undo.test.ts` (alice's record withheld; bob deletes+undoes → `_restored:bob`, no `insert`; record lands → tombstone attributed, copy provenance-only).
- **R3 (low) — refcount over-release.** The deduped `attachDocument` returns ONE shared handle, so `destroy()` calls cannot be authenticated per caller — documented contract: `destroy()` releases exactly ONE reference, at most once per acquired reference; additional shares go through the new public `retain(): () => void`, which returns a ONE-SHOT release capability that cannot over-release by construction. Internals: `_releaseReference` shared by `destroy()` and `retain()` closures. Pins in `document.test.ts` (one-shot release, retained-ref lifetime, `retain` on destroyed → `DocumentDestroyedError`).
- **R4 (low) — remount presence re-broadcast.** `clearAwarenessSelection` now skips `setLocalState` when the recomputed map is unchanged (`selections` identical by reference — the sweep only allocates on a real drop — plus a `JSON.stringify` deep-compare of the `selection` mirror). Pin: live remount → zero broadcasts; dead-owner sweep → exactly one.
- **R5 (low) — throwing waiter through provider `synced`.** `notifyDocumentReady` isolates each waiter in try/catch (`[edytor-sync]` console.error convention) — a throwing view callback neither aborts siblings nor propagates through `synced`. Pinned.
- **R6 (info) — no change seam on records, by design.** Record merges are silent pull-model updates (`map` mutated in place; every fresh `live()`/slice/renderer sees current state). Consumers needing reactive attribution watch doc `update` filtered to `a/` keys or poll `map`; a dedicated projection republish belongs to U7. Documented on `document.attribution`.
- **Attach gate** — `attachDocument` on a `doc.isDestroyed` raw doc refuses with `DocumentDestroyedError('attachDocument')` before any composition (its shared types are already torn down). Pinned.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0e/0w; prettier+eslint clean on owned files; `pnpm vitest run src/tests/crdt/{attribution,document}` **142/142** (34 attribution incl. 2 renumbering pins + torn-undo pin; presence gains R4/R5; document gains retain/gate pins). Full suite + `test:dom` in the closing report.

**Deferred/residual**: concurrent minting under one clientID by TWO replicas drops items at the ENGINE level (`(client,clock)` collision — the renumber heals the receiver only); attribution now does nothing that worsens it (republish defers past doomed transactions, nonces never mint under a dead id). `c/<shared>` winner is LWW-inherent. U7 handoff items unchanged.

## U7 — one canonical attributed projection: runs view projects native authorship through every read surface (2026-09-22)

**Source identity**: same tree (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` worktree incl. U1–U6b; U6b capture-layer fixes concurrent). Implements the handoff's read-surface unit: the maintained runs view consumes the U6 merged `ContentMap` and ports the CURRENT-STATE half of native `YNode#toDelta` attribution so `runs()`, `contentItems()`, `project()`, `contentJSON()`, `DocChange` payloads, and Svelte rendering all read ONE attributed interpretation — no second authorship algorithm. Owned files: `src/lib/crdt/text/runs.ts` (attribution types, ported governance state machine, attr observer, commitInfo union), `src/lib/crdt/edytor-doc.ts` (`EdytorDocConfig.attribution` getter + `attributionRoot`, `R.attach` opts, `contentItems`/`project` reroute), `src/lib/crdt/document.ts` (stable live-map getter — resolves the controller attached after the facade), `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts` (ContentItem boundary), `src/lib/utils/json.ts` (`JSONAttribution`), `src/lib/text/{deltas,text.svelte}.ts` + `src/lib/block/block.svelte.ts` (additive passthrough), `src/lib/crdt/index.ts` (exports), `src/tests/crdt/runs/attribution.test.ts` (new), `src/tests/mirror-incremental.test.tsx` (assertion updated to the attributed shape).

**Semantics ported from `ynode.js` `toDelta`** — not re-specified: marker-row walking drives format governance (`prevUnattributedFormats`/`changedAttributedFormats`); an attributed format marker opens governed context for following content, a `null` clear closes it per-key (per-key clears prevent stale `format:{key:[author]}` on reformat/unformat); tri-state attr semantics (`undefined` inherit / `null` clear / object merge); `combineData`/`mergeAttr`-style ambient∪own merges sliced per piece AND per attribution boundary inside a piece; attributed deleted atoms resurrect into the projection carrying original authorship while unattributed deletes stay hidden; inline atoms carry `insert` authorship. Native-parity is enforced by an oracle test comparing `content.toDelta({renderer}).toJSON()` against the view's attributed runs on the same transactions.

**Maintenance** — the view deep-observes the attribution root (`attrObserver` on `SCHEMA.roots.attribution`, independent of the block registry): a late `a/` record maps its covered id-ranges back to backing-text consumers and republishes ONLY covered blocks (torn content→record delivery self-heals — pinned); attribution commits union into `commitInfo().content` so `DocChange`/`project()` republish on metadata-only commits; prefix/suffix run identity and cross-block array identity preserved (pinned: attribution-only commit republishes only covered blocks). `attributed()` reports whether the source is live so unattributed docs pay zero overhead.

**Public shape** — `RunAttribution = JSONAttribution` (`{insert?, insertAt?, delete?, deleteAt?, format?, formatAt?, …custom}`) on `ContentRun` text/inline runs; additive `attribution?` on `JSONText`/`JSONInlineBlock`. Read-side only: writers never consume it (`jsonBlockToSpec` ignores it; no editing API writes it). Payloads are interned + deep-frozen; equal marks/data/attribution share `===` identity across runs and snapshots; `snapshot()`/`contentJSON()` return owned copies (no engine aliasing).

**Wiring** — `EdytorDocument` passes `attribution: () => this._attributionCtl?.view.map ?? null` (stable getter: the facade builds before the attribution controller attaches, and a fresh-object getter would defeat the view's identity-based caching); `project()` swaps `M.project`'s raw `contentItemsOf` for `runsView.contentItems(id)` per block while attributed; `DocChange` `added`/`content` payloads emit the same attributed items (`===` identity with `runs()` where the snapshot allows). Text/delta/Svelte layers pass the field through to rendering; `Text.value`/`edytor.value` now emit the attributed split shape (runs no longer merge across authorship boundaries — `Hello`/`!` stay separate once `!` carries `insert:[actor]`, which is why the mirror test's `{text:'Hello!'}` assertion was updated).

**New tests — 19, all green** (`src/tests/crdt/runs/attribution.test.ts`): local capture (insert/format/partial-format/unformat-close/suggestion-merge/inline-atom/delete-survivors/undo-inherits-authorship/split-keeps-both-halves), projection surfaces (`project`/`contentItems` parity, `contentJSON` export shape, deep-frozen + interned identity, subscribeBlock once-per-commit, `DocChange` payloads), remote & delivery (remote actor never local, torn-delivery heal, offline concurrent convergence), structural sharing (attribution-only commit republishes covered blocks only, undo through the maintained view). Plus the scratch-verified native `toDelta` parity probe folded into the suite's oracle.

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0e/0w; `pnpm lint` (prettier+eslint) clean; `vitest run` full unit suite **100 files / 1699 tests — all green** (the three U6b capture-layer pins — reattach-after-destroy gate, 2 clientID-renumbering cases — landed green in the concurrent U6b pass during this unit's validation). `mirror-incremental` 4/4 after the assertion update; ~40 stale model-fixture value assertions restored via a harness canonicalizer (`stripAttribution`/`canonicalValue` in `test.utils.ts` — strips provenance + re-merges authorship-boundary splits, same treatment `removeIds` gives volatile ids; `edytor.value` itself stays attributed — the mirror oracle requires it to equal `project()`). `pnpm bench:runs` before/after on the unattributed path — no regression, identical invariants (999/1000 snapshot reuse, recompute fan-out 1, split head+sibling only, 2/3 run reuse on inline metadata): warm `runs()` 0.0012→0.0004ms, `computeAllRuns` 1000-block 15.68→4.98ms mean, cold attach+read 24.45→7.70ms mean, 4000-block cold read 25.45→23.95ms (`bench/results/runs-2026-09-22T00-14-53-749Z.json` vs `runs-2026-09-21T21-23-06-661Z.json`; same-machine wall-clock, machine variance suspected in the absolute deltas — invariants are the durable signal).

**Deferred/residual**: (1) `_restored` provenance flows through `insert`-side attrs but no dedicated public field surfaces it — read it off `attribution` if a restore-operation badge is ever wanted. (2) No attributed-workload lane in `runs.bench.test.ts` yet — the governed walk is per-recompute O(pieces×attrs); add a workload if a hot doc shows the walk in profiles. (3) Attribution of the SEED stays empty by design (pending window) — seeded content exports no `insert` until an actor edits it. (4) `edytor.value` carries `insert:[anon-<uuid>]` for documents using the default anonymous actor — correct per the actor model, but consumers persisting the export should expect ephemeral actor ids in provenance fields.

## U8 — one admission boundary for every entry path; explicit readiness and refusal-preserves-data (2026-09-21)

**Source identity**: same tree (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` worktree incl. U1–U6). Implements the handoff's admission unit — every way content enters an `EdytorDocument` crosses one ordered gate. Owned files: `src/lib/crdt/admission.ts` (new), `src/lib/crdt/document.ts` (gate wiring + contract docs), `src/lib/crdt/index.ts` (admission exports + `bindCrdt().admission`), `src/lib/crdt/protocols/sync.ts` + `src/lib/crdt/providers/{indexeddb,websocket}.ts` (gate-vocabulary imports rerouted through `admission.ts` — one doorway, same symbols), `src/lib/crdt/migration/migrate.ts` (`MigrateResult.update`), `src/tests/crdt/document/admission.test.ts` (new, 23), `src/tests/crdt/migration/migration-admission.test.ts` (new, 5), `docs/crdt-v14-{providers,migration}.md` touch-ups.

**The gate** (`assertAdmission(doc, docName)`) runs three ordered reads — `assertUsableDoc` (foreign engine object → `UnsupportedDocError 'foreign'`; applied-but-unmigrated v13 layout → `'legacy'` route-to-migration) → `assertSchema` (`SchemaMismatchError` `unversioned|unsupported|foreign`) → verdict `fresh|initialized`. All pure reads — a refusal mutates nothing (v14 gate reads materialize only root-share entries, which do not encode; verified byte-identical). `inspectAdmission` is the non-throwing audit variant; `UndecodableUpdateError` wraps decode/integration failures distinctly from schema refusals.

**The admission matrix** (path → validation → refusal-leaves):

- `createDocument` — uniform `assertAdmission` on the fresh doc (trivially `fresh`).
- `loadDocument` — `admitUpdate` decodes+integrates onto a **scratch doc**, gates the merged result, composes only on admit. Refusal: nothing composed, caller's bytes untouched. (Was: apply-then-assert on the live target — same public outcome but the refused payload mutated the temp doc first.)
- `attachDocument` — gates the borrowed doc BEFORE composition; refusal leaves it byte-identical and re-attachable after healing (pinned: stamp `meta.v`+`meta.schema` → attach → `hydrated`). Never auto-seeds — always attaches `pending`. Foreign ROOTS without a schema claim admit (`sync()` seeds next to them).
- `sync()` — the readiness transition re-runs the gate: `initialized` → `hydrated`, `fresh` → `init` → `local`, problem state → typed refusal, stays `pending`, content preserved, retry-after-heal legal. **Behavioral change**: a doc carrying unversioned registry content is now REFUSED at sync (was: `init` stamped a schema over it — silent adoption the transport boundary would quarantine).
- Provider hydration / websocket admission — unchanged `applyUpdateStaged` staging (scratch → `checkSchema` → commit); the same vocabulary, imported through the shared doorway.
- IndexedDB persistence recovery — unchanged staged hydration + `_hydrationRefused` (no `synced`, `whenSynced` rejects, compaction permanently blocked — R2 contract retained verbatim).
- v13 migration — `MigrateResult.update` (the persisted snapshot) feeds `loadDocument` → `hydrated`; `attachDocument(result.doc)` + `sync()` → `hydrated`. Raw v13 payload at the load path → `UnsupportedDocError 'legacy'`.

**Readiness contract (explicit)**: `pending` — reads/`attachSync`/`adoptSemantics`/`sync()` legal, `history` refuses (`DocumentNotReadyError`), nothing bootstrap-shaped written or broadcast (pinned through a real `IndexeddbPersistence`: the BC `SyncStep2` decode yields an uninitialized doc, and the doc emits zero updates until `synced` fires — the actor's attribution records, written at attach, are the designed pre-readiness exception); `local`/`hydrated` — full surface; the distinction is who decided the content.

**History purity** — hydration/migration/admission writes never enter undo: `transaction.local === false` excludes all applies (originless included — pinned), bootstrap/init commits predate history attach, scratch-doc writes never touch the live doc or its listeners.

**New tests — 28, all green**: `admission.test.ts` (verdict matrix + write-free pins; loadDocument accept/refuse incl. byte-preservation of refused payloads; attachDocument refuse→byte-identical→heal→hydrated; foreign-roots coexist; real v13 `yjs` Doc → `'foreign'`; sync() re-check refuse/stay-pending/retry; pending→local, pending→hydrated, idempotent; stubbed-sync zero-write pin; IndexeddbPersistence SyncStep2 no-bootstrap decode + zero-updates-until-synced; accepted hydration → hydrated + empty undoStack; refused hydration → pending + doc byte-identical + refused row preserved). `migration-admission.test.ts` (migrate → `loadDocument(result.update)` → hydrated + JSON parity + empty undo; `attachDocument(result.doc)` → hydrated; raw v13 payload → `'legacy'`; failed migration → nothing activated + source rows intact; rebuilt doc inspects `initialized`/clean).

**Checks run**: `pnpm check` 0/0; `eslint` + `prettier` clean on owned files (two pre-existing prettier warnings in `edytor.utils.ts`/`selection.svelte.ts` — not this unit's); `pnpm vitest run src/tests/crdt/` **81 files / 1342 pass**; `pnpm test -- --run` **96 files / 1663 pass**; `pnpm test:dom` **146 pass**.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: (1) `sync()` with a malformed `value` can still partially write before `init` throws (pre-existing — admission covers schema, not seed-spec validation; `createDocument` destroys the document on seed failure, `attachDocument` paths leave the partial writes on the borrowed doc). (2) `pendingStructs` are not an admission input — a load payload missing causal deps restores only committed state (documented choice: load keeps whatever was offered; transport staging retains the stricter pending-aware rules). (3) `assertUsableDoc`'s `doc.get` read materializes an empty root type on a real v13 `yjs` Doc — engine-level noise on a refused foreign object, never a write to user content. (4) `attachDocument` on a doc already registered-and-live skips re-admission by design (the live document is the admission).

## U9 — public surface audit, declarations, package graph, document docs (2026-10-24)

**Source identity**: same tree (`feat/crdt-v14-engine` worktree incl. U1–U8). Implements the handoff's public-surface unit — makes the integrated `EdytorDocument` API the headline export, proves declarations for strict NodeNext/ES2022 packed consumers, documents the package graph decision. Owned files touched: `src/lib/crdt/index.ts` (reorganized into categorized export blocks — document API first, then admission/attribution, providers/protocols, migration, bound facade, runs, engine namespace — no symbol set change vs. the U8 surface, export _order_ is now deliberate and documented in-file), `src/lib/index.ts` (root barrel: component/plugin surface first, then the CRDT document re-exports + root-bound sync conveniences — same set, grouped), `docs/crdt-v14-document.md` (new user guide), `README.md` (integrated-document section + collaboration lead-in), `tests/packed-consumer/**` (document API coverage — see below), `src/tests/crdt/document/docs-examples.test.ts` (new, 6 — executable documentation), `tests/packed-consumer/README.md`, this ledger.

**Export audit** (emitted artifacts, not source greps): `edytor/crdt/edytor` runtime = **75 symbols** / declarations = **162** incl. types; `edytor/crdt` = **161** vendored engine symbols; root = explicit component/plugin/provider exports + `./plugins/index.js` + `./crdt/index.js` wildcards. Classification recorded in `docs/crdt-v14-document.md` and in-file at `src/lib/crdt/index.ts`:

- _intentional_ — document factories/lifecycle/readiness/errors, `EdytorSync` factories + providers, awareness, admission gates + errors, `DocumentAttribution`, migration, raw engine under `edytor/crdt`, `bindCrdt` engine injection.
- _internal-but-exported (kept)_ — `bind*` builders (facade/runs/nodes/providers/protocols/migration/document), low-level facade/run types tests and packed consumers already name. Removal would be breaking for zero benefit — the bind layer is the documented injection seam.
- _accidental helper leakage (kept)_ — engine constants/protocol helpers surfaced through `export *`. Left exported: tests and packed consumers already use them; hiding them now is a breaking change with no type-safety upside.

**Declaration surface** — audited emitted `dist/crdt/{document,admission,attribution/index}.d.ts`: all public signatures are concrete (`history: YUndoManager`, `attribution: DocumentAttribution`, `attachSync(sync: EdytorSync) → EdytorSyncCleanup | void`, `transact: <T>(cb: () => T) => T`, `encode() → Uint8Array`); no `any` leaks; every cross-module reference uses `.js`-suffixed relative paths resolvable under `moduleResolution: nodenext` (the packed lanes compile with `skipLibCheck: false`). `DocumentAttribution`'s `map`/`inserts`/`deletes`/`createRenderer()` types resolve to the **same vendored `dts` files** `edytor/crdt`'s types point at — `Y.ContentMap` and `document.attribution.map` are one type identity in a packed consumer, not two look-alikes (pinned in `smoke-types.node.ts` via `Y.ContentMap` assignability). Provider root conveniences keep their `ProviderStack[...]` annotations so emitted declarations reference `../crdt/index.js` — no deep-path leaks into packed consumers.

**Package graph decision — three entries, no `./crdt/document`**: `edytor` (component surface, bundler-required), `edytor/crdt/edytor` (the entire non-component surface incl. the document API — node/SSR-safe), `edytor/crdt` (raw engine). A dedicated document subpath would re-export a strict subset of the same bound entry — nothing new becomes importable — and document/provider types are deliberately one graph (`attachSync` takes the same `EdytorSync` the provider factories return). **Verified bundle scoping without a subpath**: a vite lib-build importing only `createDocument` from the _packed tarball_ produces a 798.5 kB (210.8 kB gzip) chunk containing zero provider/migration code (`WebsocketProvider`, `bindProviders`, `bindMigration` counts = 0; `IndexeddbPersistence` survives only inside doc comments) — offline creation already excludes transport code via tree-shaking, so a fourth entry adds a boundary with no payoff. Documented in `docs/crdt-v14-document.md` "Why no `edytor/crdt/document` subpath".

**Documentation** — `docs/crdt-v14-document.md` (headless `createDocument`→edit→undo→`encode`→`loadDocument`; one `<Edytor>` view; two views on one document incl. the factory-hoisting rule; provider attachment via `attachSync`/`createIndexeddbSync`; attribution reads incl. `live()`/`createRenderer`/`actorOf`/`setProfile`; readiness/pending contract; admission vocabulary reference). README gained a matching integrated-document section (headless example first, then `{document, sync}` props) — collaboration docs unchanged where they describe relay-vs-participating server semantics (U9 makes no wire-compat claims beyond what tests prove). Every snippet in the guide is backed by `src/tests/crdt/document/docs-examples.test.ts` (6 tests — the doc _is_ the test source of truth, not hand-checked prose).

**Packed-consumer coverage** — `tests/packed-consumer/` now exercises the document API against the installed tarball, not the source tree: `smoke.js` (node runtime — create→edit→undo→encode→load round-trip, `attachSync` with a stubbed factory, readiness transitions, attribution map/actors, admission errors); `smoke-types.node.ts` (strict nodenext ES2022 — `createDocument`/`loadDocument`/`attachDocument`/`EdytorDocument`/`DocumentAttribution`/admission types from `edytor`, `bindCrdt(Y)` identity, `Y.ContentMap` = attribution map type — no casts); `smoke-types.ts` (bundler lane — same surface through `moduleResolution: bundler`); `svelte-app/App.svelte` + `smoke-svelte.mjs` (two `<Edytor>` views on ONE `createDocument` + `createIndexeddbSync('packed-document')` — driver asserts edits propagate view→view through the shared document, `attachSync` attaches the idb factory once for both views, `encode()`→`loadDocument()` round-trips the shared state, plus the pre-existing editable/readonly mount/edit/teardown lanes).

**Vendor declaration freshness** — `scripts/regen-crdt-vendor-types.sh` run before deciding: emitted the known upstream `TS2589` (deep instantiation in `delta-helpers.js`, documented in-script), produced a declaration tree **byte-identical to the shipped `dts/`** (`DTS IDENTICAL — was not stale`). No regeneration retained; `vendor/yjs/UPSTREAM.md` patch manifest (P1/P2/P4) stays accurate.

**Checks run**: `pnpm package` clean → `publint` "All good!" (re-run after the U7-type export additions); `tests/packed-consumer/run.sh` full pass — tarball pack/install, node runtime smoke, vite client + SSR builds, browser mount/edit/teardown, nodenext strict tsc, bundler strict tsc ("shared document: two views + attachSync + encode/load OK"); `docs-examples.test.ts` 6/6; `pnpm vitest run src/tests/crdt/{document,attribution}` **132/132**; `pnpm test:typecheck` 0e/0w; prettier+eslint clean on all touched files. **Concurrent-U7 caveat**: while this unit was landing, the U7 projected-run-attribution stream modified `text/runs.ts` + `edytor-doc.ts` + `edytor.svelte.ts` + `test.utils.ts` mid-flight — `pnpm check` recovered to **0e/0w** once their in-flight `textOfStruct` error resolved, but `pnpm test` still carries 63 failures in 7 fixture files and `pnpm test:dom` 48 in `dom.test.ts` at this snapshot, all one signature: serialized values now split runs at authorship boundaries (`{"text":"Fist"}` → `"Fi"` + `{"attribution":{"insert":[anon-…]},"text":"st"}`) while the fixture comparator hasn't been taught attribution normalization yet; `pnpm lint` flags prettier diffs on U7-owned files (`edytor-doc.ts`, `runs.ts`, `text/deltas.ts`, a `src/tests/crdt/runs/_scratch.test.ts` scratch file, `tests/editor-dst/`). None of those files are U9-owned; all U9 deliverables are green and the packed artifact is unaffected. The U7 exports that landed mid-unit were folded into the index in the same pass (`RunAttribution`, `AttributionSource`, `AttributionIdMap`, `RunsAttachOptions`, `CommitInfo`, `ModelView`, `EngineTransaction`, `JSONAttribution`) so `EdytorDocConfig.attribution`, `RunsApi.modelState`, `RunView.modelCtx`/`commitInfo` and `ContentRun.attribution` stay nameable from the public surface.

**Unresolved risks / deviations**: (1) `CommitInfo` in `text/runs.ts` is exported by its module but deliberately not re-exported from `crdt/index.ts` — it was internal-only before; flag for U7 if their attribution-on-runs work adds a new exported type (`RunAttribution` or similar) — `ContentRun`/`DecoratedRun` re-exports carry added _fields_ automatically, but a new _named type_ needs one line in the runs export block. (2) The accidental-leakage bucket stays public by choice — a future semver-major could curate it via explicit `export {}` lists, but doing it now would break tests + packed consumers for cosmetic gain. (3) `attachSync` dedupe remains factory-identity keyed (U5 note retained — docs teach hoisting the factory). (4) Root `edytor` still hard-requires a bundler (`.svelte` imports) — documented as intentional, not a defect.
