**Validation — defects the qualification lanes caught and fixes** (each fix validated by lane re-run):

1. `marks-multiblock` fixture selected one char too far into post-mention text — selection offset corrected `1→0` in `src/tests/fixtures/dom/marks-multiblock.fixtures.tsx`.
2. U7 attribution-aware output broke legacy structural browser assertions — `tests/editor-dom/helpers.ts` canonicalizes payloads for assertions that aren't about provenance (strip `attribution`, merge adjacent equal-mark runs); `Text.value` keeps the attributed shape.
3. `Text.svelte` called `attachment.destroy()` unconditionally — readonly `Text.attach()` can return `undefined` → `attachment?.destroy()` in update + teardown paths (fixed both code-suggestion hotkey tests).
4. **Selection/history race**: `selection.svelte.ts`'s async staleness guard dropped legitimate DOM range restoration when an intermediate collapsed model state appeared during the `getTextNode()` await — caused 7 DST `selection-model-dom-mismatch` failures + 1 reverse-selection undo failure. Fix keeps the guard for genuinely superseded writes but lets history restoration survive the intermediate state; history listeners unbind on view destroy, per-view undo snapshots are keyed by view origin, only the issuing view restores its caret, range endpoints normalize and preserve direction. **Transient flake recorded**: one early DST replay failed at step −1 during initial snapshot capture (serialized value briefly omitted an inline item) — passed on rerun; read as load-settling noise in the harness, not a semantic defect, flagged here because the evidence policy requires it.

**Validation — benchmarks** (artifacts persisted under `bench/results/` with source-hash metadata; all measurements are wall-clock on Apple M4 Pro, single machine, for within-report comparison only):

- `bench:crdt` (`bench/results/latest.json`): move-100k payload v14 placement **54B** vs v13 copy 100 136B; split-100k ownership records **346B** vs v13 50 136B; merge-100k claim **35B** vs v13 100 035B; typing v14 local p50/p95 **0.0263/0.0491 ms**, remote 0.0138/0.0276 (v13 local 0.0122/0.0300 — v14 local commits are ~2.2× v13 p50, the price of ownership/attribution bookkeeping, recorded not hidden); load-1k v14 **2.2564 ms** / 188 867B (v13 1.9011 ms / 147 867B); delta-128-runs v14 p50 0.0622 ms (v13 0.0086); keystroke-1k-blocks facade p50 0.209 ms (write 0.1604 + commit 0.0457, view re-collect 0.5714+0.8142+0.2445, runs 0.0361, payload 37B); staged apply 0.0287 ms vs manual scratch 7.067 ms/update.
- `bench:runs` (`runs-latest.json`): warm `runs()` per block p50 0.0003 ms; local 1-char insert recomputes **1** block, **999/1000** sibling blocks reuse cached runs (20/20 on the small fixture); remote insert recomputes 1; full-recompute baseline `computeAllRuns` on 1k blocks p50 3.9121 ms — the maintained view avoids it per keystroke.
- `bench:browser` (`browser-latest.json`, real packed-tarball install, trusted CDP keystrokes, stage-attributed end-to-end): keystroke totalMs p50 = **7.0 ms** (6 blocks) / **6.7 ms** (1k: input 0.6 + write 1.7 + dispatchTail 3.8 + raf 4.2) / **38.8 ms** (5k: write 10.2 + dispatchTail 23 + raf 25) / **8.2 ms** after 200-churn history; shared-backing 100k-50blocks keystroke 10.9 ms. `rafMs` is frame-callback scheduling, explicitly not paint. Mount 108 ms (small) / 623 ms (1k) / **16.3 s** (5k); heap 10 MB → 239 MB at 5k.
- **Packed consumer bundle** (browser-app vite build): `index-*.js` **512 518B min / 153 378B gzip / 131 977B brotli** — defaults-on, attribution included.
- `bench/u10-attribution-overhead.mjs` (console output, re-run on final tree; ON = integrated `EdytorDocument` with unconditional attribution, OFF = bare facade — **no attribution opt-out exists**, this is the honest baseline): keystroke p50 Δ = +76.6% at 100 blocks (0.0441→0.0779 ms), +9.6% at 1k (0.1874→0.2053), +10.4% at 5k (0.9672→1.0681). 200 single-char commits: `encode()` **14 752B vs 512B (28.81×)**, update stream **19 458B/400 events vs 4 965B/200 (3.92× — the follow-up attribution commit doubles event count)**, IndexedDB `updates` rows **401 vs 201 (2.00×, 19 836B vs 5 265B stored)**. U6 cross-check: 200 records = 6 142B → **30.7B/record**, merged map 827B. The 29× encode delta on a 200-keystroke microdoc is the documented record-per-edit cost; it is durability-proportional metadata, not content amplification (attribution bytes scale with edit count, not doc size).

**Validation — exact-identity invariants** (asserted in tests AND measured in the browser bench): every sampled keystroke commit was fast-path — `commitFast` count **24/24** on all five browser lanes; runs-view `recomputedBlocks` p50 = **1** per keystroke (small/1k/5k/churned), i.e. a one-char edit rebuilds exactly one block's runs. `itemsWalked` p50 = 66 / 2 054 / 10 054 items (small/1k/5k) — the commit skeleton fanout walks ~2 items per block, O(blocks) not O(content); `markersWalked` 0 except the shared-backing lane (5 948, where all 50 siblings display segments of one 100k text and **50/50 recompute by design** — the documented `textConsumers` fanout, pinned in `gateF2/wu7-shared-invalidation.test.ts`). `range-reads.test.ts` bounds maintained range reads to ≤64+128 items per read and <1 200 for a two-segment merge (vs ~1 500 for a full walk) — all pass.

**Validation — resource lifetime**: 363 `destroy()`/`dispose()` call sites across 35 CRDT test files. Focused coverage: `document.test.ts` "destroy — owned vs borrowed" describe (owned doc destroyed exactly once, borrowed raw doc/awareness never destroyed, `DocumentDestroyedError` gates every public service, destroyed-manager replacement, `retain()` reference lifetime, provider cleanups run once), `document-views.test.ts` (sibling-view teardown isolation, origin untracking, constructor-failure cleanup — a failed view destroys only what it owned), `presence.test.ts` (awareness destroyed exactly once by `document.destroy()` or `doc.destroy()`, per-view caret removal), `gateH-lifecycle-probes.test.ts` (destroy mid-`storeState` consistency, post-dispose repair-listener detach/re-arm), `gate2/lifecycle.test.ts` (WS + IDB provider lifecycle), plus the browser `collaboration.spec.ts` awareness-cleanup-on-destroy test and the SSR event-loop proof (awareness sweep interval `unref()`'d — packed-consumer SSR exits).

**Validation — source/vendor identity**: `src/lib/crdt/vendor/yjs/UPSTREAM.md` re-verified mechanically — engine `src/` normalized for P1 (`lib0/`→`lib0-v14/` specifier rewrite) then diffed against the pristine upstream tarball `@y/y@14.0.0-rc.26` (`96c96e1f…`, sha-256 `4b5ad410…8d7b85`): **6 files differ, +475/−14 lines** (`index.js`, `structs/Item.js`, `utils/RelativePosition.js`, `utils/Renderer.js`, `utils/renderer-helpers.js`, `ynode.js`) plus **1 new file** `utils/RangeCursor.js` (407 lines) — every delta is the documented P4/P5 patch set. `global.d.ts` and `LICENSE` are **byte-identical** after normalization. Vendored test tree `vendor-tests/yjs/tests/` is **identical** to upstream after reversing the documented P2 rewrites (and dropping the P3 `sync-shim.js`). `lib0-v14` pin = `npm:lib0@1.0.0-rc.32` (aliased; v13 runtime keeps `lib0@0.2.117`).

**Semantic compression** (working tree vs HEAD `d9b7de0` — the pre-integration commit; git diff `--stat` + untracked-file line counts): production `src/lib` excluding vendored engine **+7 509/−2 402 tracked + ~2 341 new-file lines**; vendored engine **+763/−16** tracked (plus RangeCursor.js new); tests **+4 094** tracked plus new `src/tests/crdt/{attribution,document,gateF1,gateF2,hardening}`, `tests/editor-dst/` and ~15 additional spec files; docs/bench artifacts +29 172 (mostly persisted bench JSON). Independent mechanisms retired: `src/lib/crdt/compat.ts` deleted outright (the v13-shaped adapter — the facade now serves the compat contract natively); the Edytor-side private checkpoint index deleted in favour of shared engine traversal (U3); `localProvider.ts` deleted (real providers). No LOC-percentage claim is made — the numbers exist to show where code went, per the handoff's compression rule.

**Risks / not-verified**: (1) **Full firefox/webkit `tests/editor-dom` projects were NOT re-run on the final tree** — the last full serial matrix predates the U10 fixes (`docs/archive/crdt-v14-browser-proof.md` records firefox 371p/2s incl. a documented harness-only failure fixed afterward, webkit 369p/6s/1-retry). The affected surface was re-proven by chromium 410 + all-engine DST 14 + ff/webkit collab 42, but a claim that "the full editor-dom suite passes on firefox/webkit today" would exceed the evidence. (2) The DST step −1 transient (above) is unresolved as flake-vs-race — one occurrence, not reproduced, harness-load suspected. (3) `itemsWalked` ~2/block per keystroke means commit fanout is O(blocks) even when one block changes — acceptable at 5k (write p50 10.2 ms of a 38.8 ms keystroke dominated by dispatchTail+raf) but it is not constant-cost; do not quote it as such. (4) The 5k-block mount (16.3 s, 239 MB heap) and keystroke total (38.8 ms p50) are honest large-doc costs — most of the keystroke tail is DOM/scheduling (`dispatchTail` 23 ms + `raf` 25 ms), engine write 10.2 ms. (5) `runs.bench` stays skipped inside `test:crdt` by design (it's the `bench:runs` lane, not a test). (6) Warnings observed in lanes and judged non-failures: upstream "sync protocol doesnt support v2" fallbacks (vendored tests), the intentional corrupt-staged-update error log, "Yjs imported more than once" in a differential test, `cloneJson` `undefined`-drop warnings, pre-existing readonly warnings — all expected/documented, none test failures. (7) No PR/commit state asserted — the tree is dirty working state on `feat/crdt-v14-engine`, consistent with the unit's no-commit history.

## U7 — initialization quadratic removal

**Root cause (attributed).** `init` looped `M.insertBlock` per root spec inside one transaction. Each call ran `liveChildrenOf` → `view()` → `modelCtx()` → `syncTransaction()` (`text/runs.ts`), which re-folds the transaction's ACCUMULATING `changed` map (registry `subs` grow one per inserted block; each fold costs 2 `getAttr` per sub) plus the per-spec dup-check `blockNodeOf` — insert i re-read i−1 subs ⇒ Σ(2i+1) = N² mid-transaction lookups, plus the intrinsic 2N commit fold. Ownership verified by split counters on the unmodified tree: 200 blk → midTx 40,000 / commit 400; 500 → 250,000/1,000; 5,000 → 25,000,000/10,000. `collectBlocks`/`liveChildrenOf` scans were NOT the cause (registryScans stayed 1 — the maintained kids index is shared). `syncTransaction` itself was left untouched: it is only quadratic when invoked per-write mid-transaction, and `ensurePlacements` rebuilds O(changed) on the same path anyway, so an incremental cursor would not rescue general batched ops — the bulk path removes the call pattern instead.

**Fix.** `placement/model.ts`: extracted `materializeSpec` (byte-identical write shape) and added `M.insertBlocks(doc, dest, specs)` — validates the whole batch first (sanitize → parent-visible → batch-wide dup scan), then ONE `liveChildrenOf` + a local `rankBetween` chain; sequential calls mint the same chain, so emitted ranks are identical. `insertBlock` delegates. `edytor-doc.ts` `init` calls it once. Semantic change: init content is now all-or-nothing (a dup id refuses the whole batch; previously that spec was skipped). `tests/crdt/scenarios/seeds.ts` `modelSpecSeed` uses the bulk path (also removes the O(N²) collect-per-insert in unviewed seeds).

**Probe** (`node .artifacts/editor-performance-audit/initialization-probe.mjs`, extended with mid/commit split + 5k row; results in `initialization-results.json`):

| blocks | create lookups before |  after (mid/commit)   | create ms before→after | load lookups before→after |
| ------ | --------------------: | :-------------------: | :--------------------: | :-----------------------: |
| 100    |                10,200 |     300 (100/200)     |         30→12          |            0→0            |
| 500    |               251,000 |   1,500 (500/1,000)   |         256→16         |            0→0            |
| 1,000  |             1,002,000 |  3,000 (1,000/2,000)  |         870→33         |            0→0            |
| 5,000  |            25,010,000 | 15,000 (5,000/10,000) |       33,536→128       |            0→0            |

Create is now exactly 3N (N validation reads + 2N commit fold — both required by the contract); load unchanged (1 scan of N, 0 lookups). No equivalent work moved elsewhere: mid-tx fell from N² to N.

**Tests.** New `src/tests/crdt/doc/init-bulk.test.ts` (17): inline probe instrumentation asserting `lookups ≤ 8N` (3N today), repeated same-key writes read correctly mid-transaction, init→facade ops, nested/marks/inline/empty/non-empty/dup-id/concurrent-init/undo coverage, and `insertBlocks` model-level contract incl. sequential-equivalence. `pnpm test:crdt` 1,720 pass; `pnpm test -- --run` 1,718 pass; `pnpm check` + `pnpm test:typecheck` clean; eslint clean on touched files (3 pre-existing prettier violations outside U7 scope remain in dirty worktree files). `pnpm test:dom` 155/157 — 2 failures in untracked `placeholder-repair.test.tsx` + dirty `removeStalePlaceholders.ts` (another unit's in-flight work, unrelated to the CRDT path).

**Residue.** `syncTransaction` still re-folds the full `changed` map per mid-transaction read — quadratic for any OTHER caller that loops model ops in one transaction (`setBlock` children, `moveBlocks`); bounded by the O(changed) `ensurePlacements` rebuild on the same path, so left as-is. The 2N commit fold and N validation reads are the floor for this contract.

## U6 — bounded placeholder repair

**Defect.** Every committed `DocChange` ran `removeStalePlaceholdersIn(edytor.node)` plus `scheduleRemoveStalePlaceholdersIn(edytor.node)` — an immediate full-editor `querySelectorAll` sweep plus an uncoalesced, uncancellable 7-phase chain (immediate → microtask → rAF → nested timeout → 50/250/1000ms): ≈8 global scans per commit, N commits → 8N, nothing cancelled on destroy. `Block.attach` also ran an empty-text-node `MutationObserver` whose `TreeWalker` predicate (`node.parentElement === node`) was unsatisfiable — dead observer work per block mount.

**Fix.** `removeStalePlaceholders.ts` now exports `createPlaceholderRepairQueue` — one coalesced queue per `Edytor` view (`edytor.placeholderRepair`): a pending set of plain roots plus keyed lazy resolvers (`add`/`addKeyed`), one phase chain per burst window (immediate → microtask → rAF → 50/250/1000ms retries). Roots drop when detached or scanning clean; a root a pass actually changed stays queued for re-verification (Svelte can re-render a placeholder after the model settles — the reason the delayed phases are retained). `release()`, called from `Edytor.destroy()`, cancels rAF + timers and dead-mans every pending pass. `removeStalePlaceholdersIn` returns the removed count; `removeStalePlaceholders(text)` keeps its text-parent scope; `scheduleRemoveStalePlaceholders(text)` routes into the view's queue keyed by the wrapper, resolving `text.node.parentElement` lazily so `{#key}` remounts retarget live nodes.

**Scoping.** The facade `onChange` handler (`edytor.svelte.ts` `queuePlaceholderRepair`) queues `block:${id}` resolvers for `change.content`/`moved`/`meta`/`added` ids — resolved through `idToBlock`/`Block.node`, never DOM discovery; a non-incremental `applyMirrorChange` fallback queues the editor root once (affected set unknowable). `refreshDomAfterHistoryChange` dropped its per-text schedule loops; its two immediate root sweeps stay (undo/redo is wholesale) plus one coalesced root entry. `Text.svelte`'s per-placeholder 50/250/1000ms duplicate-cleanup chain folded into the queue; its `MutationObserver` stays — it covers NON-commit staleness (browser DOM text landing without a facade commit). The inert `Block.attach` observer + `TreeWalker` deleted — **1 observer removed**; empty-text anchors pinned surviving (`domTextMutationObserver` preserves them).

**Counters** (chromium `placeholder-repair.spec.ts`, 60-keystroke burst = 60 commits): `{windows:1, passes:3, rootsScanned:7}` vs old ≈8 full-editor sweeps/commit (~480 root qSA). Placeholder-selector qSA total 213 (retained per-text/per-parent local scans); editor-root scans 5 — all from `domTextMutationObserver`'s batched mutation flush (out of scope, not commit-driven). `size` → 0 within the 1000ms retry bound; `release()` freezes `passes`/`rootsScanned` under continued edits.

**Tests.** `src/tests/fixtures/dom/placeholder-repair.test.tsx` 11/11 (jsdom: coalescing, drop-on-clean, remount retarget, release, 60-commit burst, anchor survival). `tests/editor-dom/placeholder-repair.spec.ts` 9/9 chromium: lifecycle, marked-text delete-all, Enter-split+undo, IME cancel/commit, mention separators, blurred programmatic update, real-provider remote edit, keyed remount + release-freeze, burst counters. Lanes: `pnpm test:dom` 157/157; `pnpm test -- --run` 1718 pass/1 skip; chromium placeholder/undo sweep (features, input, composition, composition-cancellation, blurred-programmatic-update, inline-atomic, dom-mutation, history-coalesced-range) 222/222; `pnpm test:dst` 14/14; `check`/`lint`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` clean.

**Limits.** Deferred retries (≤1000ms) retained for the placeholder-vs-empty-text render race; adds landing inside an open window after the fast phases wait for the next deferred pass (bounded). `domTextMutationObserver`'s own placeholder calls are untouched mutation-path repairs. A block emptied to a multi-part shape (mention + empty texts) legitimately renders no placeholder — `shouldShowPlaceholder` requires a sole empty text part; pinned, not changed.

## U0 — instrumentation repair + evidence freeze

**Scope.** `bench/browser.js` rewired onto `bench/lib/browser-hooks.js` (predecessor wrote the hooks but never installed them): `MOUNT_INIT`/`DIAG_INIT` via `addInitScript`, `INSTALL_HOOKS` per page, `VERIFY_RENDERED`/`HEADLESS_DOC_RUN`/`DIAG_WRAP_WRAPPERS` driven, `bench/lib/cpuprofile.js` CDP-Profiler capture wired. Hooks bugs fixed during integration: `flushMirror` double-wrap removed (the `{mark:'mirror'}` wrap never installed → `mirrorMs` was always null), `marks.rafCb` added (rAF's frame timestamp can precede `dom` — spans off it legitimately invert), `bodyMs` depth-gated (nested `doc.transact` joins double-counted), phantom `deriveSelection` wrap dropped. No `src/`/`tests/` changes; bench-app untouched (its `__BENCH_MOUNT__`/`__BENCH__` stamps were already in place).

**Artifacts.** `bench/results/browser-U0-instrumented.json` (= `browser-latest.json`, ~4.6MB; per-sample marks + per-transaction rows preserved) and raw CDP profiles under `.artifacts/u0-traces/` (`profile-{mount,keys}-diag-{small,1k,5k}.json`). Same packed tarball as the U10 baseline (`sha256 418be6e1…`, byte-identical); `src/lib` has since moved on (`abe14f32…` — concurrent edits). Variant dimension frozen as `meta.variant.id='A'` (B=paragraph attribution, C=paragraph+editor fixes reserved for future artifacts).

**Every keystroke commits TWO engine transactions** (24/24 samples, every lane): content tx (`view-command`, ~39B update) + attribution-record tx (`Symbol(edytor.attribution)`, ~110B) born inside the content tx's `beforeObserverCalls` — explicit `parent` link. Undo = `undo-manager` (+ attribution only when attributable content exists); facade-direct ops = `local-untyped`; each remote apply = `remote-apply` + a `local-untyped` formatting-cleanup follow-up (`ynode.js` `_needFormattingCleanup`).

**Keystroke p50 totalMs** (timing lanes): small 5.6 / 1k 7.8 / 5k 62.3 / churned 9.7 / shared-100k-50blocks 12.6 (U10: 7.0/6.7/38.8/8.2/10.9 — see caveats). The old `writeMs` (mixed both txs) is split: at 5k `contentBodyMs` p50 **12.9** vs `attributionBodyMs` **~0.03**, `attributionCommitMs` 0.3; publish 0.2, mirror 0.1, commitTail 0.1 — the attribution commit itself is cheap; its cost is the second 110B update + double event count. **The 5k tail is selection restore, not engine**: `dispatchTailMs` p50 38.2 ≈ `setAtTextOffset#async`×2 = 38.2ms → `setAtNodeOffset` 33.6 → `project`×2 25.2 / `_projectedTree`×12 25.5 / `applySelectionSnapshot`×3 13.6; diag counters: `sel.addRange`×3 = 13.4ms (forced layout over 5k blocks) + `Text.refreshFromProject`×2 = 11.5ms inside `beforeinput`'s 42.2ms async settle; CDP profile: `addRange` self **414.5ms** over 30 keystrokes — the top self-time by far. Commit walk stays O(blocks): `itemsWalked` 10054, `contentItems` 10000 calls/keystroke at 5k, `recomputedBlocks` 1.

**Mount decomposition** (page-clock): 5k = init→module-eval 35ms → `mount()` call 19.5s (ctor+view inseparable consumer-side) → all blocks +129ms; verified 5000/5000 blocks, 300k/300k joined chars. Headless `createDocument` 33.7s / `encode` 35.8ms·1.61MB / `loadDocument` 56.5ms at 5k. Post-GC retained heap: 4.4MB / 38.4MB / 173MB (small/1k/5k) via `--expose-gc` + unrounded `--enable-precise-memory-info` — not comparable to bucketed baselines.

**Caveats.** (1) Run under concurrent load (another playwright suite + vite dev live) — 5k headline ms inflated vs U10; compare stages/counters, not totals. (2) diag-5k profiler window offset bound ±164.7ms under load — per-window tops indicative, profile totals reliable. (3) `dcl` reads 0 on every lane (`domContentLoadedEventEnd` not populated for this navigation — recorded raw). (4) `bodyMs` is null for engine-internal transactions (applyUpdate, formatting cleanup) — they never pass `doc.transact`. (5) `counters.ms` is call-inclusive — nested `ed.transact` joins re-count shared wall time. (6) `bodyCalls`>1 on a tx = `doc.transact` joins into one open transaction — the two-commit-per-keystroke shape is about engine TRANSACTIONS, not transact calls.

## U5 — scoped text refresh

**Defect.** `Text.refreshFromProject` read `edytor.projectedBlock(blockId)` → `_projectedTree()` → `facade.project()` + a whole-tree index walk, and `segStart` did the same via `Block.partOffsetOf` → `projectedParts()`. `facade.version` invalidates the memoized tree on every write, so each keystroke re-projected the document ~2× per text wrapper — **19 `project()` calls per 10 keystrokes** (measured by replaying the old path under a wrapped `facade.project`: `partOffsetOf` + `projectedBlock` per `insertAt`; identical 19 at 100/1k/5k blocks, O(document) each).

**Fix.** Both reads now scope to the block (`src/lib/text/text.svelte.ts`): `refreshFromProject` runs `deriveContentParts(facade.contentItems(blockId))` — the maintained, transaction-aware runs view (same source `project()` itself uses per block) — and selects the text part at `_segOrd`, unchanged indexing. `segStart` reimplements the `partOffsetOf` text branch over the same scoped parts (segOrd lookup → mirror-position fallback → plain `content` walk). `contentItems` emits `[]` for absent AND empty blocks, so the refresh gates on a new facade oracle `isVisibleBlock(id)` (`edytor-doc.ts`): `positionInView`'s ancestor walk minus sibling-index materialization — O(depth), the exact `projectedBlock → null` verdict (hidden/deleted keep last-known `_items`; the pending reconcile kills the wrapper). `hasBlock`/`contentItems`/`isVisibleBlock` are the only facade additions; `DocBlock` (`nodes.ts`) consumes the same surface. No borrowed engine values cross — `runsView` publishes interned/frozen payloads.

**Counters** (wrapped `facade.project`/`flushMirror`, warmed tree): single `insertAt` at 100/1k/**5k** blocks → **project 19→0 per 10 keystrokes** (0 even at 5k), `flushMirror` 1/commit all incremental `applyMirrorChange`, **full fallback 0**; unrelated sibling `_items` identity unchanged (no `reconcileContent` on untouched blocks). Fallback retained for remount/remote/undo/structural recovery; full tree unchanged for `projectedBlock`/`projectedChildren`/`isVisibleBlockId`/selection.

**Tests.** `src/tests/scoped-text-refresh.test.tsx` 13/13 — project()=0 at 100/1k/5k, sequential insert/delete/format, multi-op transactions, mid-transaction read-your-writes, `_segOrd` across inline atoms (scoped `segStart` ≡ `partOffsetOf` oracle), marks (attribution-split runs compared via `stripAttribution`), empty-block `[]` ≠ absent, hidden/deleted and merged-away mid-edit parity, split/merge ownership aftermath, `isVisibleBlock` ≡ `positionOf` across move/delete. Lanes: `pnpm test -- --run` 1 731 pass; `pnpm test:dom` 157/157; `pnpm test:crdt` 1 720 pass; `check`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` 0 errors; prettier+eslint clean on touched files (3 pre-existing `bench/**` prettier violations outside scope remain).

**Unsafe-to-scope paths.** None found — `partOffsetOf` itself stays full-tree for `InlineBlock` callers (atom-id lookup still needs `projectedParts`); only the text branch was replicated inside `segStart`. `displayLength`/`atomOffsetOfPartIndex` still use `projectedParts` — block-level reads, not on the per-keystroke text path.

## U1 — durable per-block attribution on the existing owners (attribution v2 stream, 2026-09-22)

**Scope.** Compact, durable, per-BLOCK attribution (`{createdBy, contributors, lastChangedBy}` keyed by `DocumentActor.id`, never replica clientIDs) integrated into the existing owners — placement model, text ownership, maintained runs, `EdytorDoc` facade, `EdytorDocument` lifecycle. The metadata commits INSIDE the user transaction (one update total per keystroke — no observer-driven follow-up transaction). The U6 `a/` per-content capture pipeline stays in place and untouched in role (removal is U2's scope); this unit's records use a disjoint namespace so the two coexist without aliasing.

**What landed**:

- **`src/lib/crdt/attribution/block.ts`** (new) — `ActorId`/`BlockAttribution` types; `BLOCK_ATTR_ROOT = 'blockattr'` replicated root (registered in `SCHEMA.roots.blockAttribution`) holding `b/<blockId>` record nodes (`c` = createdBy, `k/<actorId>` = true contributor membership); `LAST_CHANGED_ATTR = 'l'` (`SCHEMA.blockAttrs.lastChanged`) on the block node itself. `blockAttributionOf(doc,id)` is the O(1) read; `bindBlockAttribution(Y)` provides the bound write helpers (`ensureRecord`, `stampChange`, `stampCreated` with optional contributor-inherit, `unionContributors`).
- **Storage split is the undo contract**: `createdBy`/`contributors` are monotonic (add-only union — converge by construction, MUST NOT be restored by undo) → they live on the `blockattr` root, OUTSIDE the registry-scoped `UndoManager`. `lastChangedBy` must restore on undo (undo alice's only edit → `l` reverts to the previous author) → it lives as an LWW attr on the block node INSIDE undo scope; the engine's item-level restore handles it for free.
- **Suppression**: `l` writes are suppressed when THIS replica already stamped this actor AND the stored value still matches — a per-replica `WeakMap<doc, Map>` keyed on actor, NOT stored value alone: the same-actor two-replica counterexample (A edits, same-actor B edits after receiving, A undoes → `l` must still resolve `alice` via B's surviving item) requires B to write its own `l` item even though the value reads identical. Contributor adds are value-suppressed (`k/<actor>` written only when absent — union semantics make it trivially safe).
- **`edytor-doc.ts`** — `SCHEMA.roots.blockAttribution` + `SCHEMA.blockAttrs.lastChanged` entries; `EdytorDocConfig.actor?: () => AttributionActor | undefined` (lazy — the document passes `() => this.actor` before the field is assigned); `init` accepts `author` — user-supplied initial content is stamped per spec-tree, the auto-created bootstrap block gets an EMPTY `b/` record (union-safe shared node, reads as unattributed), bare `init()`/import/hydration pass no author → zero stamps. Stamping is op-level inside the op's own `doc.transact`: `insertBlock`/`insertSubtree`/`setBlock` children/`duplicateBlock` → `stampCreated` on every spec-tree block; `splitBlock` → tail authored by splitter + INHERITS the source's contributor set read BEFORE the splitter's own stamp lands (literal inherit — splitter carried by `createdBy`/`lastChangedBy`, not a contributor add); `mergeBlocks`/`mergeUnnesting` → survivor keeps `createdBy`, unions the absorbed block's contributors, records the merger (island-reset children stamped individually); content ops (`insertText`, `deleteText`, `setMark`, `unsetMark`, `formatRange`, `insertInline`, `removeInline`, `setInlineData`, `setBlock` content) → `stampChange` on the owning block. Pure moves (`moveBlock`/`moveBlocks`/`nestBlock`/`unNestBlock`) write nothing — parents are not marked for child-order changes; `deleteBlock` writes nothing (records persist by block id).
- **Semantic no-op suppression at the op layer** (the engine's booleans are insufficient): `insertText('')`, `deleteText` zero-clamped ranges, same-value `setBlockType`/`setBlockData` (full write suppressed via `jsonEquals` — no item churn either), same-value `setInlineData` (payload compared against the run's `data` — model write skipped entirely), and same-value mark ops via `marksAlready` — a folded-mark-state scan over the live `RangeCursor` pieces (covers inline atoms; mirrors `insertFormats`' `currentFormats.get(k) ?? null`) evaluated BEFORE the model write, since `formatRangeIn`/`text.format` already emit zero items when every atom satisfies the marks.
- **`document.ts`** — the facade gets `actor: () => this.actor`; `sync()`'s fresh-seed path passes `author: this.actor` (user initial content authored by the document actor); the hydrated/`loadDocument`/`attachDocument` paths pass no author — remote/restored records read verbatim, never relabeled. `attribution.block(id)` surfaces on `DocumentAttribution` (`attribution.ts`) and `DocBlock.attribution` (`nodes.ts`).
- **Coexistence**: `runs.ts` `facetOf` maps `l` → `'ignore'` (attribution bookkeeping carries no derived state; must not dirty runs or poison the claim-refinement fast path); `attribution.ts` `isCapturable` now also treats `blockattr`-only commits as metadata-only (previously only the `attribution` root itself was excluded) — unreachable via the facade but the namespace boundary is explicit. Old `a/` capture, `c/`/`u/` actor dictionary, and per-content run attribution are unchanged and still green.

**Tests** — `src/tests/crdt/attribution/block-attribution.test.ts` (21): initial-value authorship, bootstrap unattributed, spec-tree authoring, second-actor contributor union + `lastChangedBy`, inline/mark/metadata ops, same-value suppression (marks, inline data, type/data — zero-commit assertions), setBlock/DocBlock surface parity, pure-move/nest zero `b/`/`l` writes, split inherit + source touch, merge union, duplicate authorship, delete persistence, ONE-commit-per-keystroke, steady-state zero attribution bytes, compound `document.transact` single commit, remote never-relabels, `loadDocument` verbatim, actorless facades, bare `init()` no-stamp. `src/tests/crdt/attribution/block-undo.test.ts` (5): the same-actor two-replica counterexample, undo restores previous `lastChangedBy`, contributor monotonicity across undo of the only contributing edit, undo of block creation removes the block while its `b/` record persists (outside undo scope), redo replays the creation attribution.

**Checks run**: `pnpm test:crdt` **1747 pass / 0 fail** (88 files; incl. vendored upstream + U6 capture/durability/overhead/renumbering/undo unchanged); `pnpm test` **1757 pass / 1 fail** — the failure is `src/tests/gate3/app-context.test.tsx` listener-retention (expected 10 awareness listeners over 5 editors, got 5): concurrent-stream `edytor.svelte.ts` dropped the view's `awareness.on('update')` (U8a, 'change'-only) at 14:44 mid-session while the untouched test still expects two per view — outside this unit's file boundary, flagged for the concurrent stream; `pnpm check` (svelte-check) **0/0**; eslint clean on touched files; prettier applied. No commit — tree stays dirty per unit policy.

**Residuals / deviations**:

- `b/<id>` records are LWW map entries on the root — concurrent FIRST touches of a record-less block (bootstrap scaffolding and foreign/migrated docs legitimately lack records) can lose one contributor add; convergent, self-heals on the loser's next touch. Documented in the module header.
- `l`-only or `blockattr`-only commits never occur via the facade (stamps always ride inside a content commit), so the capture-path namespace guard is belt-and-braces.
- `formatRange`/`setMark`/`unsetMark` still EXECUTE the engine call on a same-value range (it writes nothing but may garbage-collect a redundant marker) — only the stamp is suppressed; skipping the call would change baseline cleanup behavior.
- `undo` of an `l` stamp on a block whose `b/` record was created in the SAME commit is consistent (record outside scope, `l` inside); `undo` of block creation deletes the block node — `blockAttributionOf` on a dead id still reads the surviving `b/` record (durable by contract).

## U8a — view fanout: selection/awareness/value (2026-09-22)

**Scope.** The ~38ms post-commit tail at 5k was selection fanout, not engine work (U0 evidence: `project()`×2 ≈ 25ms inside restore, `addRange`×3 ≈ 13.4ms self-time). Five dedupe/scoping fixes, all preserving observable semantics:

- **Scoped anchor resolution** (`selection.svelte.ts` `resolveTextAnchor`): was `edytor.projectedBlock(blockId)` → `facade.project()` + whole-tree index walk per endpoint. Now `facade.resolveAnchor` → `isVisibleBlock` (the exact `projectedBlock → null` oracle — O(depth) ancestor walk, no sibling-index materialization) → `contentItems` + `Block.deriveContentParts` → display-offset→`segOrd` map → live `Text` wrapper, with the old wrapper-content walk retained as fallback. Same pattern as U5's `refreshFromProject`.
- **Native selection write dedupe** (`domSelection.ts` + all five setters + `onBeforeInput.ts` target-range sync): new `domSelectionIsCollapsedAt`/`domSelectionCoversRange` compare the live `Selection` (endpoints + direction, `rangeCount ≤ 1` guard preserves Firefox multi-range) before any `removeAllRanges`/`addRange`/`setBaseAndExtent`. Model state is still derived/applied every call — only the DOM write is skipped when identical. `beforeinput` collapsed-delete special-casing unchanged.
- **Semantic emit dedupe**: `emitSelectionChange` keys on the full semantic state (text ids, y-offsets, collapsed/reversed, block ids, void/island flags, blocks length, content, marks, bound anchor atoms — DOM references excluded so fresh snapshots of the same position don't re-emit). `applySelectionSnapshot` still runs per `selectionchange`; only the downstream publish+notify is suppressed. `setCollapsedStateAtTextOffset`'s direct notify deliberately does NOT touch the emit key — the next `emitSelectionChange` must still carry the awareness publish.
- **Awareness publish dedupe** (`awarenessSelection.ts`): `publishedEntryEquals` compares the per-view payload structurally (text ids, offsets, collapsed/reversed, anchors by `{b, i:{c,k}, a}` — not JSON-key-order); unchanged payloads keep the existing entry and its `t` stamp, no `setLocalState`. `clearAwarenessSelection` likewise skips when the destroyed-view sweep is a no-op; a serialized-fields final guard covers torn sweeps.
- **Subscription + geometry split**: `RemoteSelections.svelte` and `Edytor` now subscribe to awareness `'change'` only (fires on add/remove/deep-change; `'update'` additionally fires on clock-only heartbeats — vendored semantics verified). `getRenderedRemoteSelections` resolves candidates first and reads NO geometry (`editor.getBoundingClientRect` included) when there are no renderable remote selections.
- **Endpoint lookups parallelized**: snapshot restore resolves start/end via `Promise.all` instead of serial awaits.

**Counters** (deterministic, `bench/results/browser-U8a-{before,after-clean,after-full}.json`; keystroke = per keystroke, undo = per undo step):

- diag-1k keystroke: `sel.addRange` **3→1**, `sel.removeAllRanges` **3→1**, `dom.createRange` 3→2, `geom.getBoundingClientRect` **3→0**; `contentItems` 24→16.
- diag-5k keystroke: identical call deltas; `addRange` ms 13.9→13.9 — the ONE surviving write is the real caret move and still pays Chromium's forced selection layout (the remaining tail: `setAtNodeOffset` ≈ that single write).
- undo @5k: **`project` 1→0, `_projectedTree` 1→0, `contentItems` 5010→12**, `resolveTextAnchor` 0→1 + `resolveAnchor`/`isVisibleBlock`/`anchorAt` on the scoped path; `restoreRelativePosition` 7.6→~0ms; undo `totalMs` 15.4→9.7. (undo @1k: `contentItems` 1016→12, `applySelectionSnapshot` 2→1.)
- awareness: `setLocalState` 1/keystroke (was re-published per emit); `awareness.emit` 2 (`change`+`update` from the single real publish).
- diag-peer-1k: remote geometry confirmed live with a peer — 4 `getBoundingClientRect` + 2 `getClientRects` + 6 `createRange`; `resolveTextAnchor` 0 (synthetic peer publishes legacy textId selections — anchor path covered by undo lane + presence tests).
- consumer lanes @5k (new): `consumer-onchange-5k` p50 52.5ms — `ed.value` **1 call = 23.2ms, 639,602 bytes** serialized in `publishMs`; `consumer-derived-5k` p50 63.7ms — `ed.value` 2 calls/1 miss (getter cache hit), recompute in `flushMs`. Full-value consumers are now measured explicitly: one `ed.value` read ≈ 23ms/keystroke at 5k.
- newly visible (preexisting, not a regression): `JSON.stringify` ~304/keystroke ≈ 0.1ms — `Text.svelte` per-element marks render-key.

**Timing caveat.** Cross-run medians are not clean (before machine faster: mount 706→1174ms; an earlier after-run under load read 5k p50 33.2ms vs 22.6 clean). Same-run p50: 1k 5.7→5.8, 5k 20.1→22.6 — headline ms unchanged because the single remaining `addRange` dominates; the win is 2/3 fewer native writes, zero stray geometry, and the eliminated restore-time projection.

**Tests.** New: `src/tests/fixtures/dom/selection-write-dedupe.test.tsx` (5 — collapsed/directional/multi-range DOM-write dedupe helpers), `src/tests/crdt/document/presence.test.ts` (25 — publish dedupe, per-view entries, legacy mirror, destroyed-view sweep, sibling preservation). Updated: `gate3/app-context.test.tsx` listener count 10→5 (one `'change'` sub per view — the fix for the failure U1's run flagged). Lanes: `pnpm test -- --run` **1762 pass/0 fail** (104 files); `pnpm test:dom` **162/162**; presence under `vitest.crdt.config` 25/25; `pnpm check`/`test:typecheck`/`test:dom:typecheck` **0 errors**; `pnpm lint` clean (fixed preexisting zero-width-space literal in `bench/lib/browser-hooks.js` + prettier on `bench/lib/cpuprofile.js`); packed consumer rebuilt (`pnpm package` + `pnpm pack`) — `smoke.js` + `smoke-svelte.mjs` pass (two-view shared doc, attachSync, encode/load, mount/edit/teardown). No commit — tree stays dirty per policy.

**Residuals.** (1) One layout-forcing `addRange` per real caret move is irreducible at this API; skipping it when focus is elsewhere is a future policy question, not this unit. (2) `emitSelectionKey` runs `JSON.stringify(currentMarks)` per emit — ~µs, acceptable. (3) The direct-notify path in `setCollapsedStateAtTextOffset` can still double-notify a consumer before the deduped emit — preexisting, emit-amplification only, never a missed notification. (4) `resolveTextAnchor`'s wrapper-content fallback remains for scoped-data misses (remote/hidden edge cases). (5) `selection.utils.ts` carries a concurrent stream's UTF-16 surrogate-boundary helper — not this unit's change, left intact.

## U2 — retire the per-edit attribution pipeline; legacy records become inert compatibility state (attribution v2 stream, 2026-09-22)

**Scope.** Remove the U6 `a/` capture pipeline wholesale — the `beforeObserverCalls` observer, the immutable `a/<nonce>/<seq>` ContentMap record writes, the `ATTRIBUTION_ORIGIN` follow-up transaction per commit, and the U7 attributed-run projection (`RunAttribution`, authorship-boundary splitting, per-run `attribution` fields). Durable attribution is now ONLY the U1 block model (`b/<blockId>` records + `l` stamp, inside the owning transaction). **Ordinary editing writes no per-edit attribution: one update per keystroke, zero `a/` records, no hidden log.**

**What landed**:

- **`attribution/attribution.ts`** — rewritten 580→~210 lines as a read-through compatibility service. Keeps: the `u/`/`c/` actor dictionary (published once per attach under `ATTRIBUTION_ORIGIN`, `actorOf`/`actors`/`setProfile` unchanged), `ATTRIBUTION_ROOT`/`ATTRIBUTION_ORIGIN` vocabulary, and `block(id)` (U1 read). Adds `legacy()` — decodes every pre-existing `a/` record with `Y.decodeContentMap` and merges into `{inserts, deletes}` IdMaps (`createContentMap` shape, feedable to the engine's `createAttributionsRenderer`); returns `null` when no legacy state exists; malformed records skip rather than poison the merge. Removes: the transaction listener, `isCapturable`, all capture state. No `doc.on` anywhere — `destroy()` only gates `setProfile`.
- **`text/runs.ts`** — the attributed projection (U7) deleted: no `RunAttribution`/`AttributionSource`/`RunsAttachOptions`, no attribution observer or invalidation path, no marker rows, no authorship-boundary splitting. `runs()`/`contentItems()`/`contentJSON()` emit plain `{text, marks}`/`{id, type, data}` — adjacent same-mark text from different authors merges into ONE run.
- **`edytor-doc.ts`/`document.ts`** — runs attach loses the attribution source wiring; `SCHEMA.roots.attribution` stays reserved (old state must round-trip); attach/destroy lifecycle for the dictionary service retained.
- **`utils/json.ts`/`text/deltas.ts`/`text.svelte.ts`/`block.svelte.ts`** — `attribution` fields removed from `JSONText`/`JSONInlineBlock`/`TextRun`/`InlineRun`/`JSONAttribution`; nothing propagates authorship onto ordinary content.
- **Legacy durability is untouched**: `a/`/`c/`/`u/` records are plain replicated state — `encode()`/`loadDocument`/sync carry them verbatim; remote replicas running pre-U2 code merge cleanly (their new `a/` records append; our reads stay total).
- **Bench/docs**: `bench/u10-attribution-overhead.mjs` cross-check now asserts `a/` count = 0 + reports the `blockattr` footprint; `bench/browser.js` + `browser-hooks.js` notes updated (`attribution-record` origin now only matches dictionary publishes; `attribution*` span fields report `null` for content ops; variant id → `B`); `.artifacts/attribution-sizing/probe.mjs` rewritten onto the real implementation (`none` vs `block` lanes — the old hand-rolled `paragraph` simulation is superseded) with hard asserts: `updateEvents === commits` on every lane, `a/` count = 0, post-reload `b/`/`l` reads; `docs/crdt-v14-document.md` attribution section rewritten (block reads + `legacy()` + explicit "no per-edit attribution / no run authorship fields"); README blurb updated.

**Sizing probe** (`node .artifacts/attribution-sizing/probe.mjs`, 200 appends, results in `results.json`): every lane emits exactly **200 update events — one per keystroke**, **0 `a/` records**; encode ratio vs no-attribution lane: 1.313 (one writer) / 1.537 (two writers) / 2.532 (alternating writers — the `l` LWW flip writes an attr item + tombstone per keystroke, ~24B/update; the U1-documented lastChangedBy cost, not per-edit capture).

**Tests.** `src/tests/crdt/attribution/removal.test.ts` (6): the headline proof — 11 consecutive keystrokes → **exactly 11 update events, zero `a/` records**; bare-facade ops one-update-each; two actors typing one block → runs merge to a single `{text}` run with NO `attribution` key anywhere in `runs`/`contentItems`/`project`/`toJSON`/`contentJSON`, while `attribution.block(id)` still resolves `{createdBy, contributors:{alice,bob}, lastChangedBy:'bob'}`; undo/redo one update per step; dictionary reads + `setProfile` without capture; encode→load stays `a/`-clean. `durability.test.ts` (7): legacy `a/` bytes verbatim through encode/load, `legacy()` merged inserts/deletes, new edits on restored docs append nothing, provider hydration preserves records, mixed old/new convergence both directions, metadata-first torn delivery, delete-side ranges surviving payload GC. Block-attribution suites unchanged and green (21 + 5). `helpers.ts` rewritten as a legacy-record seeder/reader. `docs-examples.test.ts`, `mirror-incremental.test.tsx`, `packed-consumer/smoke*.ts`/`smoke.js`, `test.utils.ts` (`stripAttribution` → segmentation-merge canonicalizer), `tests/editor-dom/helpers.ts` updated to the plain-run contract.

**Checks run**: `pnpm test -- --run` **1723 pass / 0 fail / 1 skip** (100 files); `pnpm test:crdt` **1712 pass** (84 files); `pnpm check` **0 errors**; `pnpm test:typecheck` **0 errors** (fixed the `stripAttribution` generic constraint — `JSONText` no longer carries `attribution`); `pnpm lint` clean (prettier reformatted touched files). Probe + bench scripts run clean. No commit — tree stays dirty per unit policy.

**Residuals / deviations**:

- `legacy()` decodes lazily per call (O(records) — intended for history tooling, not the hot path; the ordinary runs surface never consults it).
- Tombstoned `l` predecessors GC asymmetrically between writer/receiver replicas (standard Yjs content-GC of deleted items) — encoded byte sizes can differ by ~7B/overwrite across peers while live state converges; the probe asserts snapshot equality, not byte equality.
- Old replicas still emitting `a/` records are inert to us: preserved, decodable via `legacy()`, never projected onto runs. A future GC policy for `a/` retention is a separate unit — records key content ids, so payload GC leaves them dangling-readable (delete-side coverage pinned in durability tests).
- `ATTRIBUTION_ORIGIN` still marks dictionary publishes (attach + `setProfile`) — one small transaction per attach, not per edit.

### Test inventory

`u3-semantics.test.ts` (17): 3× local undo/redo (incl. cross-actor interleaved chain); 2× remote updates (verbatim stamps, per-replica suppression memory); 2× multi-view; 1× three-replica same-actor; 2× remote-vs-history; 1× concurrent first-touch merge; 2× upgrade; 4× admission gates.

`u3-persistence.test.ts` (6): 1× encode→load round-trip (multi-block, multi-actor, deleted block, `l` attrs byte-faithful); 2× IndexedDB hydration incl. compacted snapshot and blockattr-only rows; 2× live provider sync (two `IndexeddbPersistence` over BroadcastChannel; `attachSync(createIndexeddbSync)` on a pending doc); 1× `createWebsocketSync` over an opaque in-memory relay, attribution both directions.

### Semantic findings (observed, pinned by tests — not defects)

1. **Undo resurrects overwritten `l` items as clones.** `setAttr` keeps every `l` item in the `_map` chain (overwrite deletes the loser); undo of an overwrite resurrects the predecessor item via `redoItem` clone. So `undo` reliably restores the _prior author_, and after undoing all same-actor edits `l` still reads that actor — every resurrected item carries the same value. Deterministic, convergent.
2. **Interleaved cross-replica redo resolves `l` by item order, not chronology.** Chain `A.insert → B.edit → B.undo → A.undo → A.redo → B.redo` leaves two live `l` clones (one per actor); the winner is whichever clone's item-id outranks — client-ID dependent, deterministic per merged state, convergent, always a real contributor. The contract ("`l` = deterministic LWW resolution, no wall-clock claims") holds; the test pins convergence + actor-validity rather than a specific winner.
3. **Redo of an op whose ancestor subtree was itself redone by another replica is a no-op for the re-anchored items.** B's redone text/`l` reparent to the pre-redo (now-deleted) item ids and stay invisible — vendored `UndoManager.redoItem` behavior, convergent on both replicas, no corruption. Pinned as the observed boundary in the cross-actor chain test.
4. **First-touch `b/` race residual (known from U1) confirmed in-place**: two replicas concurrently first-touching a record-less block can drop one `k/` add on the LWW map entry; the union self-heals on the loser's next touch and never corrupts. Test asserts all three outcomes (both-kept, one-lost-then-healed, deterministic convergence).
5. **Dictionary delivery caveat (test-harness level)**: a replica's `c/<clientID>` actor binding is published once at `attach`; peers that wired _after_ that txn need a state-sync (provider SyncStep2 or `applyUpdate(encode())`) to resolve `actorOf(clientID)`. Wire docs first, or handshake — production providers do this at connect.

### Lane results

- `pnpm test:crdt` on the settled tree (16:21 local, ~40 s): **87 files passed, 1 skipped (88); 1738 tests passed, 7 skipped (1745); 0 failures.** Includes `u3-semantics` 17/17 and `u3-persistence` 6/6, plus all pre-existing attribution suites (`block-attribution` 21, `block-undo` 5, `durability` 7, `removal` 6). `pnpm test:typecheck` clean (0 errors, 0 warnings); eslint + prettier clean on owned files.
- An earlier lane attempt (16:18 local) caught concurrent agents mid-refactor of `src/lib/crdt/{placement/model.ts,edytor-doc.ts,text/runs.ts}` (`R.modelState is not a function`, `view.modelCtx is not a function`, `extentOf` arity flip) — 608 tests across 54 files failed in that window, including every suite that constructs an `EdytorDocument`. All failures cleared once the in-flight edits landed; none were attribution semantics.
- Focused runs: 23/23 green across multiple invocations.

## U4 — Requalify size and update amplification

Status: **complete** (measurement-only unit; no source edits). Fragment for merge into `docs/archive/crdt-v14-execution-ledger.md`.

### Ledger-row material

| Unit | Status   | Owned files                                                                                 | Outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| U4   | complete | `.artifacts/attribution-sizing/{u4-probe.mjs,u4-results.json,u4-results.md}`, this fragment | Matched-workload rerun of the frozen pre-U1/U2 baseline (`bench/u10-attribution-overhead.mjs` numbers): **200 one-char commits now produce 200 transactions / 200 update events / 201 IDB rows on BOTH lanes** — the per-keystroke attribution follow-up transaction is gone. Encode amplification on the identical microdoc: **28.81× → 1.313×** (512→14,752 B was `a/` record-per-edit; now 476→625 B). Update stream: **3.92× → 1.003×** (19,458 B/400 ev → 3,777 B/200 ev; scaled lanes byte-identical). Scaled honesty lanes (100 ¶ ×61 chars): **1.262×** spread / **1.278×** concentrated — attribution footprint is O(blocks), all cost at creation; same-actor typing adds zero stream bytes. | `node .artifacts/attribution-sizing/u4-probe.mjs` — deterministic (fixed clientID 1234567890; two runs byte-identical); hard asserts per lane: tx=events=local=200, `a/`=0, IDB rows=201, post-reload `b/`+`l` read-back |

### Numbers

Frozen baseline (pre-U1/U2 run, ledger ~L431) vs this run — identical fixture (`b0`/'x' + 200 × `insertText(b0, 1+i, 'a')`), identical IDB harness:

| metric                      |        old OFF |    old ON (text attr) |     new `none` |        new `block` |
| --------------------------- | -------------: | --------------------: | -------------: | -----------------: |
| `encode()`                  |          512 B | 14,752 B (**28.81×**) |          476 B | 625 B (**1.313×**) |
| update stream               | 4,965 B/200 ev |   19,458 B/**400 ev** | 3,765 B/200 ev |     3,777 B/200 ev |
| IDB `updates` rows / stored |  201 / 5,265 B |        401 / 19,836 B |  201 / 4,032 B |      201 / 4,193 B |

Scaled lanes (new model only; see `u4-results.md` for the full table):

- `spread-100` (round-robin, 2 keys/block): none 31,094 B → block 39,254 B = **1.262×**; streams identical at 3,999 B/200 ev; 100 `b/` records, all written at seed.
- `concentrated-100` (all 200 commits in b50): none 29,305 B → block 37,465 B = **1.278×**; streams identical at 4,000 B; footprint still 100 records (seeding stamps every authored block).

Growth decomposition: `encode − initial` is identical per lane pair (micro +209 B, spread +1,999 B, concentrated +210 B) — **every attribution byte is front-loaded at block creation** (~81.6 B/block on the 100-¶ doc incl. one-time actor dict; +149 B on the microdoc). `k/<actor>` contributor adds and the `l` stamp are suppressed after first initialization, so same-actor commits emit no attribution bytes at all.

### Method notes / caveats

- Lanes: `none` = bare `bindEdytorDoc` facade (`E.init` + `E.create` + `insertText`, no actor → zero attribution writes, `edytor-doc.ts` ~L1124); `block` = `bindDocument.createDocument({value, actor:alice})` + `document.transact(insertText)` — the supported document API, stamps inside the edit tx. Same lane construction as the frozen baseline's OFF/ON pair (no attribution opt-out exists).
- The `block − none` delta includes document scaffolding (actor dictionary `u/`+`c/`, `blockattr` root), not only per-block records — inherent to the supported-API comparison.
- IDB accounting verified: provider writes 1 snapshot row at attach + 1 row per update; stored bytes = `initial encode + Σ update payloads` exactly (e.g. spread/block 37,255 + 3,999 = 41,254).
- `beforeTransaction` is attached after `whenSynced`: hydration runs one empty provider-origin `transact` even on a fresh store — excluded from the commit window so "transactions per keystroke" is exact.
- Baseline drift recorded, not normalized: `none` lane reads 476 B enc / 3,765 B stream vs frozen OFF 512 B / 4,965 B on the identical code path (post-U1–U7 engine/facade state + fixed vs random clientID varint widths). All ratio conclusions use same-tree lane pairs.
- Not re-measured here (different workload, already covered by U2's probe): two-writer alternation flips `l` per keystroke → 2.53× encode on the microdoc — a per-actor-switch LWW cost (~24 B/update), honest and bounded, not per-keystroke record growth.

- vendor `yjs/src` sha256 `96dae66287b45d048e4fd017b4f2e2e30c66f3c6209910c8540ba3b5943e01c1` (33 files — matches U0's browser artifact)
- probe file sha256 `c04b08edc1d9bf68a3412fb0fafe3c0e87f65e63d40f78953e1e6b0c162b31b9`

### Handoff-contract check (U4 "Success" bullets)

- No new per-edit text records: `a/` count asserted 0 on every lane. ✓
- No attribution-added update/IDB row for ordinary typing: 200 events / 201 rows on both lanes (was 400/401). ✓
- No attribution metadata growth during repeated same-actor edits: commit-window encoded growth identical to unattributed lane on all three fixtures. ✓
- Honest reporting: absolute bytes + microdoc (1.313×) and 100-block (1.26–1.28×) ratios both reported; actor-alternation cost disclosed via U2's numbers rather than hidden. ✓

## U8b — Shared-backing keystroke fanout (browser `shared-100k-50blocks` anomaly)

Status: **complete** (fix landed — bounded narrowing of `content`-facet
invalidation). Fragment for merge into `docs/archive/crdt-v14-execution-ledger.md`.

### Ledger-row material

| Unit | Status   | Owned files                                                                          | Outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Evidence                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| U8b  | complete | `src/lib/crdt/text/runs.ts`, `src/tests/crdt/runs/u8b-fanout.test.ts`, this fragment | Hypothesis **confirmed**: a keystroke on one sibling of the shared-backing fixture invalidated all 50 consumers of the backing text (50 run recomputes / 50 commit-content roots / ~1.1ms in Node — matching the ~10.9ms browser lane at browser-CPU + render overhead). Fix: transaction-derived content extents are intersected with the text's ownership-interval row, so only owners of spans that actually changed recompute → **1 recompute / 2 commit roots / ~0.18ms**, vs **1 / 1 / ~0.05ms** on the separate-backings baseline. Format ops still fan out 50-wide — their commits arrive with an EMPTY `changed` map (mark churn writes no sequence items), which already takes the conservative all-cache path; that cost is inherent to today's event shape, not sibling invalidation. | `npx vitest run src/tests/crdt/runs/u8b-fanout.test.ts` — counters printed per lane; `pnpm test:crdt` / `pnpm test -- --run` / `pnpm test:dom` / `pnpm check` all green |

### Numbers (same-tree before/after — `resolveNarrowed` toggled to all-consumers)

`src/tests/crdt/runs/u8b-fanout.test.ts` replicates the browser fixture in
Node: `b0` with ~100k formatted chars, 49 sequential `splitBlock` calls → 50
siblings of ~2k chars each on ONE backing `Y.Text`; keystrokes target `s25`,
format samples target `s10`. Counters per keystroke (p50 of 24 ops):

| lane                        | metric                                          | before (all-consumers) |    after (narrowed) |
| --------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------: | ------------------: |
| shared-100k-50blocks        | keystroke p50                                   |           **1.100 ms** |        **0.179 ms** |
|                             | run recomputes (`_items` rebuilds)              |                     50 |               **1** |
|                             | itemsWalked                                     |                  2,419 |              **36** |
|                             | commitContent roots                             |                     50 |               **2** |
|                             | `DocChange.content` (Text wrappers re-rendered) |                      1 |               **1** |
| separate-50x2k (baseline)   | keystroke p50                                   |               0.053 ms |            0.045 ms |
|                             | recomputes / walked / commit / rendered         |         1 / 32 / 1 / 1 |      1 / 32 / 1 / 1 |
| shared formatRange on `s10` | p50                                             |               1.204 ms |            1.405 ms |
|                             | recomputes / walked / commit / rendered         |    50 / 2,416 / 50 / 1 | 50 / 2,416 / 50 / 1 |

Notes on the counters:

- `docChangeContent` was **already 1** before the fix — the wasted 49 sibling
  recomputes produced identical run arrays, so the DocChange diff suppressed
  them downstream. The browser's ~10.9 ms therefore reflects recompute cost
  plus per-invalidated-block scheduling, not 50 actual re-renders.
- `commitContent 2` on the shared lane is `{b0, s25}`: the backing-text holder
  plus the owner of the touched interval. This is the residual shared-backing
  cost — honest, bounded, and not collapsible without deeper surgery.
- Format ops (`formatRange`) show `changed map: []` — mark changes on the
  vendored engine emit no `changed` entries, so the commit falls into the
  `changed.size === 0` conservative branch (all cached blocks dirtied). The
  50-wide fanout there is inherent to the event shape, not to sibling
  semantics — deliberately left untouched.

### What landed (bounded fix)

`src/lib/crdt/text/runs.ts`:

1. **`makeExtentIndex(tr)`** (~L524): lazily walks the transaction's
   `insertSet`/`deleteSet` ranges, resolves each covered countable item to a
   text span via `resolveItemSpan`, and buckets spans by parent node.
   `null`/`undefined` buckets and opaque walks (missing client structs,
   non-countable/parentSub items) all degrade to "no hint" → conservative.
2. **Hint plumbing in `applyTypeChange`** (~L1529): a sequence edit
   (`sub === null`) on the block's own `content` node carries
   `{ content: extentOf(type) }` into `handleBlockChange`. Deeper changes
   (e.g. inside inline atoms) and attr writes get no hint → all-consumers.
3. **`pendingNarrow` + `resolveNarrowed`** (~L1308, ~L1436): instead of
   resolving inline, content-facet edits with extents are deferred to the end
   of the changed-entry fold, then intersected against BOTH the fresh atom row
   (post-edit — gained/changed coverage) and the stale row (coverage the edit
   destroyed). Deferral is required, not cosmetic: undo/redo commits union the
   undo-repair `slices` writes into the same `changed` map, and entry order is
   not guaranteed — resolving inline could read a pre-churn record index and
   miss the real owner (caught by `u3-semantics`: remote redo resurrecting
   `b2`'s tail would serve stale `'two'` instead of `'two!'`).
4. **Collapse conditions**: `flags.structure` (any slices/gone churn in the
   commit) or `commitRecordsChurn` (claim-preserving record rewrites) fold
   every pending entry back to all-consumers — narrowing never trades
   correctness for precision.

Semantics preserved: siblings whose slices merely translate stay cached;
siblings whose visible ranges actually change still invalidate (fresh+stale
row intersection); read-your-writes (`syncTransaction` runs the same
deferred fold); structural/slice/deletion facets keep conservative fanout.

### Verification

- `npx vitest run src/tests/crdt/runs/u8b-fanout.test.ts` — 3/3, counters above.
- `pnpm test:crdt` — 87 files / 1738 tests green (incl. 150-seed random
  corpus, undo-factory, selective-undo, u3-semantics).
- `pnpm test -- --run` — 103 files / 1749 tests green.
- `pnpm test:dom` — 6 files / 162 tests green.
- `pnpm check` — 0 errors, 0 warnings.

### Expectation for the browser `shared-100k-50blocks` lane

Ordinary keystrokes should drop from ~10.9 ms toward the plain-1k range — the
per-keystroke work is now 1 recompute + ~36-item walk + a 2-root commit, i.e.
the same shape as `separate-50x2k` (which measured ~plain-fixture levels in
the browser). A small residual overhead vs separate backings is expected and
correct (one extra commit root + the row intersect). The format lane will NOT
improve — it takes the empty-`changed` conservative path by design.

### Source identity

- branch `feat/crdt-v14-engine`, dirty worktree (concurrent attribution +
  harness work present, untouched); no commits created by this unit.
- Measurement host: node v24, darwin arm64 (M4 Pro).

## Review fixes (2026-09-22)

Adversarial-review fixes that landed after the unit sections above (U5/U6/U7 reviewed clean; the findings below are from the U1+U2 block-attribution review plus one U7 residual). All pinned by `src/tests/crdt/review/block-attr-defects.test.ts`.

- **H1 — recycled-id stale-record poison.** `b/<id>` records live outside undo scope, so a block whose creation was undone (or which was deleted) leaves its record behind while its id returns to the free pool; a recycled id would inherit the dead block's authorship. Fixed in `stampCreated` (`src/lib/crdt/attribution/block.ts` ~L214–231): on a stale record the creation rewrites `c` to the new creator and clears the dead contributor set — `k/<actor>` set to `false`, never deleted (item-level LWW keeps the clear convergent; `contributorsOfRecord` treats `!== true` as absent).
- **M1 — `setBlock` `''`-content no-op stamp.** A content replace whose text items were empty strings still stamped attribution on the block. Fixed in `setBlock` (`src/lib/crdt/edytor-doc.ts` ~L2313–2331): `''` items are skipped entirely (no atom write) and `stampTouched` runs only when a delete or insert actually executed.
- **M2 — `insertText('')` emitted a real update.** An empty payload still rewrote the covering slice record at a bumped generation — a real ~40–60B update on a semantic no-op. Fixed in `src/lib/crdt/text/model.ts` (~L1254): after resolving the block, `payload === ''` returns early before any write branch.
- **M4 — `jsonEquals` non-plain-leaf false positive.** Two non-plain objects with zero enumerable keys (Date, engine nodes, class instances — reachable via raw/foreign writes) compared equal, so write-suppression could silence a real normalization write. `jsonEquals` (`src/lib/crdt/edytor-doc.ts` ~L497–519) now rejects non-`Object.prototype` prototypes outright; arrays recurse element-wise, plain objects compare key sets + per-key recursion, everything else `===`.
- **`u/` republish guard.** Attach re-published `u/<actorId>` unconditionally, regrowing the profile item + tombstone on every reload. Now rewritten only when the stored profile actually differs (`src/lib/crdt/attribution/attribution.ts` ~L160–167); `c/<clientID>` still publishes fresh per attach.
- **Init dup-id fallback (U7 review residual).** Bulk `insertBlocks` is all-or-nothing — a duplicated spec id refuses the whole batch, which would have left the document permanently empty. `init` now falls back to per-spec insertion on refusal (`src/lib/crdt/edytor-doc.ts` ~L1032–1050), restoring pre-bulk skip semantics; attribution stamps follow the per-spec verdict so a skipped id never earns a record.

## Adversarial-review-2026-09-23 fix round (2026-09-23)

All eight findings of `docs/archive/adversarial-review-2026-09-23.md` resolved; each
was pinned as a failing regression first. Resolution table + follow-on
defects live in that doc's "Resolution" section. Headline mechanics:

- **Document readiness:** `EdytorDocument` gained an `onReady` event fired
  inside `sync()`; `attachSync` counts attached-but-unsynced providers
  (`_pendingSyncs`) so `whenDocumentReady` resolves synchronously. Views
  injected with a pending document (`{document}`, `readonly`, or no `sync`)
  defer seeding via `Edytor.bindReadiness` until the document decides —
  no more sibling views forking local bootstrap content into a pending
  shared doc.
- **Attribution identity:** block records carry an incarnation stamp
  (`i` = creating item id); `ensureRecord` swaps the record node on
  incarnation mismatch (lineage resolved through `item.redone`), insulating
  a recreated block from delayed writes stamped against the old physical
  block. `stampLastChanged` suppression checks tip ownership via
  `node._map` — value-equality is no longer treated as write ownership.
  Concurrent-split contract: contributors describe the logical block's
  edit lineage; tail copies the source set once at split time.
- **Selection restore ownership:** restores are guarded by
  `historySelectionRestoreVersion` + `_docCommitVersion` + `gestureSerial`
  (new — pointer/key/focus evidence). Foreign writes abort via field
  comparison (`ownedState`), not object identity. `focusin`/`focusout`
  marking narrowed: detach-blur (no `relatedTarget`) and programmatic
  focus during a restore window are not user gestures — an undo's own
  render can no longer self-abort its restore (was the DST
  `selection-model-dom-mismatch` regression).
- **Stale-write direction:** `setAtRange`'s async staleness guard now
  compares `isReversed` — an older forward write can't overwrite a newer
  backward range on the same endpoints.
- **Benchmark provenance:** `run.sh` stamps `edytor.src-sha256` with
  `hashTree(src/lib)`; `bench/browser.js` refuses a stale tarball unless
  `--allow-stale`. `hashTree` fixed to `path.relative` (string-slice
  relpaths mangled under `..`-spelled roots). Variant **C** recorded,
  `stale: false` — see `docs/crdt-v14-benchmarks.md` §20.

### Test stabilization (not a product change)

`android-caret-restore.test.tsx` re-stamps `lastDeleteCommandAt` right
before the simulated offset+1 selection echo — the 250 ms production
window could expire under parallel load; the production window itself is
unchanged.

## Generated-DST hardening round (2026-09-23, second pass)

Generated solo + collaboration schedules surfaced three product/oracle
defects and two browser-behavior classes. All resolved; durable pins added.

### Product fix — claim-release seam duplication (seed 10)

A cross-block forward delete left a duplicated boundary char (`!!d` for
expected `!d`). Root cause: the doomed end block's text atoms lived in the
_shared_ backing text (created by an earlier split). The wrapper's
`removeBlock` ran `model.delete` while the doomed atoms were still
claimable — `gatherClaims` skips dead holders (deliberate
"delete-beats-merge" semantics), so the surviving sibling's covering
slice record swallowed the released atoms _in addition to_ the salvaged
tail copy. Fix: `removeBlock` tombstones the block's displayed content
(`deleteParts`) before `model.delete` hides it, so nothing survives to
fall back. Model-level `deleteBlock` keeps its designed release
semantics (asserted by `wu1-ownership` probe 3). Durable pins:
`src/tests/removeBlock-claim-release.test.tsx` (split+prepend+remove
leak, plain remove leak, undo restores atoms+block together),
`src/tests/crdt/doc/model.test.ts`.

### Oracle corrections

- **wordDelete targetRange (seed 2):** word/line `beforeinput` events
  deliver a non-collapsed `targetRange` that the pipeline syncs into the
  model selection _before_ the command runs
  (`isDeleteTargetRangeInput` only collapses `deleteContent*`). The
  recorder now emits resolved model offsets (`startTextIndex`/`endTextIndex`/
  `yStart`/`yEnd`/`collapsed`) and `describeDelete` derives the effective
  selection from the delivered range instead of the stale pre-event
  caret. (`tests/editor-dst/browserState.ts`, `deleteOracle.ts`)
- **History dead-item pops (seed 7):** `UndoManager.popStackItem` can
  consume an item whose effects a remote edit already superseded — the
  source stack shrinks but the opposite doesn't grow. Invariant changed
  from "opposite must grow" to "source shrank OR opposite grew"; an
  ignored command (neither) still fails. Pinned in
  `browser-state-oracle.spec.ts`.

### Browser-owned selection classes (tolerances, not model changes)

- **Sticky outside escape (seed 9, webkit):** shift+click on non-editable
  chrome inside `contenteditable` can resolve the native anchor to a text
  node _outside_ the editor (page `data-testid` content). The model
  correctly keeps its sticky caret. `isStickyOutsideEscape` tolerates a
  fully-outside native range only for pointer actions with an unchanged
  model selection; the solo runner tracks persisted escapes so a
  following `preserve` step doesn't re-flag the same event, and
  `compareEngines` skips the escaped engine. Edge-pixel clicks are also
  clamped inside the element's own rect (zero-width empty texts).
- **Word-jump origin divergence (seed 12, chromium):** the DOM caret
  snapped 9→10 between snapshot and `Alt+Shift+ArrowLeft` keydown;
  `shouldRefreshSelectionBeforeKeyDown` re-synced the model from the
  snapped position, so chromium's `moveByWord` ran from 10 ([0,10])
  while firefox ran from 9 ([0,9]). `moveByWord` is deterministic given
  equal starts — a word-jump divergence can only arise from a native
  caret snap or a native-path fallback, both browser-owned. Cross-engine
  selection identity is skipped for `Alt+Arrow*` moves only; per-engine
  move legality, semantic signature, DOM repair, and history equivalence
  are still asserted.

### Instrumentation cleanup

Removed `__DST_DELETE_DEBUG__` (`edytor.utils.ts`), `__DST_INSERT_DEBUG__`
(`block.svelte.ts`), and per-action facade monkey-patching +
`__DST_MODEL_TRACE__` (`collab-runner.ts`). Retained read-only
diagnostics: DOM state, live `facadeJson`, event traces, remote-apply
counts.

### Validation

- `pnpm test:dst` (`DST_SEEDS=1-20`): **53/53** — solo seeds 1–20 × 40
  steps on chromium+firefox+webkit, collab seeds on 3 peers, all oracle
  specs (incl. new dead-pop and sticky-escape pins).
- Earlier this round: collab seeds 1–20 @3peers + peer-caret seeds
  (2-peer 24/48/50/55, 3-peer 26/28), DOM delete-shape + advanced-delete

oracles, per-peer observation, and claim-preserving replay/minimization.

### Findings pinned for review (not silently specified)

- **UNRESOLVED-1** (`del.range.nested-tail`): a head block whose entire
  text is selected survives intact when the tail is nested inside a
  container (`[alpha@0→beta@2]` keeps `alpha`), but dies when the tail is
  a flat sibling (`[alpha@0→beta@1]` → head gone). Asymmetric head-
  survival across container boundaries. **RESOLVED 2026-09-25** — a real
  defect: the ancestor-rescue branch returned before removing the other
  doomed blocks; see the remediation entry.
- **UNRESOLVED-2** (`sel.seam.nested-subtree`): a caret inside a nested
  block whose whole subtree is remotely deleted lands at root@0, not at
  the surviving sibling seam — dead-endpoint repair appears not to walk
  dead parents. **RESOLVED 2026-09-25** — a real defect: repair now
  climbs to the topmost dead child of the nearest live ancestor and the
  next keystroke lands there; see the remediation entry.
- **`conc.delete-wins-block`**: a concurrent insert into a deleted
  block's text dies with the block (tombstone-wins) — documented as the
  observed policy.
- **Headless honesty limits**: jsdom collapses reversed selections;
  synthetic collapsed deletes are UTF-16-unit (grapheme widening is the
  browser `targetRange` lane); soft/hard-line both resolve to block start
  without layout.
